## Abstract

This note summarizes, in plain language, the current state of the RH-side of the TFPT prime/zeta program, for readers of the 2026 Anthropic result that two thirds of the nontrivial zeta zeros are simple and lie on the critical line. **We state the frame first and without hedging: we have no proof of the Riemann Hypothesis, no partial zero-density result, and we claim no progress toward RH.** What we do have, and what this note documents, is: (1) an exact reduction of a Weil-positivity criterion, through a Galerkin discretization of Suzuki's localized Weil operator, to the positive-definiteness of one small matrix update per rung of a window ladder, split exactly into a 7×7 co-block half and a two-scalar half; (2) a rigorous, interval-arithmetic finite head — positivity certified on all 42 reachable rungs of the deployed ladder; (3) a certified floor for the co-block half over the ideal (not merely computed) source objects on the entire reachable surface; (4) a registered, frozen, falsifiable target inequality for the open half, which passed its first genuine blind holdout; (5) a complete measured map of the certificate languages that *fail* on the open half — twelve-plus dead routes, each with a named mechanism; and (6) — new, and the reason this note exists — an exact algebraic dictionary showing that our registered target is, verbatim, a *positive-index statement in the Anthropic currency*: an effective-rank/rank-trace inequality for a fixed 8×8 core, where integrality at dimension 8 replaces the asymptotic positive-proportion problem. The dictionary is now **machine-verified as an exact identity** (symbolic rational arithmetic, boolean-equal against both frozen surfaces: 39/39 core steps and 67/67 registry-matched rungs), and its first measured returns are in: the *two-moment* (rank-trace) version of the criterion, run through the source-only congruence, fails 0/39 at exactly the seat where the Löwner route died — an honest cross-validation of the Anthropic two-moment no-go inside our geometry — while the *four-moment* version sits near threshold ladder-wide (UB₄ = 0.83–1.60, certifying 7/39 core and 5/27 blind deep steps). Added at the day's close (§6): the deployed finite surface is now **closed end-to-end from cited inputs** — the composed wall census **B ⪰ 0.5523·I (interval-certified over the ideal source objects, 39/39; the exact-rational LDL tier certifies 0.5914 on the computed matrices) ∧ W1 (67/67) ∧ W2 (67/67 surface + 8/8 deep)** holds on all 39 matched surface + 8 deep rungs, from verified zeros (Odlyzko + LMFDB/Platt to 2×10⁷ ordinates, all below the Platt–Trudgian height, consumed through a certified cache battery) plus unconditional envelopes and exact warded composition — together with a measured **transfer law** (ZerosCertified(T) ∧ TailEnvelope(T) ⟹ M_h ≻ 0 for all deployed h ≤ H(T); H(7000) = 254, H(2M) = 1256, H(20M) = 2806; required supply T_req ~ h^2.8, growing faster than the spectral window reach) and a measured map of the all-h architectures, on which exactly one candidate survives, with a numerically specified demand. ## 1. What the object is

### 1.1 The window form

The program's central object is a family of finite symmetric matrices ("window forms") indexed by a window depth *h*: Galerkin discretizations, on a ladder of prime-power-adapted windows, of Suzuki's localized Weil operator. This identification is not an analogy; it is theorem-closed at the measure level in the suite (`v643_w1_theorem.py`): the atom layer of the window form *is* the prime measure of Suzuki's screw function, literally — positions at logarithms of prime powers, weights Λ(n)/√n (`v630_suzuki_contact.py`) — and the archimedean layer matches exactly at every lag (identity verified to ~10⁻⁵² at the kernel level, full form equality at 1.28×10⁻¹⁰). The smallest eigenvalue of the window form at depth *h* is the "wall margin" τ_h; empirically τ_h > 0 on every reachable window, decaying like a measured power law (currently p ≈ −1.93 over 95 rungs).

### 1.2 The extraction chain, and what "cofinal positivity ⇒ RH" means

The continuum extraction is the implication chain

> **cofinal finite positivity ⇒ Weil positivity ⇒ RH,**

which is *theorem-grade modulo named classical citations* in the program's bookkeeping (`PRIME.EXTRACTION.CHAIN.01`; the second arrow is classical — Weil 1952, Bombieri 2000, Suzuki). "Cofinal" is the minimal hypothesis, kernel-checked in Lean 4 (`TfptCarrier/CofinalWeil.lean`, 11 declarations): positivity is needed not uniformly and not at every rung, but on a family cofinal **in the mesh-refinement order** in which the form convergence holds — not merely cofinal in the window or cap parameter, since at a fixed mesh the deployed read is already exactly cap-independent, hence eventually constant, and converges to the Weil value shifted by that mesh's frozen quadrature defect — with the hierarchy *uniform ⊊ pointwise ⊊ cofinal* proved strictly in both directions inside the kernel. With that qualifier, cofinality suffices for exactly one reason: were the limit negative, convergence would make the catch set {j : Q_j < 0} a *tail* of the mesh order, and a cofinal set meets every tail. The honest typing, stated in the source documents and repeated here: this chain is an exact *reformulation* at RH strength (`PRIME.ERRORTERM.SCALE.01`: no unconditional slack anywhere in the reduction). Proving cofinal positivity for all *h* yields the Riemann Hypothesis through this chain — whose kernel-checked premises consume, besides cofinality itself, the per-element form convergence of the deployed ladder to the Weil functional — typed in the Lean documentation as *measured* (rates −1.58…−1.84 per level) when this article was written, and since 2026-08-13 an unconditional theorem with the explicit rate *O(D² log(1/D))* (`v912_form_convergence_theorem.py`, 35/35) — plus density and a continuity leg as classical citations. That continuity leg has been corrected in the same round: the sup-norm *C⁰*-continuity of the Weil functional at fixed support that the chain used to cite is **false** (an explicit even Lipschitz family with ‖e_n‖_∞ → 0 and |A[e_n]| growing linearly), and the true hypothesis is uniform convergence *plus* an equi-Lipschitz (Dini) condition at the origin, supplied by the admissible BV class. Every finite statement below is a finite statement.

**PREDEFINED/noninterference boundary (hardening of audit notes CCCXXXVII Q-3 and CCCLVI).** The original `CofinalHypothesis A` type formalized only the mathematical payload — an `idx`, `StrictMono idx`, and PSD at `A (idx j)` — so a constructor could inspect `A` or its signs before producing `idx`; field order was prose discipline, not an information-flow theorem. The new `TfptCarrier/CofinalPredefinition.lean` makes the strongest honest split: `cofinal_weil_for_fixed_idx` proves the implication for any explicit supplied `idx`; a kernel-checked negative example shows the old payload accepts a sign-mined selector; the exposed selector fails an extensional family-noninterference predicate; and the hardened `cofinal_weil_predefined` API additionally requires a named abstract `Predefined(A, idx)` certificate. This does **not** turn quantifier order into provenance: constantization proves that the historical producer cannot be recovered from the extensional output `idx`. The exact residual external premise is therefore the concrete interpretation/audit of `Predefined` — a source boundary frozen before evaluation and excluding `A` and its sign outputs — unless the construction language or an equivalent effect/provenance system is itself formalized.

### 1.3 The posture

The parent document (`tfpt_prime_front.tex`, ~470 pages) is a *research documentation*, not a results paper: it separates machine-verified, ledger-carried modules from exploratory sandbox probes, and it carries the fence "no claim of progress on the Riemann Hypothesis" as a governing statement (deliberately repeated thirteen times). This note inherits that posture verbatim.

## 2. What is rigorously proven

### 2.1 The finite head: 42 interval-rigorous rungs (`v897_certified_interval_ladder.py`)

The positivity σ_h > 0 of the deployed window ladder is **proven on all 42 reachable rungs h = 142…878**, with the earlier informal evaluation-error model *retired*:

- **15 exact-rational rungs (h ≤ 300):** integer Bareiss/Sylvester certificates on a rational grid, certified floors 4.53×10⁻⁵ … 2.14×10⁻⁴. Every transcendental evaluation (Gauss–Legendre nodes, archimedean kernel, atom positions) is enclosed in rigorous `mpmath.iv` intervals — the node-enclosure lemma runs a definite sign change of P₄₈ on every node interval at radius 10⁻⁹⁰ — and the decision path contains *no working-precision rounding anywhere*.
- **27 validated-precision rungs (h > 300):** interval-shifted Cholesky certificates at 120/200 digits, certified floors 5.16×10⁻⁶ … 8.35×10⁻⁵, honestly labelled "validated-precision" and never upgraded to exact-rational.
- **The negative control fires:** the identical machinery *refuses* the Epstein x²+5y² comb (no Euler product) at pivot index 10 — the certificates are reading the arithmetic, not the assembly.

What this is: a rigorous finite computation. What it is not: any statement about h → ∞. The asymptotic tail is untouched and carried as open contracts (`PRIME.PORT.TAIL.01`, `PRIME.PORT.LEADING.SIGN.01`).

### 2.2 The exact end-form: one matrix, two halves (`v901_tangent_schur_bfloor.py`)

Along the soft (minimal-eigenvalue) direction v_h of the rung-h form — backward spectral data only — the normalized update M_h splits *exactly* in tangent-Schur coordinates. Write

```
M = [ n   b* ]      n = v*Mv (scalar),  b ∈ R^7,  B = 7×7 co-block,
    [ b   B  ]      q = b* B⁻¹ b  (the Schur load).
```

Then, **with B ≻ 0: M ≻ 0 ⟺ n − q > 0.** All identities are warded at machine precision (det M = (n−q)·det B at 2.8×10⁻¹⁴; the equivalence boolean-exact on 39/39 ladder steps), and both scalars are *source-only*: no forward margin, no forward sign enters — the sign of the gap is an output, never an input. This is the end-form: the whole positivity question per transition is (i) the co-block half B ≻ 0 and (ii) the scalar half n > q.

### 2.3 The certified B-half: B ⪰ 0.5523·I over ideal objects (`v905_bfloor_ideal_certificate.py`)

The co-block half is now a **certified surface theorem**. The mechanism is worth stating because it is the round's lesson:

- Every classical certified bound applied to B directly is *dead*: Gershgorin (raw and scaled), Brauer–Cassini, Weyl are negative on all 39 steps, best bound-maximum −88.2 against the true measured floor +0.679 (`v901`, verdict CERTFLOOR-DEAD — registered as an honest negative).
- The missing piece was not a better inequality but the *certificate class*. The chain **B ⪰ ½·P_G + c_dom·I, P_G ⪰ c_G·I ⟹ B ⪰ c_B·I**, where P_G is the source-only Christoffel–Darboux Gram co-block of the wall's own positive chain, is decided inequality-by-inequality by *exact-rational LDL* (Fraction arithmetic on the float64 entries, which are exact rationals — the `v897` certificate class) on all 39/39 reachable steps: c_B min/med 0.5914/5.92, efficiency c_B/λ_min(B) ≥ 0.871. The structural surprise: **P_G is Gershgorin-positive on its own** (min +0.33 on 39/39) — the classical bounds that are dead on B are alive on the positive Gram substructure; the single non-classical link is the dominance decision.
- The interval rollout then lifts the statement from the computed matrices to the **ideal source objects**: the full pipeline (lags → FFT density → Lanczos chain → frame → Schur complements → B) carried as rigorous outward enclosures, *fail-closed* at every step (an interval too wide to decide refuses rather than passes). Six former width-refusals were closed by an Ogita–Rump high-accuracy tier (error-free TwoSum block accumulation, radius improvement ~150–300×). Result: **min c_B = 0.5523 on 39/39 ideal-object steps** (93.4% of the float reference), 0 refused / 0 failed / 0 skipped, and the smooth control world *refused* by the identical exact machine (0/35).

Honest scope, stated everywhere in the module: this is a *surface* statement — certified on the reachable 39-step ladder with a frozen frame convention; h-uniformity beyond h ~ 900 is open, and the n > q half is untouched by it.

### 2.4 Grade of these results

Sections 2.1–2.3 are machine-checked, ledger-carried, and audit-gated (the suite's exit gate requires byte-consistency across papers, ledger, and public mirror). They are finite linear algebra and interval arithmetic — rigorous, reproducible, and *not* number theory beyond the finite prime tables they consume. No RH input is used anywhere (firewalled: no zeros, no prime-table oracles; AST-checked inside the probes).

## 3. The registered target: n − q ≥ ½·μ₁(h) (`v907_halfgap_registered_target.py`)

For the open half, the program maintains one **registered falsification instrument** — explicitly *not evidence*:

> **Target.** n_h − q_h ≥ ½·μ₁(h), where μ₁(h) = 4·sin²(π/(2h+1)) is the deployed Dirichlet/KMS parity normalization.

The registration discipline is the content:

- The constant is **exactly ½, frozen**, under an explicit **no-adjust clause** that is part of the registered object: a later miss may not be repaired by 0.49, by reweighting, or by rung exclusion — a fail is a first-class result.
- **Surface: 67/67 pass**, margins (ŝ − ½) min/med/max +0.0025/+0.5273/+1.6845; tightest rung kz 98/h 997. The second-tightest rung is *shallow* (h = 199): the tightness is arithmetic scatter, not a depth trend.
- **Registry hash** sha256 `ae292e55…` (67 lines, frozen); every future reachable rung is scored verbatim by the frozen pipeline, pass iff ŝ ≥ ½, no refits.
- **First genuine blind holdout: 28/28 pass.** A 10× deeper prime table (byte-exact against the deployed one on the overlap) opened 28 new faithful rungs h = 1219…2854 outside the registry; margins min/med +0.2232/+0.6567 — the holdout minimum sits ~90× *above* the registered surface minimum. No depth erosion. Controls discriminate 3/3 (smooth, Epstein, scramble all sit far below ½).
- The four candidate algebraic origins of the ½ are typed **open**; the registration *demands* a derivation of the constant before any claim upgrade. The inequality is declared in every print as the wall margin *reparametrized* — its value is falsifiability, not novelty.

A pass is survival of a falsification attempt, never evidence. We repeat this because it is the program's own rule.

## 5. The new bridge: the registered target is a positive-index statement

The 2026 Anthropic zeta result ([announcement](https://www.anthropic.com/research/riemann-zeta); [Lean formalization](https://github.com/anthropics/zeta-23-lean)) proves that at least 0.6725 of the nontrivial zeros in every large dyadic window are simple and on the critical line (at least 0.83625 distinct), by (as we read it) moment/inertia control: trace and Frobenius-norm information about a family of operators is converted into a lower bound on the number of eigenvalues of a given sign — a **rank-trace / positive-index argument** — with an information-theoretic ceiling of ≈ 0.68 for what two moments at bandwidth 1 can yield. This section states an exact dictionary from our end-form into that currency and reports the first measured returns of executing it (frozen preregistered probes, round-63 notes CLXIII–CLXVI, all landed 2026-08-11; sandbox grade, no promotion, no claim).

### 5.1 The dictionary

Fix a ladder step and its 8×8 core M = [[n, b*], [b, B]] from §2.2, with B ≻ 0. Let c = B^{−1/2} b, so q = |c|² = b*B⁻¹b, and for a scale u > 0 define the congruence-scaled core

```
A(u) = [ u·n      √u·c* ]        A(u) = D* M' D,   D = diag(√u, I₇),
       [ √u·c     I₇    ]        M' = diag(1, B^{-1/2}) M diag(1, B^{-1/2}).
```

A(u) is congruent to M (for u > 0, B ≻ 0), so it is positive definite exactly when M is. Its two moments are closed forms: tr A = u·n + 7 and ‖A‖²_F = u²n² + 2uq + 7. Then, **exactly**:

> **(tr A)² − 7·‖A‖²_F = u·(14(n − q) − 6u·n²).**

Choose the specific scale **u_h = 7μ₁(h)/(6n²)**. At that scale the right-hand side becomes **14·u_h·(n − q − ½μ₁(h))**, hence:

> **Effective-rank ratio (tr A)²/‖A‖²_F > 7 ⟺ n − q > ½·μ₁(h).**

The left side is verbatim the Anthropic-style statistic. By the rank-trace inequality — for a symmetric matrix with tr A ≥ 0, the number n₊ of positive eigenvalues satisfies n₊ ≥ (tr A)²/‖A‖²_F — a ratio above 7 forces n₊ ≥ 8. And at dimension 8, **integrality finishes**: n₊ = 8, i.e. A(u_h) ≻ 0, i.e. M ≻ 0. (Premises: B ≻ 0 — the certified half, §2.3 — and n > 0, which holds on the measured surface; tr A > 0 is then automatic.)

So the registered half-gap target of §3 **is** a positive-index-8 statement: *the effective rank of one explicitly scaled 8×8 source-only core exceeds 7.* Not analogous — equal, by the identity above, with the frozen constant ½ appearing exactly as the choice of scale u_h.

**Verification status (upgraded from "derived" to machine-verified).** The dictionary is now an exactly verified identity in the frozen probe `anthropic_ranktrace_core_probe.py` (33/33 checks, note CLXIII): symbolic verification in exact rational arithmetic (6/6 rational tuples), materialized on the ladder at max relative deviation 8.6×10⁻¹⁰ with the inertia tie 39/39, the core dictionary **boolean-equal on 39/39 steps** (slack (n−q)/(½μ₁) spanning 2.0×10³ to 7.6×10⁵), and the full-frame reading **boolean-equal on all 67/67 rungs of the registered surface**, matched against the frozen registry sha `ae292e55…`. One important typing from the probe: this route is *state-level*, not transition-level — the wall-blindness that killed the Riccati induction barrier (row 14 of §4) does not touch it, because M ≻ 0 *is* the core wall at the rung, not a proxy for it.

### 5.2 Why the fixed-core version differs favorably

In the asymptotic-population setting, two-moment information yields a *proportion* of eigenvalues of the right sign, and the ceiling (≈ 0.68 at bandwidth 1) is the honest limit of that information. For a **fixed core of dimension 8**, the same two moments plus integrality yield an *all-or-nothing* conclusion: n₊ > 7 has no room to be anything but 8. The population problem is replaced by a threshold problem for one small matrix per rung — which is exactly the shape of statement our certificate machinery (exact-rational LDL, interval enclosures, Ogita–Rump tiers) is built to decide, and has decided on every reachable rung so far (§2, §3).

What this does *not* do: it does not make the target provable. The difficulty is conserved (as it was in every relocation of §4): proving ratio > 7 for all h is proving n − q > ½μ₁ for all h. What it does do is put the open statement in the *same currency* as the Anthropic method, so that their machinery — moment bounds, optimized test windows, inertia counting — applies to our fixed core directly. The Anthropic **no-go is adopted as a route gate** in the program's bookkeeping: their ≈ 0.68 ceiling for two moments at bandwidth 1 is a hard filter — any proposed argument that, unwound, amounts to two-moment/bandwidth-1 information on an asymptotic population is rejected at the gate. The probes below type their own added value against that gate explicitly (exact whitening / matrix-level congruence structure / per-object integrality at fixed dimension 8): the no-go is not refuted, it is *circumvented at the finite per-object level* — and, as the next subsection shows, the gate's prediction is confirmed by measurement inside our own geometry.

### 5.3 First measured returns: two moments fail where predicted; four moments sit near threshold

Both dictionary probes have now run, under the usual frozen-spec discipline with negative controls.

**Two moments through the source-only congruence: 0/39, at the Löwner seat (`anthropic_ranktrace_core_probe.py`, 33/33, note CLXIII).** The exact whitening A(u) of §5.1 consumes B^{−1/2} — target eigendata, so it is an identity control, not a certificate route. The honest source-only version replaces it by the *certified* congruence Â₀ = diag(1, L_G^{−T})ᵀ M diag(1, L_G^{−T}), with L_G the Cholesky factor of the certified minorant D = ½P_G + c_dom·I (§2.3), decisions exact-rational on the float entries. The census: the drive n·t₀ − 7q̂ is positive on 34/39 steps, but the two-moment criterion certifies **0/39** — the spread deficit 7f₀ − t₀² (med 2.72×10⁵) crushes the drive on every step; effective-rank ratios reach 2.0–3.6 instead of > 7. The loss anatomy is the round's finding: **med q̂/q = 91.3 — verbatim the Löwner table** of §4 row 4 (same median, same max seat): the congruence slack of the B ≫ D directions inflates *both* two-moment invariants, the state-level pendant of the Löwner OUTCOME-C in the effective-rank metric. A Woodbury repair hierarchy with the acceptance blockade removed (med 64/64 components accepted) still repairs 0/39 — the obstruction is the spread, not the acceptance. **We read this as an honest and rather interesting cross-validation: the Anthropic two-moment ceiling, stated by them for asymptotic populations at bandwidth 1, reproduces as a broad measured negative for two-moment information inside our fixed-core geometry, and it dies at the same named seat as our own best floor-model route.** Controls are first-class: the source-only congruence *refuses* the smooth world 0/32 and the adversarial cosh world 0/39 (even its 2 genuinely PD cores) — the criterion cannot certify a broken core.

**Four moments: near-threshold ladder-wide (`anthropic_moment_inertia_probe.py`, 29/29, note CLXIV).** The minimal-order census uses power sums m_k = tr(Âᵏ), k ≤ 2r, the sharp Chebyshev–Markov–Stieltjes upper bound UB_r on the spectral mass of (−∞, 0], and integrality: **UB_r < 1 ⇒ n₊ = 8 ⇒ M ≻ 0** (validity battery 2400/2400 on seeded random and arrow matrices; the r = 1 case ties the rank-trace criterion exactly, 0.0). Measured: the whitened identity control certifies at **r = 1 on 39/39** (UB₁ = 0.07–0.84 — the dictionary in moment language). The source-only congruence **never certifies below r = 4** (UB₁ 4.2–5.8, UB₂ 2.1–3.5, UB₃ 1.3–2.3) and at r = 4 certifies **7/39 core steps** — with **UB₄ = 0.83–1.60 ladder-wide**: the four-moment relaxation lands *near the bar 1 everywhere* and crosses it on seven steps. On the blind deep surface the profile is stable out-of-sample: **5/27 at r = 4**, whitened 27/27 at r = 1. An exact Newton–Sturm benchmark (characteristic polynomial via Faddeev–LeVerrier from the eight power sums, square-free + Sturm chain in Fractions) decides 39/39 + 27/27 in agreement with truth — honestly typed as the exact-LDL benchmark class in moment representation, no new certification power, but it shows the *same decision* is a function of eight source-only power sums. Route-gate typing: r = 1 *is* the Anthropic two-moment class (0/39, confirming CLXIII); r ≥ 2 reads 3- and 4-fold lag correlations that pair-correlation statistics do not contain.

**The quantified open demand this leaves** — and the paper's sharpest forward-looking point: a source-only congruence upgrade (weighted rather than halved P_G shares, an a-priori-frozen Woodbury ordering, or a better source-only basis than P_G altogether) that pushes **UB₄ below 1 ladder-wide** would yield a *surface-wide source-only moment certificate of the wall at r = 4* — four moments, one fixed 8×8 core per rung, integrality closing everything below 1. That congruence-upgrade demand remains open as stated; the rounds that followed on the same day took a different route to the finite surface — a verified-zero supply consumed as cited external data — and closed it entirely (§6). Also worth recording from the same round, as a bonus blind result: the core half-gap invariant n − q > ½μ₁ itself held **27/27 out-of-sample** at depth (min slack 1.71×10⁵) — a new blind pass for the invariant of §3, under the same "survival, not evidence" typing.

Grade fence, stated plainly: the CMS census is float-level with conservative refusal guards (declared; the exact object is the Newton–Sturm benchmark), and the r = 4 result is a **measured near-threshold, not a certificate**. Nothing in this subsection moves any marker.

### 5.4 What the 2/3 theorem buys here — and what it does not (`anthropic_twothirds_budget_probe`, 18/18, note CLXV)

We injected the Anthropic budget (off-line share ≤ 0.3275, off-line pairs ≤ 0.16375, mean multiplicity ≤ 1.19581 per large dyadic window; consumed as external-cited input with pedigree reservation, independent verification pending) into the deployed off-line-zero detector and exclusion machinery. What it buys: the conspirator-quadruple count shrinks ×3.053; the **masking-robust detector threshold improves by Δξ = ln(0.5/0.16375) = 1.116, T-independently** — 56.5% of the single-quadruple threshold; the two-quadruple exclusion census falls **×9.3**. These are real, unconditional improvements to our verification instruments. What it does not buy: the budget **saturates 18.4–32.2 decades above the wall demand** (the wall needs A* = 2.1×10⁻⁴…7.7×10⁻³, i.e. 0.011%–0.39% of *one* quadruple), and in the sharpest control of the round, **the zero-off-line-count world moves no deployed floor: τ(A=0) = τ₀ exactly** on all tested rungs. The wall consumes *phases*, not counts — the residual difficulty is fine-phase prime-side cancellation at the port frequencies, exactly as the program's hardness typing (`PRIME.ERRORTERM.SCALE.01`) says. We state this as the honest calibration of the collaboration: their theorem strengthens our falsification instruments materially and moves the positivity question not at all.

### 5.5 A structural note on the window family (`mt_window_family_probe`, 15/15, note CLXVI)

We transplanted the paper's optimized cosine window v*(s) = cos(√2·λ·s), λ = π/(2√2) (only the window shape consumed, support-mapped to our width-D lag slot) into a parallel Galerkin lag assembly, alongside a Fejér anchor; the parallel tent assembly reproduces the deployed chain bit-for-bit on all 10 test frames. Result: **both foreign kernels kill the wall outright on 10/10 rungs** (λ_min: MT −1.95…−3.76, Fejér −0.14…−0.46, against tent +5.6×10⁻⁶…+4.3×10⁻⁴; ŝ flips sign to −492…−7.8×10⁵), and the co-block floor refuses 10/10. The mechanism is named: **Bochner positive-definiteness** — the deployed tent window is PD as a lag interpolation kernel (+5.6×10⁻¹⁰), the MT cosine (−7.1×10⁻²) and Fejér (−2.7×10⁻²) are not. The lesson cuts both ways and seems interesting for both sides: their window is the optimizer of a *different* (pair-correlation) functional and is not admissible as a lag-interpolation window of this architecture — the window optimum is functional-specific, and our tent's Bochner positivity is load-bearing, not incidental. (The x = +1 band of §4 row 3 is also *not* a tent-alias artifact: no cosine window removes it.)

## 6. The day's close: the finite surface is closed, and the remaining quantifier is priced (rounds CLXXXII–CCV, 2026-08-11)

Everything in this section is sandbox grade under the same discipline as §5: frozen preregistered probes in the experiments tree, machine-verified runs with negative controls and disclosed smokes/amendments — no marker moves, no RH claim. It is reported because it changes the shape of the open problem: the deployed finite surface is now *closed from cited inputs*, and what remains is one quantifier with a measured price. *(Update, 2026-08-12: the composed census and the transfer law have since been promoted into the load-bearing suite as `v909_finite_wall_closure` (28/28, self-contained re-verification on a reduced frozen subset with a committed 7000-ordinate verified-zero artifact) and `v910_finite_zero_transfer` (10/10) — suite 901 → 904, full run ALL CHECKS PASSED, AUDIT OK — with the frozen language rules stated there; the account below is the research narrative of how the surface closed.)*

### 6.1 The chain compression: two 8–9-dex composition gaps dissolve into one monotone step

The W1 (co-block-floor) side of the composed certificate had carried two large composition gaps (I4 "concentration" and H2 "diagonal law", 8–9 dex each). Both dissolve in a single exact step — a measure-monotonicity inequality in the dual Christoffel form: if ν₊ ≤ ω pointwise, then G[ν₊]⁻¹ ⪯ G[ω]⁻¹, hence λ_min(G_core) ≥ λ_min(V^{1/2}K^ω V^{1/2}) — with no Gershgorin, no diagonal law, and (after a proved v-monotonicity lemma) no Cauchy–Schwarz anywhere in the chain. The composition cost falls from **+8.26 dex below truth to +0.29 dex** (the remainder being purely the declared L∞-envelope price), and H2 is exposed as +8.25 dex of *propagated* I4 deficit, not a second gap (notes CLXXXII/CLXXXIII). The assembly of the full W1 certificate over the deployed surface is then **lossless** — every supply-closed rung certifies, v-floor substitution cost med −0.05 dex — and the measurement localizes the single remaining gate at one fold seat, j = 16 (CLXXXIV).

### 6.2 The new input class, and the closure

The j = 16 gate fell to a new, explicitly declared input class: **verified zeros as cited external data**. Below γ₁ no zero lives, and every zero up to T₀ = 3×10¹² lies on the critical line unconditionally (Platt–Trudgian 2021) — so the explicit-formula fold reads are summed *exactly* over verified ordinates, and only the tail above the cache height pays the unconditional envelope price. With N_Z = 7000 ordinates the entire W1 surface closes: **39/39** (previously 7/39), composed **67/67** with the cited depth (CLXXXIX). The W2 half then followed in three steps:

1. **Exact Galerkin transport (CXCI).** The full moment→lag transport in double-double arithmetic kills the old float-cancellation blocker: the frozen 10⁻⁸ lag ward passes 66/66 at max relative error **1.23×10⁻¹⁶** (previously 2.37×10⁻²), and the remaining zero-truncation ward is retyped honestly — it demanded the vanishing of an analytic tail, undischargeable by any zero sum below the Platt–Trudgian height, not the convergence of a computation.
2. **The head-size law was an envelope artifact (CXCIII).** The verified-supply construction on the W2 pairing split collapses the fixed-head demand law from **+0.191 to +0.009 dex/log h**; the entire residual hardness is isolated in a *tail-price law* of the finite zero supply (+0.736 dex/log h). At N_Z = 7000 the recomposed certificate closes 16/67 surface rungs; the blocker is purely the tail price, with a measured demand of at most ~10⁷ verified ordinates for the whole surface.
3. **The 20-million-ordinate closure (CXCV).** A certified 2×10⁷-ordinate cache (Odlyzko's 2M table plus LMFDB/Platt tables, all below the Platt–Trudgian height; certification battery: two-sided Rosser corridor on all 2×10⁷, Backlund counts, mpmath overlap, independent ζ-spot checks) pays that price and closes **all 67/67 surface + 8/8 deep W2 rungs** (h = 142…2806), with minimal certifying head fraction med **0.003** (the unconditional-constant route had needed 0.953). The free 2M Odlyzko prefix alone closes 65/67 + 2/8 — the extension was needed exactly for the deep rungs.

**The composed census (the finite-surface main result).** On all **39 matched surface + 8 deep rungs** of the deployed ladder, the *entire wall* is certified from cited inputs:

> **B-half (B ⪰ 0.5523·I, interval-certified over the ideal source objects, 39/39 — the exact-rational LDL tier certifies 0.5914 on the computed matrices, §2.3) ∧ W1 (67/67) ∧ W2 (67/67 surface + 8/8 deep),**

with inputs: verified zeros (certified cache battery), unconditional envelopes (N(T)/Rosser corridor, Büthe), and exact, warded composition identities (CXCV; plus 28/28 W2-only surface rungs outside the W1/B ladder).

**Language discipline (route audit, CXCIX).** The correct description of this result is: *positivity certified on a finite family of Galerkin sections* — with the standing caveat that W2 is certified along the *measured critical direction* (UNIF-PATH: nothing is uniform in h or in direction). The two W2 closure routes (inner pairing-split + verified supply; outer Galerkin section + exact transport) were dependency-audited before any language upgrade, and the permitted sentence is frozen in the audit log: they are *algorithmically independent evaluations of the same localized Weil form from the same window data — a strong mutual crosscheck of the explicit-formula bookkeeping and the tail accounting — but not two independent proofs, not independent inputs, and not a double certification of the same scalar (only the inner route certifies positivity)*. Anything stronger is barred.

### 6.3 The transfer law: what the zero supply buys, measured (CCV)

With the surface closed, the closing probe compresses the closure's economics into one parametrized statement — the **transfer law**:

> **ZerosCertified(T) ∧ TailEnvelope(T) ⟹ M_h ≻ 0 for all deployed h ≤ H(T),**

with measured monotone envelope **H(7000) = 254, H(2M) = 1256, H(20M) = 2806** (full 75-rung ladder) and hypotheses named exactly (certified zero-cache battery; unconditional tail envelope with β ∈ [0,1] unassumed; the exact composition identities; the certified B-floor and the cited W1 depth). The required supply grows fast: **T_req^Wall ~ h^2.8** (log₁₀ T_req = −3.045 + 1.228·ln h, R² 0.88; per-α slope +0.880 dex, R² 0.84), and — the round's deliverable — the **ratio T_req/(π/D) is growing** (+0.897 dex/ln h), not constant: the needed zero reach outruns the spectral window reach ~ h². Per-rung anatomy: the wall curve is W2-priced almost everywhere (W1 binds on only 3/75 rungs). Diagnosis: **W-UNBOUNDED-PER-RUNG / EXTERNAL-BATTERY** — the next h-decade costs ~10¹⁰ ordinates; the finite engine does not scale by buying more zeros. (H(T) says nothing about undeployed h; the extrapolation is a fit, not a theorem.)

### 6.4 The all-h map: three architectures tested, one survivor

1. **Zeroframe unification (CXCVII/CCI).** W2 *is*, exactly, the Schur complement of a 7×7 zero-read frame operator per rung (W2 = m·(1−ρ) with explicit positive factor, 75/75) — but the uniform frame bound is blocked, structurally and by measurement: the limit object does not exist as a matrix (entries diverge +0.641 dex/α), the μ₁ floor is *lattice-carried*, not a continuum feature (BOTTOM-NOT-CONTINUUM), and the positivity seat is *prime-carried* with a measured demand law of **+0.60 dex/α**.
2. **Riccati/half-gap induction (CCIII).** The exact transported increment identity is warded at **1.16×10⁻¹⁵** on the full combined ladder — and the route is transition-locally dead: the transition inequality fails broadly in both B-treatments, the isotropic B-floor *overprices* the demand by **+1.5…+4.7 dex** (r sits in the upper B-spectrum; the directional structure of the inverse is load-bearing), m-step composition is NOT-REACHED for every m ≤ 16, and the key scaling question is answered negatively: increments are **steeper** than levels (+1.07/+1.59 dex/α vs the +0.60 level law) — the all-h carrier is a *level* law, not a transition law.
3. **The survivor: the rung-intrinsic level law.** An h-uniform, *phase-sensitive* prime-read bound at **≤ 21 fixed sub-γ₁ combination frequencies** (|ω_a ± ω_b| ≤ 10.04, all ≥ 4.09 below γ₁ = 14.134725) with **~π/D reach** per window. CCI gives the exact demand (+0.60 dex/α at the floor) and CCV gives the yardstick it must beat: any analytic bound of that reach beats the measured h² zero battery, i.e. beats H(T). *(Superseded in sharpness by the overnight round: §7 locates the level law's h⁻² scale in a phase-sensitive cancellation between two O(1) terms and renames the demand — the frequency localization stands, but "a bound on the prime reads" is no longer the right description of what is missing; see §7.2–7.3.)*

**The quantifier sentence.** What separates the finite result from RH is the depth quantifier — *all computed h* → *all h* — together with, for this closure, the direction quantifier (W2 is certified along the measured critical direction only, §6.2; the pure one-quantifier form holds for the Level-1 cells of §8, where full M ≻ 0 is certified per built cell) — and the depth quantifier does not fall to more zeros (the transfer law shows the supply demand outrunning the window reach ~ h²); it falls only to a uniform mechanism, whose demand is now numerically specified — and, after §7, typed: an identity for the phase of one near-cancellation.

### 6.5 Honesty block

- **No RH claim**, in any direction.
- **Finite deployed surface only, per rung.** The certified statement is positivity on a finite family of Galerkin sections.
- **W2 is certified along the measured critical direction** (UNIF-PATH caveat); the all-h, all-direction Weil-positivity object remains open and RH-hard.
- **The finite closure consumes published zero verifications as citations** (Odlyzko; LMFDB/Platt; Platt–Trudgian 2021), with declared error budgets — a finite verified zero sum can never prove RH; it prices the finite surface.
- The T_req/H(T) laws are **empirical fits**, not theorems; the composed all-h drafts of CCI/CCIII are conjectures with named gaps.

## 7. The overnight contracts round: five architectures executed, and the lock named (rounds CCVII–CCXXV, 2026-08-12)

Everything in this section is sandbox grade under the same discipline as §5–§6: frozen preregistered probes in the experiments tree, machine-verified with negative controls and disclosed smokes/amendments — no marker moves, no RH claim (the wiring theorem of §7.1 item 5 has since been promoted as `v911_wiring_freedom`; the analytic rounds remain sandbox). The program lead's five-contract priority plan against the §6.4 survivor was executed overnight, and the result changes the language once more: the "surviving level law" is no longer the right name for the open object. The round located where its h⁻² scale actually lives — and it is a phase, not a magnitude. A same-day phase round (CCXXIII–CCXXV) then executed the synthesis's own forward question — construct that phase and measure its functionals — and its verdicts are folded into §7.2–7.3 below.

### 7.1 The five-contract census

1. **Riccati/half-gap barrier (CCIII, completing §6.4 item 2): transition-locally dead.** The exact transported increment identity is warded at 1.16×10⁻¹⁵ on the full combined ladder, but the barrier inequality fails **38/67** true transitions (H < 0 on 36); m-step telescoping is NOT-REACHED for every m ≤ 16, additive and multiplicative alike; and the key scaling question is answered negatively — increments are *steeper* than levels (+1.07/+1.59 dex/α vs the +0.60 level law). The structural counterpoint: the deep half-gap invariant holds **27/27** blind with slack 1.5×10³…5.9×10⁴× — the invariant is re-established *rung-intrinsically*, not inherited across transitions. The all-h carrier is a level law, not a transition law.
2. **Exterior-square factorization (CCXI): exists, explains, does not deliver.** The exact source-only representation **K_h − ½μ₁·I = Gram_ρ*(S)** is warded at 6.6×10⁻¹⁵ on 67/67 rungs: all the arithmetic sits in the weights, the reads are prime-, zero- and τ-free. The rank-3 seat form has symbolically exact eigenvalues **{½, −½, −1}** — Lorentz signature (1,2) — which explains both the ½ and why sign conditions on the three seat functionals can never force positivity. The literal seat-wedge completion is **refuted** (nonnegative remainder on 0/67; the manifest square overshoots by up to 2×10⁸); the operator completion K = c_h·P + R *exists* with all three positivity conditions machine-checked 67/67 but delivers the half gap on **0/67** (median deficit 2.81 dex, diverging with depth). The representing measure is **unique up to a null channel** — "choose a positive measure" is not a modelling freedom — and the negative channels exceed the entire margin by median 7.6 dex. New typing, used again below: **Andreief-blindness** — the wedge identity (and its ½) holds in every world including the fired controls, and an identity valid in all worlds cannot explain a world-discriminating constant.
3. **One-bad-mode moment certificate (CCVII): the census's constructive result.** With the certified B-floor, Cauchy interlacing gives λ₂(M_h) ≥ λ₁(B_h) ≥ c_B — M_h has *at most one bad eigenvalue* — and a squared scaled-Chebyshev separator yields the congruence-free certificate **tr p_r(M) < 1 ⟹ M ≻ 0**, consuming only power-sum moments (no λ_min, no B⁻¹, no determinant ratios). Measured: **certifies 68/68** (40 surface + bridge + 27 deep), r* min/med/max = 14/30/119, decisions **integer-exact** at r* ≤ 64 (60/60), the order price follows the law **r* = 0.544·√(λ_max/c_B)**, and — the uniformity question answered positively — **r* is h-flat** (−0.054 ± 0.106 over the full decade including the deep block). Disguise verdict MIXED: the margin does *not* collapse like τ (screens pass), but the n-read sensitivity (med dn*/s = 0.354) is halfgap-grade and the prime oscillation is load-bearing (the window skeleton alone certifies 0/68) — the route is not a τ-relabel, yet it pays exactly the same arithmetic supply bill as the half gap. *Compressed by the phase round (CCXXV):* the Chebyshev separator of orders 14/30/119 is replaced by a proper Zolotarev rational filter with interval-certified one-sided normalization on the whole real axis, and a **single fixed filter with eight conjugate pole pairs (m = 8) certifies all 68 steps** (per-rung optimum m* = 3–5; fixed-filter margins h-flat, slope +0.009 ± 0.037) — because the filter has no polynomial tail, the certificate is exactly a resolvent-trace sum, i.e. **finitely many determinant-phase values** (68×8, stored with per-pole wards at worst 1.0×10⁻¹¹). The honest caveat travels with the compression: the rational filter's n-read sensitivity (dn*/s ≈ 28) is *above* halfgap grade — the fixed pole count bounds the observer complexity, but the route pays the same arithmetic supply bill.
4. **Zero-window bootstrap (CCIX): dead.** The go/no-go family D(H(T))/T measures **0.098** at the first anchor and falls like **T^−0.337**; extrapolated to the Platt–Trudgian height the ratio is ~10⁻⁴; the guarded (multi-violator-honest) frontier is empty everywhere; and the missing First-Violator-Isolation lemma is spec'd exactly — it is itself off-line-strip positivity, RH-hard. This confirms §6.3 from the other side: caches are verification, not a proof route.
5. **Seam wiring closed as a theorem, and gauge-audited (CCXIII + CCXV + CCXXI; seam/KMS physics side, recorded for completeness).** The exact Gröbner analysis determines a **single three-dimensional admissible wiring orbit**: pure-I and pure-J are connected by an integer rule-gauge element, and Z/X wirings are excluded by orientation propagation — an exclusion the mandatory gauge-covariance audit (CCXXI) verifies to be genuinely rule-gauge-invariant (the wiring orientation det u is frame-transport-invariant, exactly). **Pure-I is thus a deployment representative, not a compiler theorem.** The frame decision (CCXV): **θ is conventional** — no compiler demand pins the collar frame (five demand classes, all COMPATIBLE or SILENT on the angle). The audit round then proves, by exact computation, that the strict-collar RP Gram forms transform **covariantly** under the rule gauge — the collar reflections attached to the pure-J and pure-I representatives are exact conjugates under the integer element, and RP(frame, wiring) depends only on their relative angle δ — so the reflection-positivity exclusion of pure-I is a statement at the *fixed* θ_S frame, not a gauge-invariant wiring no-go: strict Hermiticity in the θ_S frame would select pure-J; the same demand in the integer-conjugated frame selects pure-I (the same wiring passes exactly in one frame and fails in the other, with identical certified spectra where it passes). A canonical wiring selection can only come from an additionally derived physical frame. The wiring question dissolves into a **compiler-freedom theorem**: freedom group = the rule-gauge frame orbit, selection quotient = the δ-circle. *(Since promoted as `v911_wiring_freedom`, closing the registered wiring-selector contract; the frozen canonical sentence bars "pure-I is compiler-forced".)*

### 7.2 The lock, characterized twice — and the phase then constructed

The round's deepest result is a double characterization of what actually blocks the open half — measured independently from the read side and from the Gram side, and landing on the same object. The same day's phase round then built that object explicitly and measured its first two functionals (third paragraph below).

**Read side (CCXIX).** The presumed h⁻² decay of the true arithmetic reads is *refuted*: the prime-oscillation read at the critical direction does **not** decay (h^{+0.061}). The h⁻² level scale arises instead from **phase-sensitive cancellation between two O(1) terms** — the smooth window read and the prime-oscillation read extinguish each other down to a factor 10⁻⁹…10⁻⁴ of their common size. The classical smooth direction does not propagate: on the full 95-rung ladder the overlap with the true critical direction collapses to 1.74×10⁻⁷ at blind depth, so the smooth model space does not reproduce the level law. And the certificate census is total: all four non-circular certificate shapes tested (absolute/isotropic channel bound; the sharpest signed two-term with a 7000-zero tail; Kato–Temple even with a measured-λ₂ oracle; the fully non-circular wall form) certify **0/75 while truth passes 75/75**. Provability typing: **OPEN-ARITHMETIC-PHASE-CANCELLATION** — known mean-value/mean-square prime theorems do not deliver the required pointwise cancellation, and separate Cauchy–Schwarz steps destroy exactly the factor h⁻².

**Gram side (CCXVII).** The directional Christoffel energy bound x^T P_h x ≥ ½μ₁ — the object independently named by *four* routes (CCXI, CCIII, CCI, CCVII) as the missing lemma — is **true**, h-uniformly, for every direction, with a one-line proof: N_h is the Gram of a positive measure, so the bound is a *corollary of the wall itself* plus the registered half gap. Precisely for that reason it can never be an input: it is **control-blind** — it holds in all five falsifying control worlds, and a quantity that survives every falsifying world cannot decide a world-discriminating question (the same typing that killed the Andreief identity in CCXI). The four-door payoff census is zero: no tested door moves with it. The lock therefore relocates and is *named*: **c_h = 1 − λ_max(N_h, P_h) ∈ [1.4×10⁻⁸, 1.7×10⁻⁴]** — the generalized spectral radius of the negative-channel Gram against the positive-channel Gram, i.e. the wall's own near-cancellation in new coordinates. The required constants are 3.0×10³…3.5×10⁷ against available 61…1.8×10⁴: median deficit **+2.81 dex, growing +3.35 dex/dex**. One structural gem survives the negative: the P_G chain of §2.3 and the Christoffel energy are the **same object in two coordinate systems** (exact measure link, warded 0.0 — the chain's folded positive measure and the level object's positive channel are two coordinate readings of one measure); but the chain constrains a point-normalized extremal problem while the energy is flat-normalized, and a directional Löwner comparison between their spaces is not even well-posed.

**Phase side (CCXXIII/CCXXIV): the Euler phase is exactly constructed, and its functionals are measured.** The wall read *is* exactly the derivative of a completed unitary Euler phase Θ_X = G_∞·∏_{p≤X} E_p⁻¹·Θ_cut·Θ_pole, built solely from the Gamma ratio, the local Euler ratios, the exact prime-power cutoff correction and the pole factor, and warded on **three levels** (classical unitary blocks external-cited; the deployed coordinates reproduced verbatim; the bare identity exact on 67/67 rungs, |Θ| = 1 to 2.2×10⁻¹⁶) — with the **lag-level ward exactly zero** and the **pole block exactly trivial** (½s(s−1) is real on the critical line, so Θ_pole ≡ 1: an algebraic fact, not an assumption). But the bare identity is a **coordinate change, not progress** — the prediction was registered and is confirmed: exponentiating a signed density survives all five falsifying control worlds at machine precision (10⁻¹⁴–10⁻¹⁵ in every world). The one measured *world-discriminating* structure of the phase architecture is the **Euler grouping** — five axioms G1–G5, all alive at machine level in truth — and specifically **G2, the parameter-free weight law μ·e^{u/2}·k/2 = u**: every control breaks a named, *distinct* axiom set (smooth G2+G3, scramble G1+G2+G3vac+G4, rescale G2+G3, Epstein G2+G3, and cosh *only* G5 — a lag-space block outside the grouping that no other axiom sees). The natural index functional of that phase is then measured out (CCXXIV): the naive Krein/Pick index census returns **WALLPAPER** — the negative index of the deployed phase's Krein kernel is *proportional to resolution* (n₋ = 64/123/241/512 at caps 128/256/512/1024, slope +0.997, no saturation: at deployed resolution the phase is not a generalized Schur function of finite index), the half-gap shift removes **zero** negative directions (Δn₋ = 0 on all declared sub-rungs in all six worlds, refuting the registered step-5 prediction of −1), and the cosh world shares truth's *full* index signature — the index does not see the injection that G5 catches at 3.6×10⁻⁴, so by the pre-frozen kill rule the route is buried. One exact identity survives the burial: **the wall block is the exact diagonal congruence image of the Krein kernel** of the half-gap-shifted phase (W^T diag(K_{Θ*}) W = Γ − ½μ₁·I at 9.4×10⁻¹⁵ on 5/5 declared sub-rungs) — the wall is identified as a Krein object for the first time — but the wall's positivity does not lift into the full Krein section, and index-type functionals of the phase are dead as world-separators.

### 7.3 The synthesis

The round moves no RH marker. What it closes, on the declared surfaces, are four certificate architectures: the local transition inductions (CCIII), the linear positive-measure completions of the exterior-square route (CCXI), the verified-zero bootstrap (CCIX), and the Christoffel energy class (CCXVII — true, but control-blind and therefore forever unusable as an input). The exterior algebra explains the frozen half and the Lorentz signature of the seat form — but not the sign. The remaining scalar is the **contraction defect c_h = 1 − λ_max(N_h, P_h)**, whose size arises from phase-sensitive cancellation between two O(1) reads — archimedean and prime — that extinguish each other down to a factor 10⁻⁹…10⁻⁴ of their common size, while every non-circular certificate form tested certifies 0/75 where truth passes 75/75. The one constructive exception: **ONEBADMODE** delivers a non-circular finite certificate with an empirically *h-flat* order price on all tested truth windows (68/68, integer-exact decisions) and none on any indefinite control world. The remaining analytic object is therefore an **Euler-factorized phase identity, not a magnitude bound** — equivalently, on the Gram side, a lower bound for c_h against a measured deficit of +2.81 dex median, growing. The phase round then sharpened this once more: the Euler phase itself is now **exactly constructed** — and precisely because the construction is exact in every world, it is a coordinate change, and the open object is no longer the phase but **which functional of the phase separates the worlds**. Index-type functionals are measured out (the Krein/Pick census returns WALLPAPER); the first — and so far only — measured world-discriminating candidate is the **Euler grouping, specifically G2's parameter-free weight law μ·e^{u/2}·k/2 = u**, whose five falsifying worlds each break a named, distinct axiom set. In one line: **geometry explains the half; exterior algebra explains the signature; a fixed 8-pole filter bounds the observer complexity; the Euler phase is constructed exactly — open is which functional of it separates the worlds, with the grouping (G2) as the first measured candidate.** This statement supersedes §6.4's "surviving level law" as the program's sharpest description of the open half.

## 8. The σ-chain round: the last cap becomes a derived chain, and the program acquires a three-level map (rounds CCLXV–CCCI, 2026-08-12; clean-map and localization rounds CCCXXIII–CCCXXXI and the adversarial-audit round CCCXLI–CCCLXI, 2026-08-13)

Everything in this section is sandbox grade under the same discipline as §5–§7: frozen preregistered probes in the experiments tree, machine-verified with negative controls and disclosed smokes/amendments — no promotion, no marker moves, no RH claim. The round is the day's second research lane, run in parallel with the promotion of §6–§7's finite results, and it changes the status language once more: the open half now has a **three-level map, measured to its endform** — Level 1 solved on the built surface (unchanged); Level 2 a class-level machine-verifiable proof object with the observer side honestly PARTIAL; Level 3 the quantifier measured to its endform and then hardened — an exact atom identity with an unfinished collapse, the three missing bounds typed (one certified, one proved-conditional, one reduced to a single measured scalar), and a legality frontier whose legal sub-ladder extends to h = 8204 before ending in what was then read as the first measured termination signal. That last reading did not survive the next day's audit: a clean-instrument round (§8.5) exhibited the termination signal — and the entire deep NEGA field behind it — as a float64 instrument artifact of the deployed chain evaluation, and on the clean instrument the construction has no measured end; the quantifier is then localized to a single scalar inequality (§8.6). A dedicated adversarial lane of the same day (§8.8) then withdrew one of the day's own sandbox claims — the θ-mean certificates of the grid-offset family — and closed five further routes as measured negatives, leaving the localization of §8.6 unchanged.

### 8.1 The corrected narrative: two different 0.665s, and the identity that ends the caps

The preceding round had left an attractive artifact: a KS-dual closure under a cap σ ≤ 0.665, numerically matching a measure-side KS invariant. The σ-chain round began by killing its own best number. First, **provenance**: the σ-side constant is max_truth(σ)·(1 + MARGIN_FRAC) = 0.604556·1.10 = 0.665012 — it moves with the probe's own margin convention, so the match with the measure-side 0.665 is a **proven coincidence**, not a mechanism (the two numbers have disjoint provenances). Second, **identity**: σ is exactly the wall's own Schur quotient, **σ = q/n = 1 − s/n** (warded at 3.8×10⁻¹⁵), so *any* cap on σ is not an input but the open half restated — mechanism-importing, in the same sense that killed the routes of §4. The consequence was drawn in full: every cap is replaced by a **derived** chain (§8.2). We record the method explicitly, because it produced everything below: the adversarial lead-auditor loop — audit kills the attractive number, the repair is forced to a stronger object — ran three full turns in this round (the 0.665 provenance audit, the interlacing disclosure of §8.3, and the SOS construction smokes, each disclosed with its repair).

### 8.2 Level 1 — solved: an exact-rational, source-only positivity certificate on every built wall-legal cell

The derived chain, per cell: **P1** n > 0 (exact, min pivot 0.082730 across the ladder); **P2** certified ordered co-block floors (round-62/Jacobi–Sturm provenance — no wall eigendatum enters any class constraint); **P3** the Gauss–Radau moment bound q ≤ RADAU_K(ν; c), K ∈ {4, 5}, with the Golub–Meurant hypothesis *discharged by P2* and the remainder warded per cell and depth; **P4** exact rational arithmetic throughout. Result: **σ ≤ 0.726909 on 151/151 built wall-legal cells** (worst bound at the kz-45 bridge cell, h = 1359, K = 5), hence with P1 and P2: **M ≻ 0** — and the 59-step deep census (h = 1173…6344) carries the same certificate below 1 on **59/59, worst margin 0.2124**. *Conditionality typing (adversarial audit `chain_adversary_probe.py`, 47/47, note CCCXXXIII, 2026-08-13):* the certified statement is **conditional**. The step matrices are τ(r1)-normalized — M̃ = Qᵀ(S(r2)/τ(r1))Q — so each per-cell certificate proves **τ(r1)·S(r2) ≻ 0** (equivalently M̃ ≻ 0), *not* S(r2) ≻ 0 unconditionally: τ(r1) > 0 is an uncertified float64 selection gate of the *previous* rung (it decides which cells get built; it never tests anything — the refusal branch fires on 0/85 built cells). The audit's scale-invariance proof makes the split exact: the certified bound is Fraction-identical under M → sM across four scales (with the certificate transported in closed form), so τ(r1) contributes **zero to the certified number and only the sign**; λ_min(M̃) equals the τ-ratio τ₂/τ₁ to ≤ 6.3×10⁻⁵ on 85/85 rebuilt step cells, and on 83/85 cells the r2 wall positivity is already an admission premise (negA = 0 / deep-ok) with S(r) ⪰ τ(r)·I saturated — there M̃ ≻ 0 is a corollary of the premise. Plainly: the chain **consumes wall positivity through its selection filter rather than proving it**; what the chain certifies about the selected positivity — the O(1)-margin, non-cancellation-currency bound — stands as stated. What makes this Level-1 statement different from every row of §4's resistance map: **no member of the chain lives in the cancellation currency.** The margins are O(1) — not 10⁻⁴-grade near-cancellations — and all relocation screens pass flat (τ-tracking slopes |≤ 0.09|, no c_h relocation seat; the smooth prime-free control leaves legality with σ = 556, the discrimination is intact). Stress and edges: the measured wall-legal σ maximum is **0.709925** (h = 1359, *before* the registration edge — the deep envelope then falls), the registered σ-envelope 0.780917 holds blind on all 111 edge steps, the finite edge family is closed, and the frozen kill line (one wall-legal step with σ ≥ 0.92 kills the architecture) is never approached. Two side routes were typed permanently on the way: the **Pick/one-bad-atom compression is DEAD** — a rational Sturm dual encloses a negative bad-atom extremal with Φ = 2.117 ≥ 1, and the mechanism is a symbolically exact **2ε/y² high-pole blindness lemma** (the Pick data cannot see high poles at the required order); and the **finite-gap reference route is ILLDEFINED** — the wall's spectral gap set is scattered, not banded, so the finite-gap class object does not exist for it (Damanik–Killip–Simon cited as the external reference theory).

### 8.3 Level 2 — the joint class relation, and the optimization becomes a proof object

The class question — does the certificate survive when the cell data is replaced by the *class* of all data sharing the certified floors? — resolves into a sharp positive with one named residual. **Separate envelopes provably leak:** propagating the moment envelopes seat-by-seat admits worlds with sup tr R ≈ 2.26, and the joint relation excludes 11 worlds that the separate envelopes allow. **The joint Radau relation lifts definiteness to class level:** taken as a class constraint, RADAU_K(ν; λ_min(J_B)) ≤ t_R·n decides the definiteness question over the *entire* class — inf λ₁(J) = **+0.0083** (K = 4; +0.00038 at K = 5) against **−574** admitted by the same class *without* the relation: the control fires by six orders of magnitude. (Class-box provenance, typed by the adversarial audit CCCXXXIII: the class box itself is the *measured data hull* log-widened by exactly the probe's own margin convention — box/hull log-width 1.2000 = 1 + 2·0.10 — so the box is data-derived, not independently motivated, and the membership census over the built cells is an identity by construction; this quantifies the earlier not-all-h typing of the class statement.) The K4 class closes at sup 0.9828 (150/151 cells; kz-45 individually certified at K5), and the certified branch-and-bound window on the tight-floor class is **[0.9727, 1.2659]** — with the numeric sup 0.9727 measured as the *structural optimum*: no σ-cap of any threshold can ever go below it. The rigor ladder that produced the window: the **CF-corner lemma** solves the continued-fraction corner refusals (warded 13/13 + 128/128 + 384/384 + point tier 85/85); **seats 2–7 are solved** via the frozen 85-branch catalog of per-member Sturm-certified ordered co-block floors; and the residual blockade is now *named exactly* — the **(λ₁ × good-block) coupling**: the thin truth branch itself carries the maximum good-seat sum, and any independent-seat decomposition has a width-independent ceiling ≈ **1.15** as long as that branch is feasible. The missing currency is a certified (λ₁, good-block) covariance, which no independent-seat decomposition can express. Disclosed with the round, as a first-class finding: the **inherited interlacing upper bracket was unsound** — the bracket λ_{m+1}(J) ≤ λ_m(J_B), consumed by two earlier upper ends, is false (Cauchy gives λ_m(J) ≤ λ_m(J_B); a control counterexample violates the claimed form by 13.43) — the earlier upper ends are retyped as not-certified, the corrected brackets are deployed and seat-warded 200/200, and the old assignment is wired in as a must-fire control.

Then the compression. The per-cell wall pivot q = ∫x⁻¹dμ is dualized by a polynomial majorant p_K(x) ≥ 1/x on the *certified* spectral interval [β, L], the pointwise inequality is certified **exactly** through the interval-SOS identity **x·p_K(x) − 1 = s₀(x) + (x−β)(L−x)·s₁(x)** with rational PSD Gram matrices — in closed form via the Markov–Lukács split, rank-1 Grams, **PSD by structure, zero lift, zero numerical error** — and the class statement becomes a *linear* moment bound Σc_j·ν_j ≤ (1−η)·n. Census: **151/151 at η = 0.273** (largest passing rational η* = 273091/1000000 = 0.273091, saturated exactly at the kz-45 bridge cell with bound 0.726909, digit-identical to §8.2); **1111 exact certificates** (5 global Remez majorants + 1106 per-cell), every one verified by zero-residue polynomial identity plus exact-rational PSD reconstruction; the SOS duals **reproduce the exact Radau K4/K5 values digit-identically or dominate them** (0 exclusions — no sharpness is lost in the compression), while the honest comparison is printed with it: interval-only Chebyshev majorants are strictly weaker (sharper than Radau on 0/158 cells), and the global single-certificate tier is honestly weak (bound < 1 on only 6/158) — the per-cell tier is necessary. The artifact: **`radau_sos_certificate_proof.json` (708 KB, sha 9d7661b4)** — exact-rational p_K coefficients, both rational SOS Gram matrices per certificate, an identity hash per certificate, and the worst-cell exhibit — with the one-line theorem verbatim in the run: *for every measure with moments ν supported in [β, L]: the ν-linear form ≤ (1−η)·n implies σ ≤ 1−η implies M positive definite — each step exact.* The Radau optimization run is thereby a short, auditable algebraic proof object that any CAS can re-verify end-to-end. The compression has since survived an independent adversarial replication (CCCXXXIII): a fresh, AST-firewalled stdlib-only verifier re-proved **all 151 certificates plus the 7 extension cells** — the SOS identity by rational evaluation at 13 distinct points (not the package verifier's coefficient convolution), Gram PSD by all principal minors over the integers, floors/ceilings and M ≻ 0 by exact integer Sylvester minors, q by fresh exact elimination — and the entry data are bit-exact: Fraction(float64) is lossless, all 85 rebuilt ladder cells reproduce the packaged M bit-identically, and the rigorous exact entry-robustness radius runs t_crit = 3.29×10⁻¹⁵…4.12×10⁻⁴ across the census (tightest cell 15 float64 ulp). The Zolotarev observer compression of §7.1, by contrast, stays per-cell: it bounds observer complexity, not the class.

**The endform round then put the named residual to the test (`coupling_currency_probe.py`, 22/22, note CCCI) — and the honest verdict is PARTIAL.** The missing coupling currency *exists*: for every floor branch and every cut, a certified moment/determinant dual (trace pigeonhole, second moment, and the determinant through det M = s·det B — every transformation sympy-exact, segment suprema outward-rounded, no global monotonicity or convexity assumed) couples the class entries to the branch geometry. It kills the level's most attractive artifact: the independent-seat thin point behind the 1.15 plateau ceiling of the paragraph above is **not a class member** (independent coverage 1.000306 against a coupled maximum of 0.984168) — the plateau was a *phantom* of the independent-seat decomposition. And it narrows the certified class window from [0.9727, 1.2659] to **[0.9727, 1.2402]**, for the thin and the full class alike. But it does not close the product class: broad boxes still decouple n and a₁ from the branch geometry, and even the determinant cuts (firing on ~10⁵ boxes) do not carry the worst residual box. What is missing is no longer a (λ₁ × good-block) identity but a certified **non-product relation of the entry moments** — exactly the moment form the deep lane measures — or a sharper joint moment dual. The positivity side (the SOS census above) is untouched by this: it remains independently closed.

### 8.4 Level 3 — the quantifier, measured to its endform: the legality frontier and the deep rate

The deep-membership probe put the limit question to the deep frame directly, and the answer splits. **There is a genuine limit structure:** the moment *shape* collapses — g_k = log₁₀(ν_k/n^{k+2}) is linear in k at **R² 0.99989** (slope +3.102 ± 0.025), i.e. ν_{k+1}/(ν_k·n) is constant and the deep moment sequence is a **one-atom form** — so the class geometry of the deep entry data reduces toward a two-parameter family (n, c, η) instead of an 11-dimensional box; 4 of 15 normalized entry data are h-stationary within the measured 2SE band. **But the magnitudes are not geometric objects:** pushing the exact three-way split S = S_AR + S_SM + S_OSC through the same step frame shows all three carriers growing like h² (slopes +2.05/+2.12/+1.99, R² 0.95–0.96) with the partial sums not even PD — the deep entries are a **residue of three h²-growing carriers**, and what converges is exclusively the scale-invariant form. The two closing probes of the day then measured this level to its endform — and the endform has two faces: an arithmetic razor at the frontier, and an unfinished collapse behind it.

**(a) The legality frontier — the quantifier's newest, and likely final, address (`legality_frontier_probe.py`, 51/51, note CCXCIX).** The frontier discovered at h ≈ 6200 is not a wall but a **field of scattered holes with collapsing margin**: the legality datum τ lives on the 10⁻¹⁰ scale with *oscillating sign* (h 6191: +3.5×10⁻¹⁰ … h 7004: +1.0×10⁻¹⁰ … h 8003: −8.2×10⁻¹¹), and the deepest built cell, h = 8003, is illegal — the third illegal cell and the first legality failure beyond the discovery window. The anatomy is sharp: all three illegal cells, and the tightest *legal* cell alike, sit on **one fixed seat** — the lowest folded core mode, participation 0.92–0.94 — so **the sign is the coin and the seat is fixed**; the thinnest registered ladder rung sits an order of magnitude above the frontier margin (min |τ| = 4.5×10⁻¹¹): the legality margin collapses in the deep frame. A legal sub-ladder exists through every built bin up to h = 7004 (canonical source-only rule MAX-TAU-PER-BIN: 6191 → 6344 → 7004), and the certified chain of Level 1 **closes on it** — 6/6 bounds < 1, worst margin 0.2124, the new deepest legal cell closing comfortably at 0.142 — so the certified chain closes *conditional on legality*, and the cofinality premise stays open at the horizon h = 8003 (frozen verdict FRONTIER-AMBIGUOUS: a single deepest illegal sample decides nothing; a deeper census costs ≥ 355 s per cell from h ≈ 9000). The discrimination is the round's deepest fact: the smooth, prime-free world is illegal at all tested depths with τ ~ −10⁴ against ±10⁻¹⁰ of truth — **at the deep frontier, wall-legality itself is the arithmetic razor, and frontier legality is a prime effect.** Cofinal legality *is* the remaining RH content of this level.

*Hardening update (`legality_horizon_probe.py`, 20/20, note CCCV).* The horizon probe then pushed the frontier, and the answer sharpened in both directions. **h = 8003 is a hole, not a wall:** its bin neighbors 7958 (τ = +5.904×10⁻¹¹) and 8204 (τ = +2.665×10⁻¹⁰) are legal, the legal sub-ladder (same MAX-TAU-PER-BIN rule, verbatim) extends 7004 → 8204, and the deepest legal horizon rises to **h = 8204**. **But beyond 8204 every built cell is negative:** 8677 (−3.053×10⁻¹⁰), 9023 (−1.498×10⁻¹⁰), 9535 (−1.743×10⁻¹⁰) — three cells over two census bins, all |τ| far above the noise floor, all with the same one-bad-mode geometry — and the frozen enum returns, for the first time, **LEGHOR-TERMINATES-MEASURED (last legal h = 8204)** instead of AMBIGUOUS: the first measured termination signal of the built horizon (honest scope: 3 of ~50 census cells in (8300, 11000] are built; a statement about built cells of the frozen bins, not about all h). The seat anatomy also corrected itself: the exact diagonal split A_jj = BASE − COIN shows the archimedean baseline stably *positive* on every cell including all negative ones (+1.6×10⁻³…+3.0×10⁻³), sign(A_jj) matches sign(τ) on only 3/8 cells, and the localized mode splits into diag ≈ +2.5×10⁻³ against off-diagonal ≈ −2.5×10⁻³ with cancellation down to 10⁻¹⁰ — **the sign is not a fixed-seat diagonal read; the coin sits in the seat-to-bulk coupling**, over a stable positive arch baseline. And the seat is not even fixed: it migrates from uf = 2 to uf = 4 on the deepest cell (9535), with doubled effective frequency (0.770 vs ~0.42) and the top atoms flipping from small primes (negative) to large primes (positive). The consequence, stated plainly: **if the termination confirms, the deployed window family cannot host the cofinal ladder**, and the all-h question moves back into the construction choice — a rescaled or extended window family — while Levels 1 and 2 are untouched (every certificate there lives on built legal cells). *(Superseded 2026-08-13, §8.5: the termination did not confirm — it was retracted. The deep NEGA field of this paragraph, the h ≈ 6200 holes of paragraph (a), and the termination signal itself were instrument artifacts of the float64 chain evaluation past its resolution floor; on the clean instrument all nine cells ever read negative or marginal (eight NEGA plus one MARGINAL) are direct-method positive with exact inertia n_neg = 0, and the legal ladder extends through every re-adjudicated cell. The legal sub-ladder statements at h ≤ 8204 stand and are re-confirmed by the clean instrument; the seat-anatomy corrections of this paragraph — arch baseline positive, sign in the coupling — were measurements of the artifact's structure.)*

**(b) The deep rate — the atom identity is exact, the collapse is unfinished (`deep_rate_mechanism_probe.py`, 69/71, the two fails being the one known bridge breach reproduced digit-identically as a gate; note CCXCVII).** The one-atom mechanism is found, and it is an identity, not an analogy: ν_k = Σ_j w_j μ_j^k exactly (max rel 7.5×10⁻¹⁵ over 37 cells) and cos²θ = w_top/ν₀ exactly (1.1×10⁻¹⁶) — "the deep moment sequence becomes one-atom" *is* literally "b becomes an eigenvector of B", and a rate for the angle is a rate for the form. The atom is named twice over: **x\* = λ_max(B)/n**, digit-close to the top folded eigenvalue above the pivot (3.8×10⁻⁴) — an object with frame algebra, not a transcendental scale (the e^{3.1} conjecture is refuted). But the collapse is **measured unfinished**: sin θ *grows* with h (+0.47 ± 0.37), the one-atom purity stands at median 0.870 with 0/37 cells above the declared 0.99 bar, and there is **no crossover h and no measured decay rate**. The angle dynamics are typed as a **cancellation of giants**: the individual carrier components perpendicular to the atom run at 10²–10⁴ while their sum lands at 0.17–0.99 — a median cancellation factor of **1.02×10⁵**; the hope that the h² carriers cancel in the angle and leave a classical remainder is measured the wrong way round — they do cancel, but the result is a difference of giants, not a geometric remainder. Against the definiteness bar 1, both honest readings are printed side by side: the **budget reading does not close** (frontier envelope 0.7876, budget +0.2124, measured scatter 0.4867 > budget), while the **direct reading closes razor-thin** — ρ₅^∞ + E = 0.5029 + 0.4867 = 0.9896 < 1, margin **+0.0104**, with the measured scatter eating 97.9% of the entire distance from the limit level to the bar. The one clean positive of the level: the limit point itself has an **exact-rational positive interior distance** to the definiteness boundary — quantized to a declared rational lattice, Hankel-PD at K = 4 and 5, and 6/6 legal deformations certified interior-positive (margin +0.497 at K = 5): the limit is not a boundary point. **PROVED-RATE is not reached and not claimed**, and the three missing pieces are named — all statements about the window construction: (i) a proved *lower* bound for the relative spectral gap of B (the one h-stationary ingredient); (ii) a proved *upper* bound for the Rayleigh deficit; (iii) a proved Lipschitz constant for ρ_K in form coordinates in place of the measured one. Honest typing throughout: the measured envelopes and fits are conjecture-grade; nothing in this level claims RH progress.

*Hardening update (`threebounds_probe.py`, 74/76 — the two fails being the one known bridge breach reproduced digit-identically as a gate; note CCCIII).* The three named missing bounds were then attacked directly, and two of them turned into proof objects. **B3 is LIP-CERTIFIED** — the first proved Lipschitz object of the Level-3 chain: dyadic interval arithmetic (400 bits, outward-rounded) with forward-mode derivatives over the explicit Chebyshev/Radau function encloses the exact-rational values (relative width 10⁻¹¹⁴/10⁻¹¹⁰), the Euler homogeneity ward is exact at zero width (it proves the gradient computation itself), the mean-value ward holds on 16/16 exact-rational probe pairs with the halved constant violated 16/16 (the ward has teeth), and the declared eigenplane constant **L_plane = L_MASS + 2·L_ATOM = 1.312480** replaces the fitted form sensitivity 1.297491 *on the plane* — same order, conservative — shipped as a 400-bit interval proof object with both limits disclosed (off-plane the linearization is useless, and the certified reach is ~10⁻¹⁵ in g — a tooling problem for affine/Taylor models, not a new idea). **B2 is PROVED-CONDITIONAL** — the deficit growth is self-limiting: the exact identity deficit ≤ sin²θ·(1 − 1/κ(B)) holds with zero violations on 37/37 cells, the cap 1 − 1/κ(B) is **h-stationary** (+0.0018 ± 0.0010), so the measured +1.02 growth cannot continue — it runs into a stationary ceiling; and the growth carrier is **geometric, not prime-side** (it regresses on the archimedean step, −0.94 at R² 0.615, while grid and depth are flat). **B1 stays DERIVED, reduced to one measured scalar:** the relative spectral gap of B reduces to an elementary entry inequality with no eigensolver anywhere in the path, non-vacuous exactly when **t = ν₁/(ν₀·‖B‖_F) > 4/5** (20/37 cells, median 0.8326), with a second, independent Cauchy-interlacing reduction positive on 37/37 — but the hoped-for archimedean seat of t is *measured false* (the exact split through the same step frame is additive, yet the prime part outweighs the archimedean gap 20–30× on every tested cell): the carrier of the missing bound is, once again, the **AR–OSC cancellation** — the same arithmetic coupling, now named in its fourth coordinate system. And the typing of the razor is final: the +0.010420 direct margin depends on no Lipschitz constant at all — it is the exactly measured scatter of ρ₅ about its limit level, an **irreducible measurement** that no certified constant can move (and none pretends to); what the three bounds buy is the typing of the two ends of the chain (left end elementary-provable modulo one measured scalar, right end locally certified), with the single remaining fit rung named (L2, sin θ → form deviation).

### 8.5 The clean-map correction: the deep holes, the termination signal, and the witnesses were instrument artifacts (rounds CCCXXIII + CCCXXIX, 2026-08-13)

The deep map of §8.4(a) did not survive its own audit — and the way it fell is the round's content. A five-stage instrument audit (`chain_audit_probe.py`, 20/20, note CCCXXIII) exhibited the defect exactly, stage by stage: the inputs bind exactly (S1), the witness coefficients convert cleanly (S3) — but the deployed Lanczos-chain columns drift from the exact values of the *same recurrence* by up to 1.7×10⁻⁸, growing monotonically through the chain, and at stage S4 the decisive scalar Q at the chain's own witness reads **−8.75×10⁻¹¹ on the chain columns and +2.22×10⁻¹⁰ on the same polynomial in Chebyshev coefficients** — opposite signs, a 3.5× discrepancy — with all three certified evaluators of the arbiter (direct enclosure, Weil kernel, Toeplitz–Hankel) standing on the polynomial side, and the honestly re-priced enclosure of the chain scalar straddling zero. The mechanism is a **resolution floor of float64 polynomial evaluation**, not a wrong formula: the chain's sign-resolution quotient runs 6.2×10⁻⁶ (h = 878) → 7.0×10⁻³ (3948) → 7.2×10⁻² (5539) → 3.54 (9447) — **sign-reliable to h = 3948 and no longer beyond** — because the deep cells have both a larger evaluation floor and a smaller decisive scalar. The earlier rounds could not see this: their self-consistency reads are built from the same columns (diag(O) deviations ~10⁻¹³ while the true column error is ~7×10⁻⁹ — a self-referential instrument cannot measure its own columns). Re-adjudicated with the Weil-kernel-direct arbiter plus exact-rational Bunch–Kaufman block inertia: **all nine cells ever read negative or marginal — 6197, 6247, 7958, 8003, 8677, 9023, 9447, 9535 (NEGA) plus 8642 (MARGINAL: |τ| below the noise bar, never a sign claim) — are direct-method positive with exact inertia n_neg = 0** (certified positive enclosures, margins 20–573× the half-width), six of them in direct contradiction to the chain protocol, plus both positive controls confirmed.

The protocol round (`verdicta_protocol_probe.py`, 21/21, note CCCXXIX) then sealed the correction on all five contractual points. The **guard is fail-loud**: a reusable chain-sign guard with a typed refusal (`ChainSignRefusal`) refuses any chain sign at resolution quotient > 2.2×10⁻², or deep without a measured floor — exactly the defect class — exact on its ward set (7/7 floor + 4/4 no-floor). The **regression battery is green**: the nine ex-NEGA cells plus the two positive controls as a frozen set, 11/11 with every stored margin reproduced digit-identically (365 s battery wall). The **three decider cells are adjudicated**: 9557, 9585, 10513 all DIRECT-POS with n_neg = 0 — the deep witness at 10513 does not survive the clean instrument, the entangled hole field in (9500, 11000] is **empty**, and the corrected ladder runs 6191 → 6344 → 8204 → 8629 → 9585 → 10513: **h\*_clean = 10513**.
