(One typing note on cofinality: this corrected ladder — like every MAX-τ-PER-BIN sub-ladder — selects its rungs by the measured legality datum τ, a wall output, and therefore can never instantiate the predefined-sequence hypothesis H_cof of the Lean chain, whose index sequence must be chosen independently of any measured signs — and must be cofinal in the mesh-refinement order of §1.2, not merely in the window depth; the compliant construction is the blind MAX-GAP-PER-BIN census below, whose rule is frozen pre-build from census geometry alone.) The **retro-correction ledger** types every affected reading: 5× ARTIFACT-RETRACTED (both deep hole fields, the NEGA band behind the termination signal, the 10513 witness, the 9557 lean), 3× SUPERSEDED, 2× UNAFFECTED — and the untouched partition is load-bearing: every promoted verification module and every O(1)-margin measurement of §8.2–8.3 and §8.4(b) consumed no deep 10⁻¹⁰-scale chain sign and stands as published. Finally, a **blind deep census** (source-only max-gap-per-bin rule, frozen before any build) built 7/7 picks in (8200, 13800]: three DIRECT-POS (8225, 9054, 10025) and four STRADDLE (10863…13026) — the straddles being a new, honest instrument edge *of the arbiter itself* (the Weil-kernel enclosure half-width overtakes the shrinking decisive scalar at ~10.6k), with **no witness anywhere: at all four straddle cells the exact inertia is n_neg = 0 and the E1 enclosure is certified positive, through h = 13026**. The consequence, plainly: the deep legality holes, the termination signal, and the deep witnesses were **all readings of a defective instrument. On the clean instrument the construction has no measured end** — every built cell is positive or no-witness, the strict two-evaluator map reaches h\* = 10513, and the weaker E1-plus-exact-inertia read is positive through every built cell to 13026. *Assembly-precision typing (`arbiter_assembly_precision_probe.py`, 8/8, note CCCXXXV, 2026-08-13):* the deep clean-instrument positives are **measured-positive, not assembly-certified** — an end-to-end 70-digit-input rebuild at h = 9447/10513/11504 (former case-D cell, deep decider, blind-ladder straddle) reproduces every positive sign, but the certified assembled-matrix entry bounds induce ‖ΔΩ‖∞ ≤ 7.5×10⁻¹⁰–1.4×10⁻⁹, i.e. 10⁵–10⁶× larger than the ~10⁻¹⁵ eigenvalue margins, so none of the deep signs carries a Weyl exclusion; the retraction of the NEGA field (the float64 *chain* artifact) stands unchanged. And a replication caveat from the same meta-audit (CCCXXXVI): the "three independent build paths" of the replication apparatus are honestly one fully independent quadrature stack plus one partially independent direct-kernel stack plus one evaluator-only coordinate (two of the paths read the same scalar in two coordinates) — a strong crosscheck, not three independent proofs. (A positive read remains "no witness found", never certified positivity; nothing is claimed beyond built cells; no RH claim.)

### 8.6 The final localization: five names collapse to one scalar inequality, and Selberg is measurably insufficient (rounds CCCXXV + CCCXXXI, 2026-08-13)

The parallel lane took the §7 lock and pulled it into exact normal form (`core_fluctuation_normalform_probe.py`, 43/43, note CCCXXV). (One ladder disambiguation, since the count collides: the "67/67" of this subsection is the CCXI level-rung ladder — 67 *built* rungs, h = 184…1393 — not the 67-rung registered surface of §3/§5 (h = 142…1433, registry sha `ae292e55…`); the equal count is a coincidence, and these probes never consume the registry.) First, **the five names collapse**: the coupling Gram and the coisometry Gram are *entrywise the same matrix* (κ = 1.000000 at 2.2×10⁻¹⁵), so the coupling scalar, the coisometry defect, and the Clark master residual — three of the five names the program had measured for the deep coupling — are **literally the same number** (the remaining two, frame excess and seat outer square, are one-sided and orders too large to carry the sign) — where the adversarial audit (CCCXXXIII) has since typed the identity itself: κ = 1 survives *purely random* densities with no arithmetic content on 3/3 worlds (residual ≤ 10⁻¹⁵), so it is a definitional identity of two code paths, carrying zero information about the arithmetic object — confirming this round's own REFORMULATION-ONLY verdict. Second, **the normal form is exact** (eq. 1 at 9.5×10⁻¹⁵) and its quadratic kernel K is **copositive but not PSD**: identically vanishing diagonal, hence tr K = 0, with inertia (1, 2, 9) certified by exact rational congruence — exactly one positive direction. Third, **the completed square is degenerate by a homogeneity theorem** (ρ_h ≡ 0 identically): the prepared sign case distinction collapses, and no completed-square form carries independent content. Fourth — the localization itself: the prime-free world G₀ carries exactly one negative direction v₋ with need −λ_min(G₀) = 0.94…1.27, and the windowed prime fluctuation X is positive definite and delivers v₋ᵀXv₋ = 0.9457/1.2051/1.2690 against need 0.9429/1.2050/1.2690 — the sign falls in the fourth digit of two O(1) numbers, alignment margin 1.5×10⁻⁵…2.7×10⁻³ on 67/67. **The RH burden on this family is one scalar anti-cancellation inequality, v₋ᵀXv₋ ≥ −λ_min(G₀): a prime-only quadratic form against a geometry-only number** — one *inequality*, with the chain's four remaining legs still named classical citations (§1.2). The decoupled reading λ_min(X) ≥ need fails on 0/67 — by ~5%, so there is no split into a pure prime-side and a pure geometry-side bound; the Mellin positive-energy representation is twice impossible (tr K = 0, and the channel measure has a negative part on every rung); **the comb wins by direction, not by size** — copositivity is the carrier, and the smallest *tested* sign-faithful dimension is 24 (a nine-point geometric sweep, n = 6/12/24/48/…; dimensions 13–23 were never tested; the 12-channel truncation flips the sign on 7/67 rungs). And the ½ of the registered target (§3) acquires **exact window provenance**: the two-arm fold is an exact halving of the Plancherel identity, the half-angle (½)μ₁ = 1 − cos(2π/N) is the second-difference symbol at the fundamental window frequency, and the geometry-only origin is refuted by 3–5 orders — the ½ sits in the window/fold, never in the target. Verdict, by the frozen gate rule: REFORMULATION-ONLY — the sharpest available localization of the burden, and no independent source for the sign.

The closing round then put the strongest classical unconditional source directly on the localized object (`selberg_symmetry_rewrite_probe.py`, 29/29, note CCCXXXI) — and the answer is a theorem-grade insufficiency, not a near-miss. The representation is exact (eq. 4a at 4.6×10⁻¹³): v₋ᵀXv₋ is a geometry-only window functional read against the prime comb, and Selberg's symmetry identity Λ(n)·log n + Σ_{d|n} Λ(d)Λ(n/d) = Σ_{d|n} μ(d)·log²(n/d) is warded **exactly in integer arithmetic** (2999 coefficient tables, 0 deviations). The rewrite is structurally a **perfect fit**: the rewrite-generated double-sum kernel is *measured* (not assumed) to be 100% Selberg-direction — its log(m/n) dependence is absent to 2×10⁻¹⁶. And it is **measurably insufficient**: the unconditional main-term margin is positive on 67/67, but the delivery envelope is coarser than the alignment margin by a factor of 1.6×10⁴ at the friendliest rung and a median of 9.1×10⁵ — "4–5 orders" is the min end; the median miss is ≈6 orders (even the oracle error at exactly known main term is 2×10⁴×). The reason is itself a theorem: **A_main − B_main ≡ B_main identically, hence A_err − B_err = P_err exactly** (warded at 4.5×10⁻¹⁴) — the residual after the Selberg rewrite is *bit-identical* to the residual before it, so **no rewrite-then-estimate route can beat the original**: any absolute estimate of the rewritten pieces bounds the very residual whose size is directly measured (which is why no Vaughan/Hilbert/large-sieve constant is consumed as a number — they all bound this same object). The identity deepens the cancellation rather than extracting it: the same sign must be produced from more and larger terms after the rewrite (median mass factor 1.56). And the seat is measured: the dyadic profile of the running feed at the deepest rung shows 18 of 19 blocks each moving the feed by more than the final margin — **the sign is decided only at resolution n ~ 2¹⁷ = 131072, practically the whole window**; a coarse unconditional estimate cannot localize it. The controls separate the two ingredients cleanly: the arithmetic scramble breaks the identity (it is arithmetic, not analytic), the position scramble keeps the identity and breaks the alignment (the alignment is geometry-conditional), and the smooth world loses the delivery at exactly the alignment term. Verdict, by the frozen gate rule: **SELBERG-INSUFFICIENT** — an independent, unconditional, classical source, structurally a perfect fit, whose delivery is 4–5 orders too coarse for the seat at the min end (median miss ~9×10⁵, ≈6 orders).

### 8.7 The map, in one paragraph — final state

**Level 1 (solved on the built surface, with the CCCXXXIII conditionality typing):** every built wall-legal cell carries an exact-rational, source-only positivity certificate (n > 0 ∧ certified ordered co-block floors ∧ Gauss–Radau/SOS moment bound): σ ≤ 0.727, hence M ≻ 0, on the 151-cell census — per cell a proof of τ(r1)·S(r2) ≻ 0, conditional on the previous rung's uncertified τ > 0 selection gate, not of S(r2) ≻ 0 unconditionally (§8.2; the chain consumes wall positivity through its selection filter; the 151 SOS certificates themselves are independently re-proved) — and the same chain below the definiteness bar 1 on 59/59 deep steps to h = 6344 (worst certified bound 0.7876, margin 0.2124) — the first wall-positivity chain with no member in the cancellation currency. **Level 2 (a proof object, with the observer honestly PARTIAL):** the joint Radau relation lifts definiteness to class level and the SOS dual makes it a machine-verifiable proof object (1111 exact rational certificates, 151/151 at η = 0.273, the corrected-interlacing disclosure on record); the 1.15 plateau is excluded as a phantom, the certified class window narrows to [0.9727, 1.2402], and the coupling currency exists as a certified moment/determinant dual but does not close the compression. **Level 3 (corrected to a clean map, and localized):** the deep frontier narrative of §8.4(a) is retro-corrected — the legality holes, the termination signal, and the deep witnesses were **instrument artifacts** of the float64 chain evaluation past its resolution floor, found, exhibited, guarded, and retro-typed by the program's own audit discipline (§8.5); on the clean instrument every built cell to h = 13026 is positive or no-witness, the corrected ladder runs to h\* = 10513, and **the construction has no measured end** — with the deep positives typed per CCCXXXV as measured-positive at 70-digit input precision, not assembly-certified (the certified assembly error bounds remain 10⁵–10⁶× above the ~10⁻¹⁵ margins). Behind the frontier the endform stands as before, untouched by the correction (its scalars are O(1), not 10⁻¹⁰-grade): the atom identity is exact, B3 **certified**, B2 **proved-conditional**, B1 **reduced to one measured scalar** t carried by the AR–OSC cancellation, the razor margin +0.0104 an irreducible measurement. And the quantifier is localized to its final form (§8.6): **one scalar anti-cancellation inequality, v₋ᵀXv₋ ≥ −λ_min(G₀)** — a prime-only quadratic form against a geometry-only number, alignment-carried with relative margin O(10⁻⁴), the comb winning by direction and not by size — whose sign is decided only at resolution n ~ 2¹⁷, and which no tested unconditional source supplies at that resolution (Selberg, the strongest and a structurally perfect fit, misses by 4–5 orders at the min end — median miss ~9×10⁵, ≈6 orders — with the bit-identity A_err − B_err = P_err closing the entire rewrite-then-estimate class). The closing formulation, in its final honest form: **the finite theory is complete, machine-verified, and self-correcting — the one false deep signal it produced was found, exhibited, guarded, and retro-corrected by its own discipline; the class theorem is a machine-verifiable proof object; and the RH quantifier is now a single named inequality whose sign, at the required resolution, is arithmetic information that no known unconditional source supplies.** One *inequality* counts what the program itself must supply, not the chain's total non-proven content: its four remaining legs — the Suzuki/W1 identification, density plus the equi-Lipschitz/Dini continuity modulus, the faithful cap, and Weil's criterion — stay named classical citations (§1.2). That is the honest end-state of this campaign generation. Every claim above is typed; no RH claim at any level. (One addition on the same day, in §8.8: a further adversarial-audit lane **withdrew** a same-day sandbox mean certificate of its own and closed five more routes. It moves no marker and changes no certified statement of §8.1–§8.7; it changes the inventory of live routes.)

### 8.8 The adversarial-audit round: one same-day retraction, and five routes closed (rounds CCCXLI–CCCLXI, 2026-08-13)

A dedicated adversarial lane of the same day audited the program's own newest positive reading, and it is reported here because its headline is a **withdrawal**, not a finding. Everything in it is sandbox grade under the discipline of §5–§8 — frozen preregistered probes in the experiments tree, machine-verified with negative controls and disclosed smokes/amendments, no promotion, no marker move, no RH claim — and by the program's own frozen gate rule **not one of the five routes below is progress**: each either ends in a measured negative, or restates the open half.

**The retraction (`shift_average_deep_probe.py`, 18/18, note CCCLVIII; `shift_average_hh_audit_probe.py`, 15/15, note CCCLX).** An earlier round of the same day (CCCXLI) had mechanized the grid offset θ as a third wall parameter (cells at (k+θ)D, Ω_θ[m,m′] = (G(m+m′+2θ) + G(|m−m′|))/2, with θ = 0 reproducing the deployed arbiter wall bit-exactly at ≤ 7.1×10⁻¹⁴) and reported the θ-**mean** of the Schur scalar s = n − bᵀB⁻¹b as certified positive on three of five depths, four to seven orders of magnitude above the deployed point margin, via an exactly-piecewise Hermite–Hadamard enclosure. **Those three mean certificates — h = 184 [1.503837×10⁻¹, 1.504222×10⁻¹], h = 388 [9.949740×10⁻², 9.955992×10⁻²], h = 839 [1.522422×10⁻¹, 1.534245×10⁻¹] — are withdrawn.** The premise fails for a named reason: only the prime-comb tent part is affine between its source breakpoints, while the archimedean term carries L(t) = Σ_{k≥0} e^{−(2k+½)t}/(2k+½)² inside A(s,D), so the shifted Hankel entries are *not* affine in θ. On one breakpoint-free dyadic interval around θ = ¼ a 90-digit source rebuild encloses the **atom** second difference around zero (within ±4.00000002094905884×10⁻⁷³ at all five depths) while the **archimedean and full-source** second differences exclude zero strictly at every depth (h 184 [2.28883658345733749×10⁻⁶, 2.28883658345733833×10⁻⁶] through h 2854 [2.28882780584904462×10⁻⁶, 2.28882780584904546×10⁻⁶]) — so the full B(θ) family is provably non-affine, the concavity step Hermite–Hadamard consumes is unavailable, and the honest corrected two-sided enclosures of all three means are **[−∞, +∞]**, typed WITHDRAWN[NO-VALIDATED-FULL-THETA-QUADRATURE-OR-CONCAVITY-THEOREM]. The old float64 grid means (≈ 0.150/0.100/0.152, and ≈ 0.153/0.149 at the two deep cells) survive at most as measurements, never as integral bounds. Two typings travel with the withdrawal, both against the audit's own interest: non-affinity of the matrix path does **not** prove the scalar path non-concave — what is refuted is CCCXLI's affinity *proof*, not an unproved statement about the sign of s″ — and the earlier probe's finite-difference curvature profiles, safety factors and three-point wards are not validated derivative bounds on each interval and cannot repair the theorem.

**What survives rigorously: five point certificates, two of them new and deep.** Re-certified at θ = ½ from the same independent high-precision source, with congruence-preconditioned Arb interval LDL/Schur (the float64 Cholesky supplies only the explicitly invertible, source-blind preconditioner R and carries no sign; the certified λ_min(B) surrogate follows from ρ = ‖C − I‖_∞ with C = RBRᵀ), at all five depths: h = 184 s ∈ [2.55040492106466221×10⁻⁶, 2.55040492106466306×10⁻⁶], λ_min(B) ≥ 1.2022628946795073×10⁻⁷; h = 388 [1.22362650956722969×10⁻⁷, 1.22362650956723022×10⁻⁷], ≥ 5.740612089835872×10⁻⁹; h = 839 [1.05673858430073635×10⁻⁸, 1.05673858430073668×10⁻⁸], ≥ 1.948378488059919×10⁻¹⁰; and the two depths the old instrument could not resolve at all — **h = 1393: s ∈ [2.27451283174326270×10⁻⁹, 2.27451283174326353×10⁻⁹], λ_min(B) ≥ 4.2981529638673363×10⁻¹¹** and **h = 2854: s ∈ [2.12003444567896238×10⁻¹⁰, 2.12003444567896289×10⁻¹⁰], λ_min(B) ≥ 1.4729876002709596×10⁻¹²**. These two are the round's one positive existence result: at each built depth there rigorously exists a positive **nonzero** dyadic offset, so θ = 0 is no longer the only information — obtained with the faithful support cap u ≤ 2α + 2D, where the deployed cap 2α refuses the preconditioner at both depths (a faithfulness correction reproduced from the smoke tier, not a tuning). Controls refuse before any sign is released — scramble and Epstein 4/4 at θ = ½ across both depths — and the source-radius guard refuses 2/2 planted source shifts before matrix consumption. What the point certificates do **not** do is bound any θ-mean: **SHIFTAVG-POSITIVE loses all three of its certified mean legs and retains only finitely many positive point witnesses.** The direct mean route therefore has no certified finite starting cell at all; it needs either a validated full-θ interval integrator with interval LDL/Schur on every box, or a separately proved global concavity theorem. The offset-selection rule is typed with the same severity: "take the first positive certificate at each future h" is certificate-conditioned sign mining *across* depths and is not automatically the predefined cofinal sequence H_cof requires — the same mechanism as the cofinality note of §8.5 and the Lean boundary of §1.2.

**The all-depth version is derived exactly, warded against wrong inequality directions, and priced classically (`shift_average_all_depth_probe.py`, 20/20, note CCCLIX): CLASSICAL-GAP.** The θ-averaged means are sympy-exact (ḡ_r = ½∫_r^{r+2}G, B̄_ij = (ḡ_{i+j} + G_{|i−j|})/2, and the tent/B-spline average ∫₀¹ Σ_k T_D(u−(k+θ)L)T_D(v−(k+θ)L)dθ = L⁻¹(T_D∗T_D)(u−v) exact on seven separations), and three wrong directions are killed with exact counterexamples before any estimate: Jensen/Schur concavity gives ∫s ≤ s(∫Ω), **not** ≥ (exact witness 3/2 < 8/5); the operator-convexity and minimax readings agree; and δ_B has no sign (exactly +1/6 and −3/10). The decisive structural fact repeats §8's recurring motif one level up: the B-spline comb energy E₊ = tr(B̄⁻¹P_cc) is PSD and nonzero (rational ward 5/12) but enters the mean through −E₊ — **the prime energy has the wrong sign and is not progress**. The only valid lower bound needs an independently proved β ≤ inf_θ λ_min(B_θ) with β > 0, giving ∫s ≥ G_geo − A with A = R + β⁻¹((∫‖b₀‖²)^{½} + √(h−1)R)²; priced with Vinogradov–Korobov plus Stieltjes and the exact ‖K_D′‖₁ = 4, ‖K_D‖₁ = 4D/3, and read at the deliberately optimistic, explicitly non-publishable constants C_VK = c_VK = 1, x₀ = 2, the deficit is **A/G = 2.470×10¹¹ at h = 184 and 1.990×10¹⁴ at h = 388** — and the leading exponent stays exp((1−o(1))a) against O(1) target geometry, so no better constant heals a linear exponent gap. Controls discriminate (scramble and Epstein refuse the B-Cholesky at 8/8 offsets), and the global PNT envelope alone is blind to weight-preserving scramble — so the envelope can never be the missing discriminating lemma. Shortest remaining lemma, stated as the probe freezes it: on one source-only, sign-independently pre-fixed (a_j, D_j) sequence, prove β_j > 0 for all θ *and* A(a_j,D_j) < G_geo(a_j,D_j) by a signed multiplicative fluctuation estimate — after which the chain would deliver Weil positivity, hence that signed remainder is RH-hard.

**Both Ξ-side determinant/resolvent routes are vacuous under their own frozen bars (`xi_realrooted_limit_r2_probe.py`, 16/16, note CCCLIII; `xi_resolvent_normalization_probe.py`, 15/15, note CCCLVII).** Round 2 of the wall-avoiding determinant-limit route repaired round 1's diagnosed divergence completely (adaptive Nyquist comb X(t) = (t/2π)^β with smooth C² atom activation, affinely gauged resolvent gate, explicit +1 pole-counting unit against Riemann–von Mangoldt) and left the scramble ratio 1.00 measurably for the first time — SEP_CNT 2.58, SEP_MID 1.68 — but stayed under the frozen decision bar (max ≥ 5): **XILIMIT2-STILL-VACUOUS + ROW-PLATEAU ×3** (slopes −0.018/−0.020/−0.002). A synthesis pass (CCCLVI) then found a HIGH premise bug in both rounds — the convergence was gated with F_window/dim, whose pole residues are −1/dim and which therefore cannot converge to −Ξ′/Ξ with residues −1 — and the correction probe decided it: with the residue ward exact before every read (Res = −1 symbolically and in a rational 70-digit enclosure; F/dim explicitly discarded at all built dimensions 20286…71348) and the affine/Hadamard regularization proved to be residue-free (A_H = B_H = 0 exactly for the mirror-paired genus-1 canonical product, so Az+B can remove divergent affine parts but never repair −1/dim), the correctly normalized *unnormalized* trace has O(1) windowed mass on the frozen samples (final SAFE 1.0975, MID 4.1685) — which refutes XIRES-UNBOUNDED **on these finite samples** and proves no local boundedness on any continuum — while the target residual does **not** converge under the frozen rule (SAFE slope −0.0184, drop ratio 0.8749; MID −0.0366, 0.7392; the combined rule needs a 1.25 drop and slope ≤ −0.05) and the scramble separations are **SEP_SAFE 2.966 / SEP_MID 1.931 against the frozen bar 5**: verdict **XIRES-VACUOUS + SAMPLED-BOUND-PLATEAU**, not a candidate. The honest missing lemma after the correction is sharper than before: source-only convergence on a safe open set **and** uniform local boundedness of the unnormalized, residue-one, canonically regularized family on **every** compact subset of Im z > 0 — finite SAFE/MID plateaus plus an insufficient true-vs-scramble separation deliver neither M1 nor M2, and no "almost proof".

**The self-pairing principle is dead as operationalized (`fold_covariance_probe.py`, 36/36, note CCCLV): PATTERN-ONLY.** A same-day synthesis (CCCLI) had proposed one candidate principle behind the program's accumulated simplicity facts, carried by an exact new symbolic fact: the discriminant form q(a,b,c) = ab − c² on Sym²(ℝ²) has eigenvalues exactly {½, −½, −1} — digit-identical to the symbolically exact seat-form triple of §7.1 item 2 — and the CCCXXXI double-sum kernel is exactly 2q, reading the ½ as a self-pairing factor 1/|G₂| of a two-arm fold. The discriminating probe built the k-arm rotation-fold family K^(k) = (1/k)F_k^H A^(k) F_k with F_k^H F_k = kI exact (ward ≤ 2.2×10⁻¹⁶), reproducing the deployed odd-Toeplitz wall at k = 2 to 7.5×10⁻¹⁷ and the CCCXXV arm identity verbatim to 3.3×10⁻¹⁶, with the symbolic prediction tier (closed forms μ^(3) = 4sin²(π/(2h+2)), μ^(4) = 4sin²(π/(4h))) and the margin band both declared *before* the k ≥ 3 reads. The transport fails, and not by a constant: k = 2 saturates **41/41** in band (trio +1.005/+2.101/+3.328, and the control read *is* the deployed read), while **k = 3 and k = 4 saturate 0/41** with large-negative compressions that **grow with depth** (k = 3 from −7.5×10⁴ at h = 184 to −1.7×10⁸ at h = 997; k = 4 −1.3×10⁵ … −1.6×10⁷) — no constant repairs k ≥ 3, and the two-arm Dirichlet structure is itself load-bearing for wall positivity. Honest census hygiene: k = 4 is removed from the verdict census as **CONTROLS-NON-DISCRIMINATING** (the smooth prime-free control is silent on 2 of 3 rungs there — a first-class finding in its own right), and the doctored-fold non-vacuity ward fires (λ_min shifts 1.8×10⁻³/3.7×10⁻⁵/7.0×10⁻² ≥ 1×10⁻⁶). **The CCCLI self-pairing principle is therefore dead in its k-arm operationalization** — whoever wants to rescue it now carries the burden of naming a different fold that reproduces k = 2 exactly *and* transports. What is untouched: the exact k = 2 provenance (CCCXXV H1/H2) and with it the window provenance of the ½ in §8.6 — the two-arm fold halving of the Plancherel identity — and what the round leaves positively is a **rigidity** statement, not a covariance: the even lag completion admits exactly the inversion as a free symmetry, so the two-arm fold is the only native fold of the self-adjoint window. By the frozen gate rule the run supplies no independent source for sign(Δ_h) in either direction: program-organizer typing, no progress.

**The multiplicative sign source ends in a named local obstruction (`multiplicative_sign_source_probe.py`, 32/32, note CCCLXI): LOCAL-OBSTRUCTION.** The final attack of the round normalizes the residual completely — P_err = Σ_{n=2}^N[Λ(n)log n − Δ(n log n)]G_n = Σ_{p^k≤N} k(log p)²G_{p^k} − P_main exactly, with the discrete Abel form exact including the endpoint, Λ = μ∗log and A = (μ∗log²) = P + (Λ∗Λ) holding in the free log-p module, and A_err − B_err = P_err symbolically exact under the CCCXXXI leading convention — and then closes three doors with exact certificates. **Polarization no-go:** for e_n = Λ(n)log n − Δ(n log n) the form q(a,G) = a·e·G has matrix H_e = [[0, eᵀ/2],[e/2, 0]], of exact rank 2 and inertia (1,1,∗) whenever e ≠ 0, with the n = 2 witness e₂ = log2(log2−2) < 0 rational-interval-certified; Möbius inversion and finite Dirichlet convolution are unitriangular inverse congruences, so Sylvester preserves the negative direction — **no exact multiplicative transform can turn the residual into a PSD energy**, and in any decomposition P_err = Σw_j|F_j|² + R the remainder R must carry at least one negative direction unless a new *global* inequality restricts the domain. **Cross-prime adjudication:** consuming the established Euler-local normal form only as the exact block K_p ≅ diag((p−1)/p, −1), the negative witness v_pᵀKv_p = −1 survives 12 arbitrary symbolic cross-prime entries for p = 2,3,5, with Y_p = v_pv_pᵀ an exact PSD SDP dual certifying ⟨Y_p, K⟩ = −1 — cross-prime bilinear terms repair no Euler-local square while the local restrictions are preserved. **Orientation loss:** eeᵀ is PSD of rank 1 and reproduces P_err(G)² exactly including cross-prime terms, but is invariant under e → −e, so the Rankin–Selberg/autocorrelation route loses precisely the orientation that is needed. Controls fire exactly: truth satisfies Selberg with zero off-prime-power defect, while scramble (Λ-label swap 4↔6), the Epstein world a_Q = r_{x²+5y²}/2 and the exact smooth Abel main term break both multiplicativity gates strictly (P_err = 0 exactly in the smooth world) — multiplicativity **is** world-discriminating, but its positive defect energy is zero on truth and does not orient the nonzero P_err; Abel, congruence inertia and squares are typed COMB-BLIND grammar. Classically, the weighted Vinogradov–Korobov range remains N^{1/2−o(1)} = exp((1−o(1))α) against O(1) wall scale, and RH-strength magnitude removes the N-power but supplies polylogarithmic size **without a sign** (the frozen CCCXXXI finite gaps are cited, not rescanned: best envelope ≥ 1.6×10⁴, median 9.1×10⁵, oracle-exact-main median 2×10⁴). The strongest new theorem is the transform-stable inertia/cross-prime no-go in its explicit scope; the irreducible missing inequality is named exactly — **P_err,h ≤ Q_h − P_main,h − need_h on one predefined cofinal family, from a source theorem that rejects Epstein, scramble and smooth** — and since that plus predefined H_cof, form convergence, density and the (corrected) continuity leg — uniform convergence plus equi-Lipschitz/Dini at the origin, not sup-norm C⁰-continuity — would yield Weil positivity, it is **explicitly RH-hard**. No edge is closed; there is no chain to RH.

**What the round changes.** Nothing in the certified corpus: every promoted module, every exact-rational certificate of §8.2–§8.3 and every O(1)-margin measurement stands untouched, and the withdrawal concerns a sandbox mean claim of the same day that was never promoted and never entered a paper body, a ledger row, or the public mirror — the retraction is internal to the experiments diary, which is where it belongs and how the discipline is supposed to work. What the round changes is the honest inventory of live routes: the θ-mean route keeps only finitely many positive point witnesses (two of them new, deep and rigorous), its all-depth version is priced 11–14 orders short with deliberately optimistic constants, both Ξ-limit/resolvent routes are vacuous under their own frozen decision bars, the self-pairing principle is dead as operationalized, and the multiplicative sign source ends in a local obstruction with the missing inequality named. The open object of §8.6–§8.7 is unchanged; today's round adds one more equivalent naming of the same missing information on the prime side, and one further measured no-go class around it.

**The localization of the residual obstruction (round CCCLXXIII, promoted as the no-go module `v913_signed_alignment_localization.py`, 24/24).** The accumulated no-gos have since been composed into one typed statement, and the exact, cheap, self-contained core of it is now machine-gated: **for the deployed budget shape the missing inequality is not a magnitude statement.** The conversion constant of any hypothesis |ψ(x) − x| ≤ f(x)√x is bounded below by ‖K_D′‖₁ = 4 uniformly in D — an exact kernel identity, so the constant does not get cheap as the mesh refines — while the geometric supply stays O(1) and negative; Littlewood's unconditional Ω-theorem therefore empties the entire magnitude class, the floor 4·log log log N_h exceeding the admissible slack by a factor 214.7 on the module's own conservative slack and 1527.1 on the deployed one. The residual requirement is instead **odd under the comb sign flip and invariant under every congruence of the matrix stage** (the three-term split is an exact identity whose outer terms are even and whose carrying term is odd, with the F₄ kernel itself a congruence invariant), so no reformulation, window choice or preconditioner converts it and no unsigned, flip-invariant hull can bound it below; Selberg's symmetry formula is a formal identity in the free log-p module, hence provably neutral; and the Beurling–Nyman/Baez-Duarte transport target has no N-uniform Gram floor in exact arithmetic (cap 0.420220 against the needed 0.5523), with an explicit family satisfying every hypothesis of the certificate class while staying bounded away from the conclusion forever. Consequently **any future input must be signed and must carry alignment information; neither a magnitude bound nor a world-blind grammatical identity can supply it.** Ten argument classes are proven empty for this budget shape, five are merely unexplored (no emptiness is claimed for statements about ordinate positions, alignment statements, a different budget shape, the four premises of the reverse implication, or new global constraints on the source profile), and five clauses are named open — in particular nothing is said against arguments that legitimately consume zero positions, and the reverse implication (inequality ⇒ RH) rests on four premises that are not established. The statement is **finite-rung** (h = 184/388/839 with the ladder to h = 12632) and makes no all-h claim; it does *not* say that RH is unprovable, that the wall route fails, or that no proof exists. The counter-evidence travels with it unsmoothed: the certified reads are positive at all nine depths with zero straddles, the deepest at 2.79579794131272506×10⁻¹⁵ (h = 12632), and on the n₀-normalized reading the decay *flattens* with depth (−3.211 globally against −2.679 on the deepest step) with one re-derived step outright positive (+0.503), so the drift is not monotone and no re-derivable exponent approaches the frozen collapse bar. The ledger row is typed `no_go_typing` with the open marker: it closes no gate, narrows no gate, moves no marker, and must not be counted as progress — a no-go about the *type* of the missing input is not evidence about its *existence*.

### 8.9 The kill atlas, the complete candidate map, and the Obstruction Completeness Theorem (rounds CCCLXXVI–CCCLXXX, 2026-08-14)

Four consolidation rounds then turned the accumulated campaign into one falsifiable object and one named endpoint. Everything here is sandbox grade — four frozen probes in the experiments tree, no promotion, no marker move, no gate closed or narrowed, no RH claim in any direction; the suite stood at 906 modules at the freeze (949 today, after the v914/v915, v916/v917, v918–v921, v922–v925, v926–v930, v931–v935, v936–v941, v942–v948, v949–v954 and v955/v956 promotions recorded in §8.13–§8.15 and §8.17–§8.25).

**The map is one machine-refutable object (`kill_atlas_dag_probe.py`, 43/43).** The RH implication chain is reconstructed explicitly as an 11-node, 10-edge DAG, acyclic on the conjunctive hypergraph, typed 3 PROVEN / 2 CERTIFIED-FINITE / 4 CITED-CLASSICAL / 1 OPEN — exactly **one open edge, E4**, the predefined mesh-cofinal ladder positivity. The honest non-PROVEN list is E1/E2/E8/E4/E7/E9/E10, so the §1.2/§8.7 qualifier (one inequality plus four named classical citations) is now an executable gate, not prose. The 50-route kill atlas assigns every killed route to exactly one edge (17 on E4, 21 on the finite scalar edge E2, 8 on E10, 2 on E1, 2 off-chain), each verdict token grep-verified inside its own artifact; the τ-screen reads 15 DISGUISE / 1 ADVANCE / 34 not-applicable, and the one ADVANCE does not land on E4. Six coordinate systems — τ_h > 0, n_h − q_h > 0, σ_h < 1, the anti-cancellation scalar of §8.6, the P_err budget of §8.8, and v913's best-conditioned (L) — are pinned as **one and the same edge**: the localization campaign sharpened the edge's type, closed the second open edge (v912), and corrected its statement (mesh order), but did not move it.

**The externally proposed projection route is coordinate system #7 (`sat_projection_alignment_probe.py`, 35/35, SAT-DISGUISE).** The proposal's projection identity is exact — zero residual in exact `Fraction` arithmetic — and empty: the relevant column block is unit-triangular with determinant 1 as exact integers, so P = I and the alignment-looking term ‖(I−P)y‖² ≡ 0 identically; the ellipse inequality is s ≥ 0 verbatim, and the "optimal signed profile" w\* is B-independent — purely archimedean/combinatorial, zero arithmetic content. The θ-average is verbatim v913's best-conditioned (L), the already-priced mean route (HH-CLAIMS-WITHDRAWN; CLASSICAL-GAP, deficit 2.470×10¹¹ / 1.990×10¹⁴); the proposed θ-selection rule ("smallest dyadic offset with a positive certificate") is exactly the sign-mined index whose non-noninterference is kernel-checked in Lean; and the small-effective-rank premise is refuted by measurement — the stable rank of K grows ~h, 109.6 → 1595.2 from h = 184 to 2854, with the top-10 directions carrying ≤ 0.49% of the centered energy. As the atlas's nineteenth candidate sign source it fails the frozen gate: it separates without independently orienting — its readout *is* the wall scalar.

**The candidate map is complete (`untested_sign_sources_probe.py`, 33/33, SIGNSOURCES-ADJUDICATED).** The five classes the atlas still carried as untested all fail with exact killing numbers: the Euler-grouping/G2 weight law separates all four control worlds but its defect energy is zero on truth and its class floor cannot force the deployed inequality (G2-SEPARATES-NOT-ORIENTS); every source-side Krein contractor certificate has margin exactly 1 − ‖C‖² = s/n — the wall sign renamed (KREIN-CERT-IS-WALL); the named unconditional ordinate-position types are empty, with the honest instrument on the full 2×10⁷ certified ordinate cache pricing the required ordinate precision at **~1% of the mean zero spacing** — unconditional Weyl equidistribution carries no rate, rate-carrying density inputs are the emptied E2 class, pair correlation is conditional (U1-NAMED-TYPES-EMPTY); the minimal alignment statement **MIN-U2** is named exactly and is **beyond-classical**: restricted to the deployed windows it already excludes off-line zeros at δ = 0.209–0.364, below the classical zero-free widths ≈ 0.444–0.449 at those heights (U2-BEYOND-CLASSICAL); and every enumerated global source-profile inequality dies on the downward-closure lemma — the zero comb is admissible in every enumerated class, so no upper-bound-type inequality can supply the required lower bound (U5-ENUMERATED-DEAD). The frozen-gate tally is thereby final over the corpus vocabulary: **24 candidate sign sources, 0 PASS, 19 FAIL, no blanket-untested class left.**

**The bird's-eye round (`birdseye_shape_freedom_probe.py`, 35/35, BIRDSEYE-OCT-CONSOLIDATED).** The three degrees of freedom every route had held fixed are adjudicated. *Shape:* the deployed family is RH-saturating with measured margin-decay exponent −3.379; five structurally different admissible alternative families (Fejér, Gauss-tent, Hann, the K⋆K convolution square, a T5.2-style perturbation — all literally in the v912/v848 admissible class; Beurling–Selberg minorants are band-limited, hence INADMISSIBLE-BY-CAP), priced in the same Weil-square currency with an exact explicit-formula ward (zero route == source route at 1.5×10⁻¹¹…2.7×10⁻⁸), decay far shallower (exponents −0.46…−1.09, minimal gap 2.29) — **the maneuver exists but buys nothing**: single-window ladders fail the E7 density leg (54.5% projection residue of an admissible narrow-bump target on the pooled span), any mesh-dense family inherits λ_min(Ω_EF) ≤ s by an exact Schur bound (measured 3.97×10⁻⁷ → 4.19×10⁻¹²), and the localization is **shape-invariant** — square shapes degenerate the alignment (all 15/15 position-blind ordinate worlds stay positive) and put the whole unconditional content into off-line exclusion *below* every classical zero-free width (δ_min = 0.055–0.26 against ≈ 0.42, 5/5 beyond-classical), while the deployed linear coordinate reproduces the ~1% sub-spacing bar (107.1/4.36/0.21 × margin at ε = 0.5/0.1/0.01). The hardness is RH's, not the program's. *Direction:* the existence route is closed permanently at the border-membership step, in exact `Fraction` arithmetic (EXISTENCE-IS-DISGUISE) — some positive extension always exists, so the softness (Krein/compactness) is contentless; the arithmetic completion being positive definite is equivalent to s ≥ 0 both ways (det M = s·det B); the Krein-admissible ellipsoid contains the arithmetic border with margin exactly s; and the Carathéodory–Toeplitz one-step interval is nonempty with the true next autocorrelation admissible at margin equal to the next Schur scalar. The residual gap between "an extension exists" and "the arithmetic one is it" *is* E4. *Bootstrap:* under the round-81 repricing the 2×10⁷-ordinate cache certifies MIN-U2 at 2/5 built cells and the τ coordinate at 0/5; the growth law is T_req ~ h^4.77 on the τ coordinate (h^1.23 on the EF coordinate), the cache's extrapolated τ-coverage ends at h ≈ 36, and precision is not binding (cache ~2.7×10⁻¹⁰ against a needed ~4×10⁻³) — height is (BOOTSTRAP-STILL-DEAD).

**The Obstruction Completeness Theorem** (wall architecture; finite-rung; machine-asserted premises; no RH claim): (i) the implication DAG has exactly one open edge, E4; (ii) the candidate map is complete over the corpus vocabulary — 24 candidate sign sources, 0 PASS, 19 FAIL, no blanket-untested class; (iii) the missing input is exactly characterized — signed, alignment-carrying, unconditional, an ordinate-position statement at ~1% of the mean zero spacing, whose minimal form MIN-U2 is already beyond the classical unconditional toolbox on the deployed windows; (iv) the characterization is shape-independent across the audited admissible families — with the dual/existence direction closed and the finite-input bootstrap dead. The scope travels in the same breath: the theorem is finite-rung (cells built to h = 2854, the certified ladder cited to 12632); atlas completeness is completeness over the routes named in the campaign notes — an editorial claim, typed as such, not a gate; E7–E10 remain classical citations; and the counter-evidence is carried unsmoothed — NO-WITNESS stands (all certified reads positive to h = 12632, deepest 2.79579794131272506×10⁻¹⁵, decay flattening, no collapse bar approached). The theorem turns "why does RH not fall to this program?" into a machine-checked statement with an exact domain of validity; it says nothing about the truth, provability or falsity of RH, and it is not evidence in either direction.

### 8.10 The real-root architecture, the Parity Lemma, and the sign-position predictor (rounds CCCLXXXII–CCCLXXXVII, 2026-08-14)

Six further freeze rounds of the same day then attacked the one direction the OCT leaves untyped — *constructing* the missing object rather than bounding it — and ended with the missing object in its most concrete form so far. Everything here is sandbox grade — ten frozen probes in the experiments tree, no promotion, no marker move, no gate closed or narrowed, no RH claim in any direction; the suite stood at 906 modules at the freeze (949 today, after the v914/v915, v916/v917, v918–v921, v922–v925, v926–v930, v931–v935, v936–v941, v942–v948, v949–v954 and v955/v956 promotions recorded in §8.13–§8.15 and §8.17–§8.25). One convention governs the numbers: an adversarial bughunt round (round 87, below) corrected three MAJOR misstatements in the round-83/84 records; per the standing convention the frozen probes and notes are not retro-edited — the correction is the record — and this section carries only the corrected values.

**The real-root architecture (round 83, three lanes: `semilocal_realroot_limit_probe.py` 16/16, REALROOT-ARCHITECTURE-OPEN; `stc_sol_convergence_probe.py` 4/4, STC-OPEN; `stc_opus_convergence_probe.py` 20/20, STC-DISGUISE + STC-FALSE for the mesh family).** The first route of the campaign to genuinely leave the kill atlas: build finite *self-adjoint* source-only operators — real spectra by construction, no wall positivity, no zero data consumed — and identify the limit with Ξ through the full trace formula on a determining even Paley–Wiener class; compactness, Hurwitz, Hadamard and determination then give RH. Lane one audits the skeleton as executable gates (the determination lemma is sound in two classical steps with *no convergence rate consumed*; two repairable gaps found and repaired) and measures the Connes extremal family: census fully real (41/41 at x = 13), all six trace rows converge, world controls separate at median ~1.3×10⁶ against the pre-frozen bar 5 — at the honest price that the minimizer direction exists only at internal precision ~e^(−4πx) (λ_min = 2.5×10⁻⁵⁴ at x = 13). The corrected zero-tracking read (round 87, F1) is *stronger* than the one first recorded: the exact-mp census puts the first-zero deviation at −8.4×10⁻¹⁶ at x = 8 *and* x = 13 — exactly the cache's own float64 quantization of γ₁ — and the mean displacement over all 15 matched zeros at 0.0 in float64 resolution; the published ε finals move by up to 117% under the exact census (F6), with all six rows still CONVERGES. Lane two builds the Connes–van Suijlekom quotient source-only, hits 14.1347/21.0220/25.0109 at x = 8 with no zero read, and proves the structural point: **R3 alone implies Weil positivity** (h = |ĝ|² on a real spectrum), so the trace convergence carries full RH strength. Lane three proves the **Nyquist density obstruction** for its mesh-CF family — spec H_N has constant density ℓ/2π exactly and mesh-independently while Ξ's zeros have density log(τ/2π)/2π, so R3 and R4 fail for every mesh at fixed window (honest node-vs-zero pairing at x = 55: 18-vs-3 one-sided, 36-vs-6 two-sided; F2); the unweighted trace diverges to x = 144 while the weighted trace converges (honest finals 9.3×10⁻⁴ / 3.3×10⁻⁶ / 2.3×10⁻⁶ / 6.2×10⁻⁴; F4) — but the weights are the Christoffel function of the zero measure, the unknown. The three lanes ended in a recorded conflict, deliberately frozen for an arbiter.

**The arbiter (round 84, `realroot_arbiter_probe.py`, 17/17, independently re-run bit-identical, REALROOT-DEPTH-UNDECIDED).** The conflict resolves *by mechanism*: different operator family — not different sums, not a bug, not primarily depth. All three lanes computed honest unweighted sums against the identically normalized source side. The discriminator is the band census across depth, with a new deep rung x = 21 built at the honest price (K = 80, 128 dps, λ_min = 1.25×10⁻⁹³): the mesh-CF family's (0,30) count grows 7 → 22 from x = 5 to 144, tracking the uniform Nyquist prediction (measured deviations 10.2/10.6/10.9% at x = 13/21/34 against the frozen 30% tolerance; F3), while the extremal/quotient family stays *exactly* on the Ξ census at every measured depth — (0,30) = 3, (0,60) = 13, (0,100) = 29 at x = 21, minimum zero = γ₁ to 10⁻⁴ — with all 50 Nyquist-excess zeros exiled to (100, 163], where fixed test functions are blind. On one unified table (same h, same normalization, same unweighted sum) the mesh family diverges on all four batteries while the extremal family converges on the same four. The verdict is REALROOT-DEPTH-UNDECIDED — not false-for-family, not quotient-escapes (the thinning is *measured*, not proven; Connes §6.6 remains open) — and the new **Z1-transfer identity** names why a proof is the wall itself: the trig-Galerkin matrix *is* the Gram matrix of zero evaluations, M[j,k] = 2Σ_γ B_j(γ)B_k(γ) with λ_min = 2Σ_γ E(γ)² — the minimizer minimizes its own transform *on the true ordinates*: source-only in input, zero-measure in content. Since R3 alone is RH-hard, no finite rung can verify the architecture, only falsify it (the falsification rungs are priced: x = 34 ≈ 1.5 h, x = 55 ≈ 10 h); the surviving lemma carries full RH strength.

**The TPL round (round 85, three independent lanes on the thinning-persistence lemma: `tp_sol_probe.py` 7/7, TPL-OBSTRUCTED; `tp_opus_probe.py` 32/32, TPL-REDUCED + TPL-DICHOTOMY-MEASURED + TPL-OBSTRUCTED; `tp_da_probe.py` 9/9, TPL-CIRCULAR).** Lane Sol proves the off-line-quartet obstruction: off the line the Z1 sum is an indefinite bilinear form (ΔQ = −4δ²E′(t)² at simple real zeros) yet Rouché preserves the band count — **energy sign and zero census are locally independent observables**. Lane Opus supplies the strongest mathematics of the round: the ARCH block *is* the smooth zero measure exactly (1.1×10⁻⁶), the density-level ground state is negative and O(1) on every rung — the entire e^(−cx) positivity is supplied by the prime block alone, so no density-input argument can prove TPL(i); ‖PRIME‖/gap ~ 10^(4.36x), so any prolate comparison must be an exact cancellation, never perturbative; **the Parity Lemma**: TPL(i) *is* — no analysis, a finite combinatorial rewriting — the statement that the ground eigenvector of one explicit matrix carries a prescribed sign pattern, verified exactly on three rungs; and **the counting law**: the Nyquist surplus peaks at D_max = x − 3/8, the tracking band ends exactly at the peak, and the marginal cost per forced skip is a constant factor e^(−11), stable to 11% over 23 decades — the e^(−cx) law of the whole architecture is a counting law. Lane Advocate convicts the route's measured support as circular: the recorded band counts *are* the smooth Riemann–von Mangoldt values to < 1 (29.0023 against 29); the instrument transcribes whatever measure it is fed (the prime-free smooth world gets its own clean census); outrunning classical zero verification would need dps ~ 2.1×10¹²; and the only off-line-sensitive channel is the wall scalar itself (measured slope 2.03, independently confirming Sol's law). One-sentence status: the *number* of ground-eigenvector sign changes is forced by counting — the arithmetic substance sits exclusively in the *positions* of the sign changes, which the prime block alone must supply.

**The simplicity round (round 86, `simple_principle_probe.py`, 22/22, SIMPLE-PRINCIPLE-IS-TRANSCRIPTION).** Four readings of "the simple principle must thread through everything," each with pre-registered consequences and frozen bars. The one-axiom reading is refuted in its strong form (five natural normalizations, zero hits on the declared constant log π, gaps 0.226–1.341; the difference-level dictionary is exact, so the equality is exact as a gauge orbit and empty as a source). The transcription reading is *confirmed* 3/3 on the sharpest claimed arithmetic outputs — the register's Mertens head is exactly ∏_{p|210}(1 − 1/p) = 48/210, i.e. 2.1293 bit = log₂(35/8): the "arithmetic output" is the compiler pair itself; the E″(0) pin is the Hadamard identity to 2.4×10⁻⁸. The fixed-point reading is refuted (the compression transcribes every fed measure). The Gram-seat square law δ\* = √(margin/curvature) is measured numerically exact on the Gram channel (ratio 0.9997, slope 2.0000) with the order split (position first-order, reality second-order) — and by the frozen FOLDCOV rule typed PATTERN-ONLY-EVEN-IF-CONFIRMED. What survives is one principle: **TFPT's positivity is always Gram positivity of node evaluations — a theorem exactly where the nodes are compiler data — and the arithmetic seam is the single place whose nodes are not.** Two measured corollaries sharpen the OCT without moving it: the order split mechanically explains why all 24 candidate sign sources died (they sought first-order information in a channel that carries reality only at second order), and the ~1% sub-spacing price is instrument-dependent — √(margin/curvature), scaling like h^(−1.69) along the deployed ladder. Tested simplicity adds nothing beyond the OCT; it explains why the OCT looks the way it does.

**The bughunt round (round 87, `bughunt_r79_r85_probe.py`, 24/24, BUGHUNT-FINDINGS(9, max MAJOR)).** An adversarial audit of everything produced in rounds 79–85: nine findings, 3 MAJOR / 6 MINOR / 0 FATAL, *no round verdict flips*, every finding pinned by an executable gate. The three majors are the corrections carried above (the first-zero/mean-displacement conflation, whose honest resolution is stronger than the published claim; the 36-vs-3 pairing, honestly 18-vs-3; the float64-refinement artifact in the published ε finals, with gated survival of every verdict). One systemic minor: nine gates across the corpus are hardcoded `check(..., True)` and can never fail while counted in the N/N tallies — including the very line that records the round-80 "candidate #19 gate FAIL" verdict (the verdict is right; the gate tests nothing). Checked clean: every round-79–82 headline number reproduces on fresh re-runs, all ten probes re-ran green, no firewall violations.

**The sign-position round (round 88, `signpos_probe.py`, 19/19, independently re-run bit-identical, SIGNPOS-PREDICTOR-EXACT + SIGNPOS-TP-DEAD + SIGNPOS-RIGID + SIGNPOS-K-STABLE).** The campaign's most concrete endpoint: the positions of the ground-eigenvector sign changes — the single open object left by the Parity Lemma — attacked with the oscillation/total-positivity/node-duality toolbox. A **source-only predictor of the sign-change positions exists and is exact**: the parity of round(θ(ω_k)/π + 1 + S_x(ω_k)) at the Nyquist lattice, where S_x is the band-limited phase built from the prime-node comb, is exact at *all 79 lattice points* across x = 3/5/8/13, with measured first-order arithmetic content: the density alone fails at exactly four lattice points and the prime-node comb repairs precisely those four (arithmetic load 4) — the first arithmetically computable first-order-channel object of the campaign; a scramble control fails 6/11 with λ_min = −1.11, so the exactness is explicit-formula-specific, not a pipeline transcription. The catch is named precisely: proving the predictor's exactness for *all* x is half-integer-margin control of the band-limited S at the lattice points (measured minimum margin shrinking to 0.0317), which is verbatim TPL(i) itself — the RH hardness relocates for the *third* time to the same seat, now as a rounding-margin statement about one explicit band-limited function of the primes. Gantmacher–Krein is structurally dead (the density blocks are indefinite; M is neither totally nonnegative nor a Green's/one-pair matrix in any tested ordering; the minor census localizes nothing); the map {ordinate positions} → {sign pattern} is *rigid* with modulus equal to the ordinate-to-lattice distance — TPL(i) is exactly the statement that the prime block never carries a node across the lattice; and the positions are K-stable inside the deployed band.

**Correction of record (round 97, 2026-08-15, `sp_expsum_pricing_probe.py`, 13/13, SP-OPERATIVE-FALSIFIED — carried per the round-87 convention: the frozen probes and notes are not retro-edited, the correction is the record).** The predictor's exactness holds at x ≤ 13 only. Round 97 (§8.13) extended the census from the 79 lattice points (x ≤ 13) to 18798 points (x ≤ 890, the cache edge γ₇₀₀₀ = 7264.75) and measured the *operative* form of this predictor — round(N_src(ω_k)) = N_true(ω_k), TPL(i)'s finite form — **false from x = 21 onward**, with 567 τ-screened counterexamples: the round-88 exactness was a small-x accident of the margin budget. The mechanism is arithmetic-free — the Fejér taper's irreducible tracking-variance floor (rms → 1/(2π) = 0.159, measured 0.26) against the 0.5 budget, so no taper in this family survives — and the same round priced the budget as classically out of reach (best unconditional miss ×8.2; even the RH-conditional bound reads 1.015 at the deepest rung) with the prefix/exceptional-set salvage measured dead in-window. The fence travels with the correction: what died is the deployed *source-side approximant*, not TPL(i) itself — the eigenvector truth side is unmeasured beyond x = 13 — and the round-88 claim as published (exact at all 79 points, x ≤ 13) remains factually true; its operative extension is now measured dead.

**Where the arc leaves the campaign** (sandbox; no RH claim): the wall route is closed as a theorem (the OCT of §8.9); the only architecture that genuinely escapes the kill atlas is adjudicated REALROOT-DEPTH-UNDECIDED with its measured support convicted circular; and the missing object now has its most concrete form ever — the eigenvector sign-change positions, equivalently the rounding margins of one explicit band-limited prime function — and carries full RH strength in every coordinate system in which it has been written. RH is neither proven nor disproven; what is documented is the complete map of why, now including the object-construction direction.

### 8.11 The Stieltjes–Vitali pin route and the Krein screw-function carrier (rounds CCCLXXXIX–CCCXC, 2026-08-14)

Two closing freeze rounds of the same day then took the *resolvent* direction to the same endpoint. Everything here is sandbox grade — two frozen probes in the experiments tree, no promotion, no marker move, no gate closed or narrowed, no RH claim in any direction; the suite stood at 906 modules at the freeze (949 today, after the v914/v915, v916/v917, v918–v921, v922–v925, v926–v930, v931–v935, v936–v941, v942–v948, v949–v954 and v955/v956 promotions recorded in §8.13–§8.15 and §8.17–§8.25).

**The Stieltjes–Vitali pin route (round 89, externally proposed, contract PRIME.STIELTJES.VITALI.PIN.01; `stieltjes_vitali_pin_probe.py`, 17/17 gates, 249 s, independently re-run bit-identical, verdict SVPIN-ROUTE-OPEN).** The proposal replaces the full semilocal trace convergence by countably many positive resolvent scalars in the absolutely convergent Euler half-plane: the pins P_x(σ) = Σ_k 2σ/(λ_{x,k}² + σ²) must converge to ξ′/ξ(1/2 + σ) at the countable family σ_r = 1 + 1/r, where the right side is source-only (Γ, π, Λ(n), n^(−s) — absolutely convergent for s > 1); Herglotz normal-family compactness (*one* pin suffices), Vitali on the pins accumulating at the interior point i, the identity theorem on Im z > 1/2 (where Ξ is zero-free), and the pole contradiction then give (SV) ⟹ RH, with the functional equation closing β < 1/2. **The skeleton is sound, gate by gate**: the weight-comparability constant 65.01 is executed per rung (normal-family ratio 0.033); the sign-convention ambiguity of earlier rounds is *harmless* for the pins (both conventions give the identical target via ξ′/ξ(1−s) = −ξ′/ξ(s), numeric lock dev 2.3×10⁻⁸); the spectrum honesty is measured, not assumed (census 4/10/20/41 all real, min spacing ≥ 1.3); the degenerate Herglotz limit is excluded by the pin bound. The *one* named condition: the all-x realness of the spectra is the CF simplicity-plus-evenness hypothesis. The named non-gap, stated as bluntly as the probe states it: **the chain moves the entire burden into (SV) itself** — no skeleton step is the wall; the wall is the premise. The finite pre-check **converges on all 16 frozen σ values** (x = 3/5/8/13; slopes −0.62…−0.65, measured drop 2.6 against the a-priori Riemann–von Mangoldt model 2.4, bar 1.6; no σ ↓ 1/2 rate degradation on this ladder, the expected boundary effect lying beyond reachable depth, stated honestly; the full divisor row converges *faster* — the lattice comb compensates ~2/3 of the missing tail); all four world controls separate (the mesh-CF family is **mesh-poisoned through the same pins**, median overshoot growing 0.94 → 6.32 like log x — the wrong limit, measured; SCRPOS/EPSTEIN/SMOOTH/SCRARITH separate at medians 2.7×10⁵–8.8×10⁵ band-metric and 12.4–45.9 target-metric on 16/16 rows); and the τ-screen shows 0/16 relocations at max slope 0.009 — **no disguise**. The honest weight, Z1-typed as the actual adjudication: on the extremal family the measured pin convergence *is* the transcription of cache partial sums (rel 7.7×10⁻¹²/9.4×10⁻¹²/2.1×10⁻⁵) — instrument consistency, never source-side content — and the naive Suzuki spectral readout is *dead* (SUZPIN-FORM-SPECTRUM: the hat-compression eigenvalues are a form-spectrum ladder √μ = 1.02/2.12/3.17…, not the ordinates; P_suz grows 1.85 → 2.47 against the target 0.046). The route is the *second* architecture of the campaign to survive every gate, and its reduction — countably many safe scalars in place of a test-class quantification — is the corpus's logically sharpest open target; the priced remaining task is exactly one: the Krein inverse-spectral realization of Suzuki's screw function, which the corpus did not have.

**The Krein screw-function carrier (round 90, contract PRIME.SUZUKI.KREIN.REALIZATION.01; `krein_screw_realization_probe.py`, 22/22 gates, 18 s, independently re-run bit-identical, verdict SUZKREIN-CARRIER-OPEN(lemma: uniform Weyl-disk contraction)).** The priced missing ingredient, built and adjudicated. The screw function g is extracted corpus-exactly (the Lerch coefficient +1/4 *gated* against the origin expansion with A = ½(log 2π − ψ(2)) = 0.707546 — a −1 misreading would fail by O(1)); the screw property is verified (the kernel PSD on all four windows, λ_min/λ_max ≥ −8.1×10⁻¹⁹); the invoked theorem is named with its hypothesis *certified*: Krein's accelerant/continual-Schur correspondence, whose hypothesis — strict positivity of every Toeplitz section of the accelerant −g″ — is certified by the completed Szegő run (max|α| = 0.156); and the normalization is *derived, not fitted* (two independent lag routes agree to 1.8×10⁻¹⁰; the Weil formula identifies the target ξ′/ξ(1/2+σ) layer by layer, archimedean/pole/prime each gated). The inverse solve is classical — **the Levinson/Szegő recursion *is* the discrete Krein/Gelfand–Levitan equation** — the Verblunsky coefficients are the realized Hamiltonian data, causal by exact Toeplitz nesting, and **the realized Hamiltonian sees the primes**: |α|-spikes at r = log q for q = 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, offset ≤ 1 bin, contrast 2.8–5.2 (conditioning trivial: amplification ~23 under 10⁻²⁵ perturbations — the feared e^(cx) explosion belongs to the trig ladder, not the screw sections). The decisive measurement: with the 16 frozen σ values taken verbatim from the SVPIN spec, **all 16 rows converge, 16/16 truth-tight** (σ = 0.6: Δ = +7.30×10⁻⁵ → +6.33×10⁻⁷), the Weyl-disk contraction *measured* as R ~ e^(−(σ+c)L) with c = 0.85–1.76, median 1.36 (limit-point behaviour), and the round's surprise: **the disk center resums the missing tail** (median ratio 1.000; at σ = 0.6 it beats the truncated source sum by a factor ~600). The controls die *at the positivity level itself* — the strongest separation of the campaign: the prime-free SMOOTH accelerant **loses screw positivity** at window depth r = 0.264 (the same r at both mesh sizes — a continuum property), the scrambled world at r = 0.744: **the true primes — positions and weights — are necessary for the screw positivity of these windows.** And the screen every previous pin carrier failed is passed for the first time: **no Z1 cache transcription** (min over all N ≤ 7000 of the max-σ relative deviation 8.5×10⁻³ against the bar 10⁻⁶; AST firewall zero-free), with the carrier explicitly distinct from the dead de Branges route in input, direction and adjudication. The repricing, from measurements: the literal SVPIN trace-class norm is *measured flat* (16/16 non-decaying; the appended Verblunsky blocks grow — prime atoms arrive forever), so the correct currency is Weyl-disk contraction, and the minimal missing lemma is stated exactly: *let g be Suzuki's screw function with every finite section of −g″ positive (= localized Weil positivity); then the Weyl disks of the Krein realization contract with R_a(σ) ≤ C(ε)e^(−(σ+c)·2a) uniformly on σ ≥ 1/2 + ε, with limit point ξ′/ξ(1/2 + σ)* — suppliers: Krein's accelerant theorem and de Branges chain monotonicity; open is the uniformity as σ ↓ 1/2 — **and the honest price, unchanged: the all-a section-positivity hypothesis *is* localized Weil positivity itself.** The carrier localizes the remaining task (one disk-contraction rate for one explicit canonical system) and replaces the wrong trace-class currency by the right one; it does not remove the positivity input.

**Where the day's arc ends** (sandbox; no RH claim): the wall route closed as a theorem (the OCT of §8.9); the real-root architecture adjudicated with its support convicted circular, the missing object reduced to eigenvector sign-change positions with an exact source-only predictor (§8.10); the pin route as the sharpest reduction — countably many safe scalars in the absolutely convergent half-plane; the Krein carrier as the first host whose convergence is *not* a transcription of cached zeros — and at the bottom of every route, machine-verified, the same single input: Weil positivity, in one currency or another. RH is neither proven nor disproven; the program's map of why is now complete through the object-construction and resolvent directions, with the two open lemmata precisely stated: Weyl-disk contraction of one explicit canonical system (stated by round 90 with a σ ↓ 1/2 uniformity that round 92 later showed not load-bearing — pointwise at the pins suffices; the adjective is retired of record with round 109, §8.15), and the sign-change positions of one explicit ground eigenvector.

### 8.12 What any cohomology for Spec ℤ must supply: a machine-grounded specification (rounds CCCXCII, CCCXCIII and CCCXCV, 2026-08-15)

Three closing rounds then turned the campaign's characterization of the missing input into a literature-facing deliverable. Everything here is sandbox grade — four frozen probes in the experiments tree, no promotion, no marker move, no gate closed or narrowed, no RH claim in any direction; the suite stood at 906 modules at the freeze (949 today, after the v914/v915, v916/v917, v918–v921, v922–v925, v926–v930, v931–v935, v936–v941, v942–v948, v949–v954 and v955/v956 promotions recorded in §8.13–§8.15 and §8.17–§8.25).

**The patterns round (round 91, two probes, both verdicts honest negatives, both informative).** On the mandate "find new patterns, proofs, predictions," the program's first magnetar giant-flare QPO bed (`magnetar_qpo_ladder_probe.py`, 8/8, grep-verified unclaimed) tested SGR 1806−20 (18/26/30/92.5/150/625/1840 Hz) and SGR 1900+14 (28/53.5/84/155 Hz) against the geometric (3/2) relaxation ladder with a 20000-draw log-uniform MC null, a 17-base look-elsewhere battery, a power gate (planted ladder p = 5×10⁻⁵) and a specificity gate (integer harmonics p = 0.078 not misread as a ladder): base 1.5 gives p = 0.3163 and p = 0.3558, Fisher-joint p = 0.3584 — **verdict NULL**, S15-typed, the standing favorite remaining torsional crustal/magneto-elastic modes (on record and not escalated: placebo base 1.80 is best in both sources, an LEE-loaded base-battery artifact candidate). The LEE-controlled scan for an unclaimed dimensionless observable hit by the frozen compiler vocabulary (`unclaimed_observable_scan_probe.py`, 5/5 after the disclosed amendment A1 — gate G02 was mis-specified as a minimality bar and amended to a membership bar, no verdict bar moved) returned **SCAN-UNDECIDABLE** with the structural finding as the actual content: the vocabulary is *saturated* at 2σ tolerance — even the two positive controls have cheaper accidental hits than the corpus expressions (median placebo C_min 3.66 ≤ control C_max 5.46), so, read together with the round-86 transcription verdict, new *predictions* provably cannot come from numeric scanning and must be derived theory-side; new pattern *beds* remain legitimate, but this one is honestly NULL-typed.

**The Verblunsky round (round 92, `vbk_invariant_probe.py`, 23/23, deterministic 14.1/14.0 s, VERBLUNSKY-NO-INVARIANT(mechanism exhibited)).** The invariance attack on the well-conditioned screw coordinate, with three foundational corrections pinned first. (V0a) The σ-correction: σ_r = 1 + 1/r lies in (1, 2] and accumulates at the *interior* point 1, so pointwise Weyl-disk contraction on exactly that countable sequence plus the one-pin normal-family bound suffices for (SV) ⟹ RH — the σ ↓ 1/2 uniformity of round 90 is not load-bearing. (V0b) The exact δ₁ no-go: pin convergence cannot prove strict section positivity backwards — the carrier cannot supply its own existence premise. (V0c) The **Euler–Pick criterion, audited and pinned**: with P(z) = ξ′/ξ(1/2+z) and σ_j = 1 + 1/j, RH ⟺ P_N = [(P(σ_j)+P(σ_k))/(σ_j+σ_k)] ⪰ 0 for every N — forward, each zero contributes symbolically exactly the sum of two rank-one Grams; backward, the classical Pick theorem (Pick 1916; countable case by normal families), interior accumulation at 1, the identity theorem and the logarithmic pole residue give exactly the SVPIN pole contradiction, hypotheses gated one by one and finite N never sold as all N. The source-only falsification ladder from Γ, π, n^(−s) and an own Λ-sieve (cap 4×10⁶, 283146 primes, ψ ward 3.7×10⁻¹⁰) reads λ_min(P_N) = 4.59×10⁻² down to 2.44×10⁻⁶² for N = 1…12 — all positive (a certified negative eigenvalue would disprove RH), condition number 2.27×10⁶¹ at N = 12. And the **(AC) identity** c^T P_N c = ⟨−g″, f_c ⋆ f̃_c⟩ is gated symbolically exactly and independently numerically against the corpus-exact screw function g (8.581810362×10⁻² vs 8.578893345×10⁻², rel 3.4×10⁻⁴ at the declared L = 9 tail/taper): **Euler–Pick positivity *is* Weil positivity on exponential autocorrelations, by identity.** The kill itself: the complete p-packet update law is exact (Toeplitz, causal, inverse-update residuals ≤ 3.7×10⁻¹⁵), but a complete packet is *not* rank one — it is a high-rank indefinite Toeplitz update of displacement rank two (p = 2 at dims 128/160/180: ranks 26/90/130, inertias (13,102,13)/(45,70,45)/(65,50,65)), the induced Verblunsky step is nonlinear and prior-state dependent (measured state effect ×316), and sharpest: the true complete prime-packet sequence itself *exits* the Schur disk after p = 2/3/5/7 (r = 1.122/1.620/1.950/2.400) and only re-enters through later packets (after p = 11: max|α| = 0.313; full: 0.184) — no region inside the disk is preserved by every packet; the rescue is collective across packets, not multiplicative-local. The unit-disk control wards reproduce SMOOTH r = 0.264 and SCRARITH r = 0.744 exactly, with the r = 0.264 event *diagnosed*: not multiplicativity but a **support error** — the true prime measure has exactly zero mass before log 2 = 0.693 while the PNT density inserts false mass from u = 0 (0.282 by depth r); past log 2 the scramble shows the second condition — position kept, weight ×1.451, death at 0.744: exact log p/p^(m/2) weights are necessary, but no local conservation law exists. The only exact survival condition consumes the inverted prior section and is WALL-EQUIVALENT. Clean kill; the remaining all-depth principle is Weil positivity in yet another currency.

**The specification sheet (round: this section's close, `cohomspec_probe.py`, 20/20, 8.8 s, SPEC_SHA 423d934e, COHOMSPEC-COMPILED(7 properties: 6 machine-grounded, 1 citation-typed)).** The program's most valuable literature-facing artifact is the *measured shape of the missing theorem*, and this round compiles it as a requirements document: seven numbered properties that any candidate positivity mechanism — concretely, any cohomology/intersection theory for Spec ℤ supplying "a Weil pairing with structural positivity" — must have, each machine-grounded by grep ward or independent recomputation in its owning frozen artifact:

1. **It must produce the signed, alignment-carrying, unconditional, sub-spacing input.** The one open edge E4 consumes exactly the class v913 isolates (signed AND alignment-carrying) and round 81 prices (~1% of the mean zero spacing; minimal form MIN-U2, beyond-classical), over a completed candidate map (24 candidate sign sources, 0 pass, 19 fail). Its sharpest concrete corpus forms travel with it: the Parity Lemma counting law (surplus peak D_max = x − 3/8, marginal cost e^(−11) per forced skip over 23 decades — only the *positions* open) and the sign-position predictor (exact at all 79 lattice points, arithmetic load 4, minimum half-integer margin 0.0317 — **corrected of record by round 97, §8.13**: the exactness holds at x ≤ 13 only, the operative extension measured false from x = 21 onward with 567 τ-screened violations to the cache edge x = 890, a small-x accident of the margin budget; TPL(i) itself untouched, the eigenvector truth side unmeasured beyond x = 13). *(Grounded in `kill_atlas_dag_probe.py`, `v913`, `birdseye_shape_freedom_probe.py`, `tp_opus_probe.py`, `signpos_probe.frozen.log`; the correction in `sp_expsum_pricing_probe.py`.)*
2. **It must be shape-independent in the measured sense.** The requirement survives every audited admissible window family — the deployed family decays at exponent −3.379 (recomputed by OLS from the frozen five-cell ladder: −3.3795), the five alternatives are shallower by a measured gap ≥ 2.2 and lose the E7 density leg — so a pairing that works only for one test-function shape is not the missing theorem. *(`birdseye_shape_freedom_probe.py`, SHAPE-INDEPENDENCE-MEASURED.)*
3. **It cannot be a magnitude, density, or existence statement.** Littlewood empties the |ψ(x)−x| class (floor/slack 214.7 own-conservative, 1527.1 deployed); zero-density inputs are empty for A > 2 with A = 2 exactly critical; and soft existence is closed at the border-membership step — some positive extension *always* exists, and "the arithmetic completion is positive" is equivalent to s ≥ 0 both ways via det M = s·det B with ellipsoid margin exactly s (recomputed in exact Fractions in the probe). *(`kill_atlas_dag_probe.py`, `birdseye_shape_freedom_probe.py`, EXISTENCE-IS-DISGUISE.)*
4. **Its Gram form on exponential autocorrelations must *be* the Euler–Pick matrices.** This is the concrete interface any cohomological trace formula must match: if the theory's pairing produces Weil positivity, then evaluated on the test class f_c(v) = 1_{v≥0} Σ c_j e^(−σ_j v) it is *forced*, by the (AC) identity, to reproduce the matrices P_N — recomputed in the probe symbolically exactly and numerically (floor ladder N = 1…12 reproduced at max rel dev 3.8×10⁻⁹; the (AC) weld at rel devs ≤ 4.3×10⁻¹¹ against the frozen reads). A proposed trace formula can be tested against this interface *today*, at any finite N. *(`vbk_invariant_probe.py` V0c.)*
5. **Its positivity must fail on the control worlds exactly as measured.** The control deaths are consumption requirements: the pairing must consume the exact prime support (the prime-free smooth world dies at window depth r = 0.264 by the sub-log 2 support error — recomputed) and the exact log p/p^(m/2) weights (the golden-scramble world dies at r = 0.744 with positions kept and first weight ×1.451 — recomputed). A "structural positivity" that survives either control world is structurally incapable of being the missing input. *(`vbk_invariant_probe.py`, `krein_screw_realization_probe.py`.)*
6. **It must survive the τ-screen.** Under the frozen screen (PASS band |slope| ≤ 0.30, RELOCATION at ≥ 0.70), fifteen campaign routes died as the wall scalar renamed, and the single ADVANCE does not land on the open edge — a cohomological positivity whose margin tracks τ at slope ~1 is coordinate system number eight, not a theorem. *(`kill_atlas_dag_probe.py`, gate A3.2.)*
7. **The function-field precedent, mapped to where it breaks (citation-typed).** In Weil's function-field proof the pairing is the intersection pairing on the surface C × C, the structural positivity is the Castelnuovo–Severi/Hodge-index inequality, and Frobenius supplies the special cycle whose self-intersection the inequality controls. For Spec ℤ the sought analogues are: positivity ↔ properties 1–6 above; Frobenius ↔ the arithmetic flow — Deninger's foliated dynamical system, or the Connes–Consani scaling action whose periodic orbits already carry lengths log p; the surface ↔ the missing absolute-geometry substrate ("Spec ℤ ×_𝔽₁ Spec ℤ", the square of the scaling site). The break points, verified against the current literature: Deninger's program has the *shape* of the cohomology and reads the explicit formula as a dynamical Lefschetz trace formula (Doc. Math. ICM 1998; arXiv:math/0505354), and the conjectured trace formula is now *proved* for smooth foliated flows with simple closed orbits and transversely simple preserved leaves (Álvarez López–Kordyukov, arXiv:2402.06671) — but no arithmetic dynamical system realizing Spec ℤ has been constructed, so the trace formula has no arithmetic host yet. Connes–Consani have Riemann–Roch on the periodic orbits C_p of the scaling site and for the ring ℤ (Selecta Math. 2017 and sequels) but *not* on the square of the scaling site — which is exactly the surface substitute whose Hodge-index inequality would be the structural positivity; their semilocal/prolate line supplies the archimedean-place positivity (arXiv:2006.13771, Selecta Math. 2021) and reduces RH to an Euler-factor property P(n) (arXiv:2310.18423) — Weil positivity in the corpus's measured currency, with the prime places exactly where property 5's control deaths sit; and Connes' 2026 extremal construction (arXiv:2602.04022; zero reality by Connes–van Suijlekom Thm 6.1) leaves open precisely the convergence the corpus adjudicated REALROOT-DEPTH-UNDECIDED (§8.10). The corpus-checkable content of this property is that the cited extremal construction is carried and was executed here, and that the interface such a theory must match is exactly the (AC) object of property 4; the external literature claims are citations, never gates.

**Honesty fence, in the same breath.** The specification sheet is a *requirements document derived from machine-checked no-gos*: it proves nothing about Deninger's program or Connes–Consani, asserts no defect and no progress of either, claims no progress on RH in any direction, and explicitly types as **open** whether any cohomological theory can meet the specification — the standing counter-evidence (NO-WITNESS: all certified reads positive to h = 12632) and the finite-rung scope of the OCT (§8.9, limitation 8) travel with every property. What the sheet adds over the published state of the art is precision, not evidence: the missing theorem's interface is now stated sharply enough to be *tested against* — at finite N, by machine — before anyone proves anything.

### 8.13 The three lemma forms, the certified instruments, and a correction of record (rounds CCCXCIV and CCCXCVI–CDIII, 2026-08-15)

Nine further freeze rounds of the same day — several run as concurrent lanes — then finished the campaign's instrument tier and, in one case, corrected its own published record. Everything here is sandbox grade — eight frozen probes plus a Lean formalization round in the experiments tree, no promotion at the freeze, no marker move, no gate closed or narrowed, no RH claim in any direction; the suite stood at 906 modules at the freeze. *Promotion of record (2026-08-15):* the certified Euler–Pick floors of rounds 95 and 100 have since been promoted to the load-bearing suite as `v915_eulerpick_certified_floors.py` (the N ≤ 3 floors and all cited-bound wards recomputed in-run, the cap-10¹³ N = 4 intervals pinned from the run-of-record with the split disclosed, and the §8.15 reach cap typed in: disproof-sound but reach-capped at γ ~ 5–8, zero live falsification power in the classically certified range); the suite stands at 949 modules (after the later Epstein detection-arc, prime-front theorem-arc, spectral-balance/edge arc, endgame-arc, triple-cofinal-arc, post-bughunt-arc, mechanism-arc and Bughunt-X-arc promotions, §8.17–§8.24). Everything else in this section remains sandbox. One correction of record travels with this section and is carried where the corrected claim lives (§8.10, §8.12 property 1, and limitation 10): round 97, below.

**The collective-rescue round (round 93, `collective_rescue_probe.py`, 10/10 wards + 7/7 law gates, COLLECTIVE-IS-SUPPORT).** Round 92's exit/re-entry phenomenon is *fully explained* — no new structural law, no prime conspiracy, no Lyapunov functional. Half of the law is a theorem: the tent moment c_d reads only atoms within one mesh of d·δ, so truncating the stream above u_miss leaves the Verblunsky prefix bit-identical (verified spread exactly 0) and the exit can never come before the exact causal onset; the only empirical content is the upper offset, measured at **onset + ~2 lags** across all truncations (the round-92 re-entry demystified: nothing is rescued, the window just gets covered). The completed stream's cumulative max|α| is *constant* at 0.183932 from r = 2.568 to 4.788 with band maxima falling — strictly a finite-prefix measurement, never quantifier evidence. All Lyapunov candidates are killed with numbers (by causality the fixed-prefix candidate class is *empty*), and the mass-restored control dies at the identical lag as the truncation it imitates — **mass-without-atoms does not rescue**: the exit law is support bookkeeping, but in-window survival requires the exact arithmetic weights.
