**The Euler–Pick certified ladder (round 95, `eulerpick_ladder_probe.py`, 31/31, EULERPICK-CERTIFIED + EULERPICK-GENERIC-DECAY + EULERPICK-DETECTION-LAW).** The round-92 falsification ladder rebuilt to *certified* standard: outward-rounded interval floors end to end — N = 1: [4.5917135×10⁻², 4.5917136×10⁻²], N = 2: [9.0288701×10⁻⁶, 9.0289075×10⁻⁶], N = 3 certified positive at cap 1.6×10⁷: [2.3643695×10⁻¹⁰, 1.1497752×10⁻⁹] — the falsification channel implemented, self-tested on a planted negative family, and **empty** (a certified hi < 0 at any N would disprove RH by the exact forward direction alone). The honest wall is the tail, never precision. The decay is *generic*: a control decomposition on the same nodes (Cauchy → RvM density → gap → quantile-atom discreteness) lands within 1.2× of the Euler value with the true γ₁ appearing nowhere — the decay carries no arithmetic information. The detection law from planted off-line quadruples reads **N\*(δ) = 6.1 + 1.95·log₁₀(1/δ)** with measured γ-blindness (N\*(0.1) = 8/12/22 at γ = 14/20/30; γ = 50 undetected through N = 36 — reach costs O(γ) rungs). Promotion was honestly declined at the freeze — the certified slice sees off-line zeros only at γ ~ 5–8, territory classical verification closed decades ago — and later executed with exactly that reach cap typed into the module and the ledger row (`v915_eulerpick_certified_floors.py`, 2026-08-15).

**The rigidity round (round 96, `rigidity_inverse_probe.py`, 9/9, RIGIDITY-TIGHTENING).** The inverse problem answered: screw positivity to depth r pins the measure onto the prime comb *exponentially tightly*, and the pinning rate is exactly the conditioning price. Survival moduli shrink with depth (median factor 3.78 over 22 channels); **all 17 adversarial impostor candidates die** — the shifted-prime comb p → p+2, the golden quasi-prime comb at exact prime density, equal-spaced crystals, mass-matched crystal tails (atomicity plus correct mass is *not* enough), sub-mesh splits at every scale, moment-matched jitters, a single deep weight swap — and all 15 single-atom deletions are fatal within J ≤ 8 lags of causal onset. The moduli law ln η\*(r) = −1.073r − 7.08 gives c_mod = 1.073 against the λ_min ladder slope c_λ = 0.814, within the frozen band and sharper via the rigorous eigenvalue survival floor: **rigidity is the conditioning price up to an O(1) alignment factor**, not a new arithmetic-free law. The theorem candidate is typed honestly (anchors machine-measured, law fitted, all-depth open), and its hypothesis remains localized Weil positivity — unchanged currency.

**The SP-pricing round (round 97, `sp_expsum_pricing_probe.py`, 13/13, SP-OPERATIVE-FALSIFIED + SP-PRICED + SP-CLASSICALLY-OUT-OF-REACH(×8.2) + SP-CONDITIONAL-LADDER + SP-PREFIX-DEAD-IN-WINDOW + SP-LITERAL-NONVANISHING-IN-RANGE — the correction of record for §8.10 and §8.12).** The round-88 rounding-margin statement priced against the full classical exponential-sum arsenal — and the measurement overtook the pricing. The pricing first, all constants generous, deepest rung x = 890 / t_top = 6990 against the need 0.5: trivial ℓ¹ 5.53, van der Corput second/third derivative 5.23/5.53, the Bourgain exponent pair 5.53, the Vaughan+Vinogradov ceiling via the explicit S(t) = O(log t) 4.11 — **the best unconditional miss is ×8.2**, Lindelöf/density give no pointwise constant, and even RH itself (Carneiro–Chandee–Milinovich, plus Montgomery's Ω) stays above the budget at 1.015 on the deepest rung: the budget is unreachable *and* measured exceeded. The measurement: the census extended from 79 points (x ≤ 13) to 18798 points (x ≤ 890) shows the operative predictor form **false from x = 21 onward with 567 τ-screened counterexamples** — the round-88 exactness was a small-x accident of the margin budget, by an arithmetic-free mechanism (the Fejér taper's tracking-variance floor, rms → 1/(2π) = 0.159, measured 0.26, against budget 0.5 — no taper in this family survives); the prefix/exceptional-set salvage is dead in-window (the lowest violation drops to k/K = 0.010 and the prefix depth stalls at ≤ 131); the literal non-vanishing form survives trivially and is contentless without a repulsion law. The fence: what died is the deployed source-side approximant, not TPL(i) itself — the eigenvector truth side is unmeasured beyond x = 13.

**The CCM round (round 98, `ccm_pn_adjudication_probe.py`, 22/22, CCM-CHECKED + CCM-SAME-CURRENCY).** The one external *published* RH reduction adjudicated: Connes–Consani–Moscovici's P(n) property (arXiv:2310.18423; P(n) for all n ⟺ RH, open for every prime-bearing n > 2, and quantifying over an infinite-dimensional cone per fixed n — not finitely checkable in full). Two results. First, a **new finite instrument**: Galerkin sections of Q_n itself (24 tents, both pole constraints projected exactly, the prime side exactly finite) — **all six prime-bearing sections positive** (λ_min = 2.30×10⁻³ at n = 2.5 down to 1.01×10⁻⁵ at n = 13, certified budgets ≤ 5.8×10⁻⁶⁰; the pole constraint is load-bearing — the unconstrained form is −0.093 at n = 3); a certified negative anywhere would have refuted RH, and the channel stays empty. Second, the interface test: CCM's published local terms reproduce ξ′/ξ(1/2+σ) to rel 2.3×10⁻⁵⁰, and their quadratic form on the exponential-autocorrelation family **reproduces the corpus Euler–Pick matrices entrywise** — specification-sheet property 4 executed on the external reduction itself; the τ-screen types it τ-RELOCATION: the wall renamed. The controls die through the same construction (SMOOTH between n = 3 and 6, SCRARITH at n = 6, EPSTEIN at n = 6/13) — P(n) is arithmetic-sensitive at the positivity level exactly like the screw carrier, and the honest comparison is an identity: **P(n) *is* localized Weil positivity**, n-localized but infinite-dimensional per instance. What the corpus adds to CCM's own gap: the (AC) interface makes any semilocal prolate candidate finitely testable today, the control deaths are consumption requirements now reproduced in the P(n) currency, and the certified Euler–Pick ladder prices certified P(n)-type checks.

**The Lean round (round 99, four new modules under `TfptCarrier/`, full incremental lake build green, audit 7/7; 27 kernel-checked headline theorems with axiom footprint exactly [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound], no sorry, no native_decide).** The reduction chain's best pieces are now kernel-checked. `EulerPick.lean`: the exact per-zero identity, the Gram-sum form, and `pickMatrix_posSemidef` at the SV nodes σ_j = 1 + 1/(j+1) for every N — **the forward direction of the Euler–Pick criterion, kernel-checked for finite ordinate families** (the identification with ξ′/ξ and the backward Pick direction stay cited classical input, docstring-typed). `DeltaOneNoGo.lean`: the δ₁ counterexample package — PSD but never positive definite for n ≥ 2 while the Carathéodory value stays positive. `ParityLemma.lean`, the largest piece: blow-up laws, existence directions, the combinatorial core, and the **full round-85 law** zero_count_even_iff_opposite_residues — under *one named simplicity hypothesis* (alternating signs at canonical gap midpoints; exactly where zero simplicity enters, docstring-typed), with boundary signs and gap-sign constancy proven, not assumed. `SVSkeleton.lean`, partial by design: the pin geometry proven (accumulation at the interior point 1), the hypothesis package with three cited fields, the composition sv_implies_rh — and **the honesty lock** `skeleton_not_unconditional`, an instance with RH = False: the packaging alone yields nothing.

**The N = 4 certification round (round 100, `sieve4_eulerpick_n4_probe.py`, 22/22, 2682 s; segmented byte sieve to cap X = 10¹³, with the hard external ward π(10¹³) = 346,065,536,839 reproduced exactly by the instrument).** **λ_min(𝒫₄) ∈ [8.278338×10⁻¹⁵, 1.3840906×10⁻¹⁴] is certified positive** (margin 0.749), the N ≤ 3 floors tightened massively (lo₃ width 9.1×10⁻¹⁰ → 1.3×10⁻¹⁶), and the falsification channel stays open and empty. The round also corrects round 95's upgrade-path model, load-bearingly: its X_req omitted the tail floor beyond the Büthe range x > 10¹⁹ — with ψ ≥ 0 alone that floor exceeds λ₄ *at any cap*, so the named path was not worst-case executable; one airtight elementary input unblocks it — **Nair 1982: lcm(1..n) ≥ 2^(n−1), hence ψ(x) ≥ (x−2)·ln 2** (warded against the round's own sieve data) — giving the corrected X_req(4) = 10^11.52 (the realized crude assembly also certified at 10¹³; Nair makes the certificate a-priori guaranteed and triples the margin). And the N = 5 wall is corrected: it is *not* sieve-walled — the beyond-10¹⁹ floor exceeds λ₅ by a factor 4.7×10⁵, so no sieve of any size helps; **the knowledge wall starts at N = 5**, not N = 6 as round 95 estimated.

**The α-induction round (round 101, `screwind_induction_probe.py`, 10/10 wards + 5/8 law gates — the three fails are findings, typed; companion `screwind_meshdrift_check.py`; SCREWIND-KICK-LAW(exact) + SCREWIND-STEP-IS-WALL + SCREWIND-DICHOTOMY(descriptive-only)).** The one inductive attack never attempted, in the best-conditioned coordinate — with a **depth record**: all sections positive to r = 9.0 at both meshes (the corpus record was 4.788), sup|ρ| = 0.183932 with margin 0.816 attained at k = 0 — the stream never exceeds its own first coefficient. The flatness is carried by a cancellation of giants (band medians ρ_arch = +288.5 vs ρ_prime = −288.5 at u = 9 against net RMS 0.023), and **the exact kick law** holds at every atom arrival (affine-exact, deviation ≤ 2.8×10⁻¹⁶, numerator pure arithmetic in (p, m, δ)); the informative gate fail: net arrival α is *positive* on 33/39 and the atom-bin median |ρ| sits below background — **prime arrivals stabilize**; the naive atoms-push-down picture is wrong. The sharpest true statement is an induction whose side condition is wall-equivalent (literally the round-92 object) — **the wall relocates into the certificate** — and the named open piece is stated exactly: a source-certified two-sided energy floor e(u) ≥ C·e^(−κ₊u) with κ₊ < 1/2 would make the kicks self-improving (kick exponent κ − 1/2 = −0.322 < 0 measured everywhere), but that floor *is* localized positivity: the cleanest statement yet of where the wall sits in this coordinate.

**The Hausdorff safe-point round (round 102, `hausdorff_safepoint_probe.py`, 40/40, 143.6 s; composite verdict HAUSDORFF-EQUIVALENCE-SOUND + HAUSDORFF-REPRODUCED + HAUSDORFF-CERTIFIED-DEPTH(86; γ\* 189/563) + HAUSDORFF-REPLICATION + HAUSDORFF-DETECTOR + HAUSDORFF-Z1-ZEROMEASURE + NOT-WORLD-NEW).** An externally proposed moment-field criterion built, audited and adjudicated: RH ⟺ positivity of the full Pascal field C_{n,k}(a) at the safe point a = 256 in the Euler half-plane. **The equivalence skeleton is sound**: the Hausdorff/Widder hypotheses need no extra boundedness, no-atom-at-0 is gated, and the one sharp continuation step closes (shared Taylor data, identity theorem on the slit plane minus the discrete pole set, removability at any putative off-axis pole — contradiction), with the companions symbolically exact. The proposer's spec is numerically faithful in every reproducible detail (561/561 cells positive, weakest C₁₆,₁₆ ratio 1.000220 to claim, pins to 6.452×10⁻³⁷). The headline: **certified depth 86 (5290 cells) with γ\*-reach 189 at a = 256 and 563 on the a = 1024 arm — versus Euler–Pick's certified N = 3 (γ ~ 5–8): the cheapest certified RH-consequence instrument in the corpus by a wide margin** (143 s total; the min-cell margin decays by the spectral law, slope −0.3099 vs prediction −0.304, not Cauchy-catastrophically). Three-carrier replication: signpos, the round-90 Krein carrier, and the SV pins agree — **three source-only carriers with no zero cache anywhere in construction**. The detector, honestly priced: the matched-ray phase is O(δ³)-stationary, so detection runs only through the k = 0 channel and the measured law d\* ~ 1/δ holds below γ₁ — **above γ₁ the Pascal field is provably blind at the safe point**, made concrete by the Epstein control: an off-line witness zero *located* at ρ = 0.69692705 + 36.37406369i (RH-for-Q provably false; correction of record, round 123/§8.17: that zero was never γ-minimal — the located off-line census of ξ_Q holds four pairs below 45, and the γ-minimal *driver* 0.9330+15.6682i carries 26× its excess) while the Q Pascal field stays positive to depth 100 exactly as the phase model predicts; the smooth world dies immediately (first negative at depth 8). Z1 typing: source-only in *input*, zero measure in *content*, like Krein. And the honest novelty fence: **NOT-WORLD-NEW** — Zhang (arXiv:2303.09396, Thm 2) is the identical criterion at the central point; the novelty is the safe-point shift into the Euler half-plane, the source-only decoder, and the certified conditioning. The naked remaining task: positivity of *all* C_{n,k}(a) — Weil positivity in Hausdorff currency, **the third lemma form** beside the standing two (and, per the rounds-108/109 correction of record, §8.15, the *only* one of the three that is a proven RH-equivalence); the certificate extends the finite no-witness region far beyond Euler–Pick but touches the all-(n,k) quantifier nowhere.

**Where the campaign now stands** (sandbox; no RH claim; wording corrected of record by the round-108 logic audit and the round-109 bughunt, §8.15): the one open input now holds **three RH-sufficient lemma forms** — Weyl-disk contraction of one explicit canonical system (pointwise at the pins, per the round-92 correction; the stale adjective "uniform" is retired with round 109), the sign positions of one explicit ground eigenvector, and Hausdorff cell positivity of one explicit Pascal field. Of the three, **the Hausdorff form is a proven RH-equivalence**; the Weyl-disk and sign-position forms are **one-sided pendants** — form ⇒ RH established, RH ⇒ form open (their converses are, respectively, the round-90 open lemma and the CF-realness/Connes-§6.6 hypotheses) — and every mutual equivalence that holds routes **through RH**: no direct form-to-form edge exists, **transfer is none** (the round-108 counterexample world: an off-line quadruple at γ = 20 keeps every Hausdorff cell positive at n + k ≤ 50 while the Euler–Pick section fires at N = 12), and **certified regions are form-local**. Around them: **three agreeing source-only carriers**, **two certified falsification instruments** (the Euler–Pick ladder at N ≤ 4; the Hausdorff field at depth 86 — both disproof-sound but reach-capped, §8.15), the rigidity law explaining quantitatively why every control had to die, and 27 kernel-checked theorems around the reduction chain. At the bottom, unchanged: localized Weil positivity. RH is neither proven nor disproven; the record is now instrument-grade in three currencies.

### 8.14 The road from here: a machine-gated path to a complete proof (rounds 103–105, notes CDV–CDVI, 2026-08-15)

Three closing rounds of the same day — a maximal Form-A proof attempt, the adjudication of the last unexamined classical theorem class, and a compilation round — then produced the one artifact the campaign never had: a staged, milestone-gated research roadmap from today's frozen state to a complete RH proof, in which every stage is falsifiable against the corpus's own instruments. Everything here is sandbox grade — three frozen probes in the experiments tree, no promotion, no marker move, no gate closed or narrowed, no RH claim in any direction; the suite stood at 906 modules at the freeze. *Promotion of record (2026-08-15):* the round-103 Form-A theorem (extended by round 106, §8.15) has since been promoted as `v914_pascal_region_theorem.py` — a certified finite theorem from the cited inputs, form-local, explicitly not RH evidence — and the certified Euler–Pick floors as `v915_eulerpick_certified_floors.py`; the suite stands at 949 modules (§8.17–§8.25); the roadmap itself remains sandbox. The honesty fences travel first: **a roadmap is not progress on RH; no stage is claimed started; and the mathematics of Stages 1–2 does not yet exist** — the roadmap's entire value is that every step is now falsifiable and interface-defined, so any candidate, from anyone, anywhere, can be adjudicated against frozen gates in minutes.

**The two feeder rounds.** The Form-A round (round 103, `moonshot_sol_probe.py`, MOONSHOT-PARTIAL) proved, from cited external inputs only (Platt–Trudgian 2021: all zeros to height T = 3,000,175,332,800 on the line; Rosser's 1941 counting envelope; the classical γ₁ isolation — no zero table, no zeta evaluator), the **largest positivity region of the campaign**: at the safe point a = 256, the full Pascal field satisfies C\_{n,k}(256) > 0 for *every* n ≥ 0 and every 0 ≤ k ≤ 10¹⁹ (extended 530-fold to k ≤ 7.4×10²¹ by round 106, §8.15) — an infinite half-strip in n and ten quintillion complete k-columns, with the honest scope stated in the theorem itself: it touches the k → ∞ quantifier nowhere and therefore proves nothing about RH; the same round killed the three suggested proof routes with named mechanisms (the exact prime-side transmutation turns the positive Beta spectral window into a sign-changing generalized-Laguerre source window — the classical Laguerre zero theorem, so no termwise-positive prime comparison exists; the Killip–Simon/Szegő direction dies because the zero comb is pure point, w = 0 a.e., entropy −∞, floor e ≥ 0 only; the asymptotic-a transport is nonuniform in (n,k)). The class round (round 104, `levinson_class_probe.py`, 23/23, LEVINSON-ADJUDICATED) priced the last formally unexamined classical theorem type about zero positions — the positive-proportion-on-the-line class (Selberg 1942, Levinson 1974, Conrey 1989, up to the standing record κ > 0.417293 of Pratt–Robles–Zaharescu–Zeindler 2020) — against the wall budget: the literal (liminf) class constrains *nothing* at any finite height, and even the granted-effective reading breaches every deployed cell by 2–3 orders of magnitude; a proportion theorem would have to come within 1 − κ\* < 4.8×10⁻³ of *all* zeros (κ\*\_ledger = 0.9952/0.9983/0.9988 at the three cells, each a lower bound) before it could feed the wall — **the atlas tally moves 24 → 25 candidates, 0 PASS**, and with it every named unconditional classical theorem type about zero positions is formally priced: zero-free regions, zero density, Weyl equidistribution, pair correlation, positive proportion. What remains open is exactly the named object itself: a signed, alignment-carrying, sub-spacing ordinate statement beyond the named types.

**The roadmap (round 105, `roadmap_probe.py`, 29/29, SPEC_SHA `79092ebf`, verdict ROADMAP-COMPILED(stages, gates 29/29)).** Every machine-groundable claim below is asserted by the probe — acceptance-gate values re-computed from cited constants or grep-warded verbatim in their owning frozen artifacts, the stage DAG checked acyclic with exactly the named open stages, the downstream chain's artifacts present with verdicts grep-verified.

*Stage 0 — done and frozen (what the roadmap starts from).* The candidate atlas complete and adjudicated (25 candidate sign sources, 0 pass); the implication DAG with exactly one open edge E4; the three lemma forms (each RH-sufficient; only the Hausdorff form a proven equivalence — the rounds-108/109 correction of record, §8.15); the two certified falsification instruments with open-and-empty channels (Euler–Pick floors N = 1: [4.5917135×10⁻², 4.5917136×10⁻²] down to N = 4: [8.278338×10⁻¹⁵, 1.3840906×10⁻¹⁴] at sieve cap 10¹³; the Hausdorff field at certified depth 86, 5290 cells, γ\*-reach 189/563); the largest proven positivity region (all n, k ≤ 10¹⁹ at a = 256 — re-derived inside the probe from the cited constants, decisive ratios 1.176577/335.8/1416.8, the mechanism wall re-located between k = 1.40×10¹⁹ and 1.41×10¹⁹); the rigidity law (ln η\*(r) = −1.073r − 7.08, conditioning-priced); the seven-property specification sheet; and 27 kernel-checked theorems.

*Stage 1 — the substrate (open; new mathematics).* The missing mathematics is **structural positivity of Hodge-index type for Spec ℤ**. The verified literature state (§8.12 property 7, machine-warded): Deninger's dynamical Lefschetz trace formula is *proved* for smooth foliated flows (Álvarez López–Kordyukov, arXiv:2402.06671) but **no arithmetic dynamical system realizing Spec ℤ exists — the trace formula has no arithmetic host**; Connes–Consani have Riemann–Roch on the periodic orbits of the scaling site and for the ring ℤ, but **not on the square of the scaling site** — exactly the surface substitute whose Hodge-index inequality would be the structural positivity. Two alternative milestones, either sufficient: **(1a)** an arithmetic dynamical system hosting the proven trace formula, whose periodic orbits are log p with weights log p/p^(m/2) — *acceptance gate*: its trace formula, evaluated on the exponential test class, must reproduce the (AC)/Euler–Pick interface c^T P_N c = ⟨−g″, f_c ⋆ f̃_c⟩ at the 16 frozen σ of the SVPIN spec (`vbk_invariant_probe.py` V0c; `krein_screw_realization_probe.py`) — the corpus can check any candidate in minutes; **(1b)** or Riemann–Roch on the square of the scaling site — *acceptance gate*: the resulting intersection pairing, evaluated on exponential autocorrelations, must reproduce the 𝒫_N ladder, with the certified N ≤ 4 floors above as the frozen acceptance values.

*Stage 2 — the index inequality (open; new mathematics).* The Hodge-index/Castelnuovo analog on the Stage-1 substrate — the structural positivity statement itself. Its acceptance gates are the corpus's measured consumption requirements: it must **fail on the surrogate worlds exactly at the measured depths** (the prime-free smooth world at window depth r = 0.264 by the sub-log 2 support error; the weight-scrambled world at r = 0.744 — a positivity surviving either control is structurally incapable of being the missing input); it must be **signed and alignment-carrying** (the v913 class — no magnitude, density or existence statement can substitute); it must **survive the τ-screen** (frozen bands 0.30/0.70 — a margin tracking the wall scalar at slope ~1 is a coordinate system, not a theorem); and its **finite sections must reproduce the certified Hausdorff cells** (depth 86) **and strictly extend the k ≤ 10¹⁹ theorem's reach** — enlarge the certified region, not rename it.

*Stage 3 — the descent (conditional-complete; assembly, already machine-held).* From the Stage-2 index inequality to localized Weil positivity in *any one* of the three lemma forms, then the extraction the machine already holds: `v848_extraction_chain.py` (EXTRACTION-CHAIN-COMPLETE — cofinal finite positivity ⇒ Weil positivity ⇒ RH, hypothesis (H) isolated), `v912_form_convergence_theorem.py` (the unconditional rate O(D² log(1/D))), the Lean kernel tier (`CofinalWeil`/`CofinalPredefinition`, `EulerPick.pickMatrix_posSemidef`, `SVSkeleton.sv_implies_rh` with the honesty lock `skeleton_not_unconditional`, `ParityLemma`, `DeltaOneNoGo`), the SVPIN skeleton (SVPIN-ROUTE-OPEN), the Hausdorff equivalence (HAUSDORFF-EQUIVALENCE-SOUND), and the CCM interface (CCM-SAME-CURRENCY — any external candidate lands in the same currency). No step of Stage 3 is new mathematics; what it consumes is exactly the Stage-2 output. The round-108 fence travels with "conditional-complete" (§8.15): the only kernel-checked end-to-end extraction (v848 + CofinalWeil.lean) accepts *only* the wall form H_cof, which is not among the three entry forms and is not producible from them without RH (test-class mismatch; the Lean extraction tier never mentions the Hausdorff family, absence-gated); the per-form descents are probe-gated plus classical — and the weakest link is the Stage-2→3 *interface* itself: the acceptance gates are finite (cells n + k ≤ 86) while the entry forms are infinite-quantifier statements, and no corpus machinery bridges that jump (v912 bridges convergence, not positivity).

*The falsification spine (parallel, permanent).* The two certified instruments keep running as standing disproof channels — a single certified negative at any Euler–Pick N or any Hausdorff cell would be an RH-disproof candidate; both channels are open and empty. The round-108 reach cap travels with the headline (§8.15): both instruments are *disproof-sound* — the disproof direction consumes only the proven forward implication — but *reach-capped* far below Platt–Trudgian's 3.0×10¹² (Euler–Pick's certified N ≤ 4 sees off-line zeros only at γ ≈ 5–8; the Hausdorff field is provably blind above γ₁ = 14.13 at the safe point), so "open and empty" is a theorem given the cited inputs, not an observation — the certified ranges carry zero live falsification power. The named extension walls are knowledge walls, not compute walls: the Euler–Pick N = 5 rung needs ψ-knowledge beyond 10¹⁹ (the floor exceeds λ₅ by 4.7×10⁵ at any sieve cap), and the Form-A n = 0 mechanism exhausts at k ≈ 1.405×10¹⁹ (this mechanism wall relocated by round 106's stronger mechanism to k ≈ 7.44×10²¹, §8.15).

*The honest pricing.* Stage 1 needs **new mathematics** — an arithmetic host for the proven trace formula (Deninger's program; the Álvarez López–Kordyukov side is done, the arithmetic side unconstructed) or the square-site Riemann–Roch (Connes–Consani's program; on the orbits and for ℤ done, on the square open) — and the people working closest to both milestones are the named authors of those programs. Stage 2 needs **new mathematics** — the structural positivity itself; the corpus proves its complete interface and its obstruction map, not its existence, and the standing counter-evidence (NO-WITNESS: all certified reads positive) travels with it. Stage 3 is **assembly**, already machine-held. The corpus's contribution to every stage is the same: the acceptance gates that turn each milestone into a minutes-scale machine check. No timeline is promised; no stage is claimed started; nothing here is progress on RH.

### 8.15 The frontier extension, the code reading, and the campaign audited — the corrections of record (rounds 106–109, notes CDVII–CDIX, 2026-08-15)

Four closing rounds of the same day — run as concurrent lanes — extended the roadmap's Stage-0 frontier, adjudicated the program's founding intuition under its own name, and then turned the audit instruments on the campaign's own headlines. Everything here is sandbox grade at its freeze — four frozen probes in the experiments tree, no promotion at the freeze, no marker move, no gate closed or narrowed, no RH claim in any direction; the suite stood at 906 modules at the freeze. *Promotion of record (2026-08-15):* the round-106 frontier extension below — with the round-103 base theorem and the HSW attribution resolved from the paper itself (Cor. 1.2 is the N(t) bound used; Cor. 1.4 is the companion S(t) bound, not used) — has since been promoted as `v914_pascal_region_theorem.py`, and the certified Euler–Pick floors as `v915_eulerpick_certified_floors.py`; the suite stands at 949 modules (§8.17–§8.25); the audits and the code reading remain sandbox. This is, first of all, a **corrections section**: the round-108 big-picture logic audit and the round-109 bughunt (the first adversarial audit of rounds 86–105; the round-87 hunt covered only 79–85) convicted the published "three equivalent lemma forms" language and two related headlines, and the corrected wordings are carried in §8.13, §8.14 and limitation 11, per the round-87/97 convention — frozen probes and notes are not retro-edited; the correction is the record. No number was wrong; no verdict enum flips.

**The frontier extension (round 106, `moonshot_o3_probe.py`, 16/16 gates, ~104 s, MOONSHOT3-EXTENDED + MOONSHOT3-PARTIAL + MOONSHOT3-OBSTRUCTED).** The round-103 theorem extends 530-fold: **C_{n,k}(256) > 0 for every n ≥ 0 and every 0 ≤ k ≤ 7,444,682,106,464,286,365,865** (≈ 7.44×10²¹), proven from Platt–Trudgian 2021 plus the *corrected* Hasanalizade–Shen–Wong zero-counting bound (JNT 235 (2022), Cor. 1.2: |N(t) − M(t)| ≤ 0.1038·log t + 0.2573·log log t + 9.3675) — with the honest source correction load-bearing: the commissioned (0.110, 0.290, 2.290) constants bound S(T), not N(T) directly, and their Cheng–Graham input was later identified as invalid — *replaced, not patched*. The mechanism retains the previously discarded q^k factor, counts verified zeros in 16384 RvM packets below T − 1, encloses the unknown tail by a Gaussian series above T, and closes a 33-cell endpoint partition; the worst gate is the last cell (lower/tail ratio 1.000001535, minimum packet count 25,397,045.4), and the next 2×10⁻⁶ relative step fails this mechanism — the frontier is mechanism-exact. **The general law is derived: k_front(T) = (λ∞ + O(1/log T))·T²/(a − ½) with λ∞ = 0.226987054723979955** (defined by erfc(√(a/(a−½))·√λ) = √(a/(a−½))·erf(√λ)) — verification height qT buys q² columns — and the formal geometric ceiling k_geo = (T² + ¼)/(a − ½) is intrinsically unreachable by this mechanism (RvM mass above T dominates near it; the route stops at 0.227·k_geo). New exact structure travels with it: the two-variable generating function in **closed form**, (X + Y − XY)·G = X·a·F(a(1−X)) + Y·(a/(1−Y))·F(a/(1−Y)), equivalently G(X,Y) = Σ_ρ y_ρ/((1 − X·y_ρ)(1 − Y·(1 − y_ρ))), with the pole-divisor *equivalence* (all joint pole divisors positive-real and > 1 ⟺ every y_ρ ∈ (0,1) ⟺ RH — an equivalence, not an RH proof); under RH the array is **totally sign-regular in the reversed-k orientation** (the standard Pólya-frequency orientation is wrong — the 2×2 minor is manifestly negative — reversing the k-order restores total positivity); the important correction: **Hankel positivity alone is not Form A** — the uniform measure on [2,3] has every Hankel matrix positive definite yet C_{0,1} = −3/2; the localizers H⁺ and H − H⁺ are load-bearing; the **fixed-N partial theorem** (b_m(a) = (√a·log a/8)·(½)_m/m! + O_N(√a), so every fixed Hankel and localizer determinant is *eventually* positive as a → ∞); and the exact **downward-transport obstruction**: the flow is not sign-closed — for two atoms d(log det H₂) = 4 − 3(y₁ + y₂), either sign possible — so eventual fixed-N positivity cannot be propagated down to a = 256 without controlling the full mixed/localizer hierarchy.

**The code-synthesis adjudication (round 107, contract PRIME.CODE.SYNTHESIS.01, `code_synthesis_probe.py`, 18/18 gates + wards, 2.2 s, CODE-READING-TRUE-DECODING-DIRECTION).** The program owner's founding intuition — *the primes as an error-correcting code in TFPT* — received its first formal adjudication under that name, and it is **vindicated as measured structure**: the primes *are* the unique codeword of an error-correcting code in the compiler's own Gram grammar — ambient space the nonnegative atomic measures on (0, r] at tent mesh δ = 0.006; codeword the prime-power comb; check family the nested Toeplitz sections T_k(−g″), one check per depth; **minimum distance the round-96 rigidity law, re-measured independently and identical to four digits** (ln η*(r) = −1.0730·r − 7.0783); decoding radius the exact round-96 impostor exits (6/6); rate → 0 (the survival box shrinks in every coordinate). It is the **same grammar as the compiler's literal E8 code layer** ([8,4,4] self-dual, weight enumerator {0:1, 4:14, 8:1}, Construction A → 240 roots — rebuilt exactly in-probe): both membership tests are nonnegativity of a Gram pairing family, so TFPT's one axiom *instantiates* at the arithmetic seam as this code — structure, not progress. But it is a **different dual type**, and the decisive invariant is now a measured number: the E8 layer has dual rank 4 (finite, fixed distance), while **the arithmetic code's dual rank grows with slope 1.0000** (the binding check index always sits at the frontier and never stabilizes; the syndrome rank grows 5 → 12 with genuinely new checks for deep errors; a non-codeword passes every bounded-depth check family *bit-exactly* below its defect), and the **adversarial local-testability gap → 0** beyond any bounded family's support — precisely the error class the RH quantifier cares about (local errors, by contrast, are locally testable and depth-improving). The honest close: the code runs in the **decoding direction** (finite checks ⇒ identification of the measure) while RH needs the **encoding direction** (codeword ⇒ all checks pass) — *the quantifier does not compress, and the reason is now a measured number*; both surprise channels (a stabilizing binding set; a depth-independent adversarial gap) were carried as live gates and are **empty**.

**The audits (round 108, `bigpicture_logic_probe.py`, 31/31 gates, 2.5 s, BIGPICTURE-FINDINGS(3); round 109, contract PRIME.BUGHUNT2.R86.R105.01, `bughunt2_r86_r105_probe.py`, 30/30 gates, 11.6 s, BUGHUNT2-FINDINGS(7, max MAJOR): 1 MAJOR / 6 MINOR / 0 FATAL — the corrections of record).** The campaign's grand claims were audited joint by joint, and the previously unaudited rounds 86–105 were adversarially re-derived — **independently convergent on the same headline finding**. The corrected three-forms statement, with the full form-by-direction status:

| Form | form ⇒ RH | RH ⇒ form | net status |
|---|---|---|---|
| A — Hausdorff cell positivity (all n, k at a = 256) | audited-sound (the H1c continuation chain, independently re-derived **gap-free** in round 109) + literature (Zhang arXiv:2303.09396, Thm 2) | elementary + gated | **proven RH-equivalence** |
| B — Weyl-disk contraction (pointwise at the pins; "uniform" retired per rounds 92/109) | skeleton-sound (SVPIN + V0a + the Lean composition `sv_implies_rh`, citation-typed hypotheses) | **open** — literally the round-90 lemma, measured 16/16 truth-tight, never proven | RH-sufficient pendant |
| C — ground-eigenvector sign positions (TPL(i)) | ladder ⇒ RH via v848 modulo (H); C ⇔ ladder under the named simplicity hypothesis (ParityLemma.lean) | via the Z1 transfer (measured 6.3×10⁻⁵), conditional on CF-realness/Connes §6.6 | RH-sufficient pendant |

The genuine two-sided equivalences in the corpus are Form A, screw positivity of *all* sections at *all* depths (Suzuki — cited, never assumed), and Euler–Pick for *all* N (Pick cited forward, Lean kernel backward) — and **every mutual equivalence that holds routes through RH**: no direct form-to-form edge exists (gate J1.G5); the only direct bridge is the (AC) weld, stopping at the exponential-autocorrelation cone. **Transfer: none**, demonstrated by an explicit counterexample world — an off-line quadruple at γ = 20 (above γ₁) keeps *every* Hausdorff cell positive (n + k ≤ 50, b_n to n = 240) while the Euler–Pick section *fires* at N = 12 (certified negative pivot at 200 dps); the on-line control world is positive in both; a below-γ₁ quadruple fires the Hausdorff n-channel at n = 90. Consequence: **the k ≤ 10¹⁹/7.4×10²¹ Form-A theorems imply nothing about screw sections or Weyl disks — certified regions are form-local** — and their zero-location content sits below γ₁ = 14.13, where Platt–Trudgian is infinitely stronger. The two further fundamental findings are carried as fences in §8.14: the Stage-3 entry-form mismatch (F2) and the falsifier reach cap (F3). The honest OCT boundary is made concrete (J2, absence-gated at 0 atlas hits each): five classes outside the corpus vocabulary are named and **unpriced** — ergodic/measure-rigidity positivity transfer; model-theoretic/o-minimal counting; bigger-group representation-theoretic positivity; GMC/Fyodorov–Hiary–Keating (reduces-in-spirit to magnitude, with an enumeration gap); higher-order Fourier uniformity of Möbius — so "no blanket-untested class left" is vocabulary-relative, exactly as the OCT's own editorial fence says. And the localization lattice is typed (J3): "localized Weil positivity" is a *family* of six inequivalent localizations with different quantifier structures, each with its own completion leg, and mesh-monotonicity is false in general (explicit aliasing counterexample: coarse sections all PSD, fine section λ_min = −14.96) — the corpus's mesh-cofinal correction was load-bearing; no missing arrow is treated as present in the corpus prose (checked). The round-109 minors, all on record: one hardcoded-true gate in the round-104 tally (the only live one across all 17 probes — the round-87 F5 class reproduced after its own warning); two decorative tautological conjuncts in the round-92 probe (the countable Nevanlinna–Pick step is asserted-with-citation, honestly typed everywhere); the screwind docstring's mesh misattribution (0.207 is the δ = 0.008 round-93 value — the true δ = 0.012 sup is 0.2219; MESH-DAMPED stands); the stale "uniform" adjective in Form B's name (contradicting the surfaces' own round-92 correction — retired now); the roadmap probe's stale r95 N = 3 acceptance value (2.3643695×10⁻¹⁰, though r100 tightened lo₃ to 6.9310239×10⁻¹⁰ — acceptance ~2.9× weaker than the corpus's own certified state; frozen probe, finding on record); and two header slips in `sieve4_helper.c` (the certificate unaffected: true worst per-term 13.33u ≪ K_TERM = 32u). Against all that, **the load-bearing re-derivations are clean**: the round-103 theorem re-proved end to end with an own implementation (B₀ = 7.575655711×10⁻¹⁰ exact match, all four partition ratios to 10⁻⁹, the wall root k* = 1.40544×10¹⁹ consistent), the N = 4 certification re-derived (Nair exact for n ≤ 60, far-floor necessity confirmed, the Cholesky certificate logic sound), the Hausdorff H1c continuation **has no gap**, the Lean theorems say exactly what round 99 claims (axioms exactly the standard three), all 16 in-scope probes re-ran green during the audit, and **no round verdict flips**.

**Where this leaves the record** (sandbox; no RH claim): the campaign's map is unchanged in substance and sharper in language — the one open input, localized Weil positivity, now stands in three RH-sufficient lemma forms of precisely graded converse status, guarded by two disproof-sound but reach-capped instruments, with the largest proven positivity region extended to k ≤ 7.44×10²¹ under a derived quadratic law, and the founding code-reading intuition vindicated as structure in the decoding direction. The audits found no fatal misstatement anywhere: the joints hold exactly where the corpus had set its own fences, and the three fundamental findings are formulation/architecture findings, not silent contradictions. ### 8.16 The vocabulary boundary priced, and the resolvent closure adjudicated — the CCM operator is the corpus's own record family (rounds 111–112, notes CDXI–CDXII, 2026-08-15)

Two further freeze rounds of the same day — run as concurrent lanes — closed the two open flanks the §8.15 audits had left: the five argument classes the round-108 audit named as genuinely *outside* the atlas enumeration, and the owner's constructive resolvent-closure architecture. Everything here is sandbox grade — two frozen probes in the experiments tree, no promotion, no marker move, no gate closed or narrowed, no RH claim in any direction; the suite stood at 906 modules at the freeze (949 today, after the v914/v915, v916/v917, v918–v921, v922–v925, v926–v930, v931–v935, v936–v941, v942–v948, v949–v954 and v955/v956 promotions recorded in §8.13–§8.15 and §8.17–§8.25).

**The unpriced classes adjudicated (round 111, contract PRIME.UNPRICED.CLASSES.01, `unpriced_classes_probe.py`, 26/26 gates, 88 s, SPEC ec787fc5, deterministic re-run bit-identical, UNPRICED-ADJUDICATED — tally 25 → 30, 0 PASS).** All five classes outside the original enumeration are priced and killed; none survives as an open lever. The headline is U-A, the priority instrument: **Zagier's published horocycle RH-equivalence** (Zagier 1979/81: the horocycle average obeys m_y(f) = m(f) + o(y^{1/2}) unconditionally, and RH ⟺ error O(y^{3/4−ε}); Sarnak CPAM 1981; Verjovsky 1994) — a published RH-equivalence never adjudicated until now, confirmed absent from every atlas artifact. The instrument built: for the incomplete Eisenstein series ψ(t) = t²e^{−t} the horocycle integral equals 3/π plus a scattering contour integral, warded two-route (dev ≤ 1.5×10⁻⁸; |φ(1/2+it)| = 1 to 9.9×10⁻³² — the unconditional 1/2 exponent is *unitary*, no spectral gap; the 3/π residue two-route). Measured on the ladder y = 2^{−k}, k = 2..20: **deep-window exponent 0.7451** (the RH-range 3/4 in the classically verified region), and planted-zero synthetic scattering worlds at β = 0.6/0.75/0.9 measure exponents 0.6971/0.6222/0.5473 against the law 1 − β/2 (|dev| ≤ 0.0029) — **the horocycle rate is exactly the affine reparametrization θ = 1 − Θ/2 of Θ = sup Re ρ**: the emptied zero-free/density currency (E2), with the alignment content linear log-y phases — the round-97 class (×8.2). Rigidity technology is rate-free (Ratner) or non-horospherical (Lindenstrauss–Mohammadi–Wang CRAS 2023 covers arithmetic quotients of SL₂(ℂ)/products with unspecified exponents, explicitly beyond the horospherical case — while the modular horocycle *is* horospherical, where the classical spectral 1/2 has stood since 1981): verdict UA-RATE-IS-ZERO-CURRENCY, a disguise by rule R1. The pole leg is a genuine Pick function with an **exact Herglotz point mass** 2·sinh²(a/2)·δ_{−1/4}; the Pick-class reading is **refuted with margins** (λ_min(L_f) < 0 at all 14 rungs, missing wall positivity by 9.4–87.9 orders; refinement deepens); the smooth cell is PSD at the nodes by the exact **commensurate-sampling mechanism** (sin(2a·ω_k) == 0 — lattice geometry, not a prime fingerprint); the dream statement dies honestly at the prime leg (a positive rewrite would be the wall positivity itself). The r45 LOEWNER-DEAD distinction is carried: a *third* reading is killed here; the IIKS/RHP lane is untouched.

## 9. What we can offer, and what we ask

**Offer.**

- **Data and frozen registries:** the 67-rung surface and the 28-rung blind holdout with the frozen registry (sha256 `ae292e55…`), the full per-rung tangent-Schur scalars (n, q, b, B), the source-only congruence cores with their eight power sums and UB_r ladders (§5.3), the certified floors, and the complete resistance-map measurements — all reproducible from the public suite.
- **The closure stack (§6):** the certified 2×10⁷-ordinate zero cache with its disclosed builders and certification battery, the composed wall census (39/39 + 8/8 from cited inputs), and the per-rung T_req curves and H(T) transfer-law envelope over the full 75-rung ladder — a clean, reproducible benchmark for any proposed uniform bound.
- **The moment certificate (§7.1):** the one-bad-mode Chebyshev-separator route — congruence-free, integer-exact decisions, certifying the full combined ladder 68/68 with a measured *h-flat* order price r* = 0.544·√(λ_max/c_B) — with per-rung moments and r* ladders; plus its Zolotarev compression (a single fixed 8-pole rational filter certifying all 68 steps, with the full 68×8 determinant-phase certificate stored as a compact artifact). The first certificate form whose order price does not grow with depth, and a concrete shared object for moment/inertia machinery.
- **The SOS proof object (§8.3):** `radau_sos_certificate_proof.json` — 708 KB, 1111 exact rational sum-of-squares certificates (interval-SOS identities with closed-form Markov–Lukács Grams, PSD by structure), census 151/151 at η = 0.273, an identity hash per certificate, and the one-line theorem — machine-verifiable end-to-end by any CAS, with no floating-point step anywhere in the consumed path. Probably the single most shareable artifact the program has produced.
- **Reproducible modules:** the verification suite (904 scripts, deterministic, audit-gated; the modules cited here are `v897`, `v901`, `v902`, `v905`, `v906`, `v907`, and the newly promoted `v909`–`v911`), with negative controls (Epstein, scramble, smooth) wired into every load-bearing module.
- **A Lean 4 tree** (`TfptCarrier/`, building against Mathlib): kernel-checked modules for the extraction-chain hypotheses (`CofinalWeil.lean`, `CofinalCurrent.lean`, and the noninterference hardening `CofinalPredefinition.lean` of §1.2 — including the negative example and the constantization limit), the grade no-go and Krein defect theorems, and per-rung wall-ladder certificates (`WallLadder/RungKz*.lean`); the composition of the finite-head certificate chain through `krein_cofinal_weil` is in progress.

**Ask.**

1. **The lock, as the phase round leaves it (§7.2–7.3)** — the collaboration question, sharpened once more: the Euler phase of the cancellation is now *exactly constructed* (and thereby shown to be a coordinate change), so the question is no longer the phase but **which functional of it separates the worlds** — equivalently, on the Gram side, a lower bound on c_h = 1 − λ_max(N_h, P_h), against a measured demand of +2.81 dex median, growing +3.35 dex/dex (the ≤ 21 sub-γ₁ frequency localization of §6.4 stands). The measured state of the functional question: index-type functionals are dead (Krein/Pick census WALLPAPER, §7.2), and the one measured world-discriminating candidate is the **Euler grouping — G2's parameter-free weight law** — whose analytic consequences are the honest open question. All characterizations are frozen, machine-verified, and reproducible, and the H(T) transfer law remains the numerical yardstick any proposed bound must beat. The census warning travels with the ask: every tested magnitude-side bound is either 0/75 or control-blind, and the index of the constructed phase grows with resolution.
2. Their **moment/inertia machinery on two concrete moment objects**: the fixed 8-dimensional core at r = 4 (§5.3: UB₄ sits at 0.83–1.60 ladder-wide, integrality closes everything below 1, and the demand is a source-only congruence upgrade that buys the missing factor), and the new one-bad-mode separator census (§7.1: 68/68, integer-exact, measured h-flat order price; in its Zolotarev compression a single fixed 8-pole filter, i.e. 68×8 stored determinant phases — what it lacks for all-h is a source-only control of the global spectral width or of the eight fixed phase values, plus the same arithmetic supply as everything else). If their optimized inertia bounds, or a moment refinement their framework already contains, sharpen either object, the gaps are exactly of the stated sizes.
3. **The endform open problems (§8), stated precisely — with the localization final:** (i) **the open problem is now one scalar anti-cancellation inequality** (§8.6; one *inequality* — the chain's four remaining legs, the Suzuki/W1 identification, density plus the equi-Lipschitz/Dini continuity modulus, the faithful cap and Weil's criterion, stay named classical citations, §1.2): v₋ᵀXv₋ ≥ −λ_min(G₀) — a prime-only quadratic form against a geometry-only number, relative margin O(10⁻⁴), alignment-carried (the comb wins by direction, not size; the decoupled split fails by 5%, the completed square is degenerate, the Mellin positive-energy form does not exist, the smallest tested sign-faithful dimension is 24) — with the measured seat: the sign is decided only at resolution n ~ 2¹⁷, practically the whole window, and Selberg — the strongest classical unconditional source, structurally a perfect fit — is measurably insufficient by 4–5 orders at the min end (median miss ~9×10⁵, ≈6 orders), with the bit-identity A_err − B_err = P_err closing the entire rewrite-then-estimate class. Any source of arithmetic information at that resolution is the collaboration question. (The former item here — cofinal legality with a measured termination signal — is retro-corrected: the termination and the deep witnesses were instrument artifacts, and on the clean instrument the construction has no measured end, §8.5; what stands is the corrected ladder to h\* = 10513 and the no-witness census through 13026.) (ii) the **last unproved deep-rate bound**, untouched by the correction — of the three named bounds, the Lipschitz constant is now certified (L_plane = 1.3125, a 400-bit interval proof object) and the Rayleigh-deficit cap is proved-conditional (self-limiting growth against an h-stationary ceiling); what remains is a proved lower bound for one measured scalar, t = ν₁/(ν₀‖B‖_F) > 4/5, whose carrier is the AR–OSC cancellation, against a direct-reading margin of only +0.0104 that is itself an irreducible measurement (the budget reading does not close); (iii) the **observer coupling** — the certified moment/determinant dual excludes the 1.15 plateau phantom and narrows the certified class window to [0.9727, 1.2402] but leaves the compression PARTIAL: the missing object is a certified non-product relation of the entry moments or a sharper joint moment dual.
4. A conversation about **window design as a functional-specific optimum** — we tested their optimized cosine window inside our Galerkin lag slot and it kills the wall outright (as does Fejér), because our tent's Bochner positive-definiteness is load-bearing (§5.5). The interesting joint question is what the *right* optimization problem is for a window that must serve both a pair-correlation functional and a PD lag-interpolation slot.
5. A joint look at the **origin of the ½** — our frozen constant has four typed open candidate derivations; their scaling analysis may recognize it.

## 10. Honest limitations

1. **No RH claim.** Nothing here is progress on the Riemann Hypothesis, and we say so as the governing statement, not as a formality. The reduction is exact *at RH strength*: the open half is RH-hard by the program's own typing. What separates the finite closure of §6 from RH is the depth quantifier (*all computed h* → *all h*) together with the direction quantifier that closure leaves open (its W2 is certified along the measured critical direction only, §6.2; the pure one-quantifier form holds for the §8 Level-1 cells, where full M ≻ 0 is certified per built cell), and the depth quantifier does not fall to more zeros — nor, by the overnight census (§7), to any of the six tested certificate architectures; the missing ingredient is typed as a world-discriminating functional of an exactly constructed Euler phase (the phase itself is a coordinate change, and its index functional is measured out), and naming — or even constructing — the carrier object is not proving anything about the functional. The final localization (§8.6) does not change this: reducing the quantifier to one named scalar inequality is a coordinate achievement, not progress on its sign — and the clean-map correction (§8.5) removes a false termination signal, which restores the open question to fully open, nothing more.
2. **Surface-only certificates.** Every certified statement (§2) lives on the reachable finite ladder (rungs h ≤ 878 interval-rigorous; the B-half chain exact-rational/interval on the 39 reachable steps, h ≲ 900). The 67-rung registered surface extends to h = 1433 and the blind holdout to h = 2854 at the declared deployed-pipeline (float) level only. The §6 closure certifies positivity on a finite family of Galerkin sections — per rung, deep block at float level, W2 along the measured critical direction — and depends on published zero verifications as citations. The §8 σ-chain certificates are exact-rational but likewise finite: 151 built wall-legal cells plus 59 built deep steps to h = 6344 — *built* cells only, with the h > 1450 flank and the deep-membership lane beyond the census not constructed. h-uniformity is open everywhere.
3. **The target is an instrument.** The registered inequality (§3) has passed one blind holdout; that is survival of a falsification attempt, not evidence. Its constant ½ has no derivation.
4. **The dictionary conserves difficulty.** §5 is a coordinate change with a favorable integrality structure — now verified as an exact identity on both frozen surfaces — but it creates a shared language, not a proof path with any certified step beyond what §2 already carries. The measured returns respect this: two moments fail broadly (as the adopted no-go predicts), and the four-moment result is a **measured near-threshold, not a certificate** — the CMS census is declared float-level with conservative refusal guards; its exact companion (Newton–Sturm) is a benchmark class, not new certification power. All four probes of §5.3–§5.5 are frozen sandbox rounds: no promotion, no marker moves.
5. **External result as cited input.** The Anthropic 2/3-result, its budget constants, its window form, and its ≈ 0.68 ceiling are used here as cited external inputs with a pedigree reservation (independent repo-side verification pending); the route gate of §5.2 is our adoption of their stated no-go, at their stated scope. Likewise, the §6 closure consumes published zero verifications (Odlyzko; LMFDB/Platt; Platt–Trudgian 2021) as cited external inputs with declared error budgets.
6. **The §6–§8 results originate as frozen sandbox rounds.** Three of them — the composed census, the transfer law, and the wiring theorem — have since been promoted into the load-bearing suite (`v909`/`v910`/`v911`, suite 904, AUDIT OK), with the honest typing frozen there (W1/W2 as algorithmically independent evaluations of the same localized Weil form, direction-conditional positivity, the deep block float-level, external-cited zeros). Everything else — the all-h map, the entire overnight contracts round and phase round (five-contract census, both lock characterizations, the moment certificate and its Zolotarev compression, the Euler phase construction with its grouping and Krein index censuses), and the entire σ-chain round of §8 (the Level-1 census, the joint class relation and its branch-and-bound window, the SOS proof object, and the endform measurements — deep membership, legality frontier, deep rate, coupling currency, the hardening round's legality horizon and three-bounds probes, the 2026-08-13 clean-map and localization rounds: the chain audit, the Verdict-A protocol with its retro-correction ledger, the core normal form, and the Selberg rewrite; and the 2026-08-13 adversarial-audit round of §8.8: the grid-offset shift-average family with its withdrawn means and its surviving point certificates, the all-depth mean contract, both Ξ-limit/resolvent rounds, the fold-covariance discrimination, and the multiplicative sign-source attack) — remains machine-verified frozen-spec probes in the experiments tree: no promotion, no marker moves; the clean instrument's deep positive reads are "no witness found", never certified positivity, and its own resolution edge is measured at ~10.6k (four straddle cells, E1-plus-exact-inertia positive through 13026, the strict two-evaluator map to 10513); the T_req/H(T) laws, the r*/deficit laws, and the deep stationarity/rate measurements of §8.4 are empirical fits or conjecture-grade, and the composed all-h drafts are conjectures with named gaps. The §7.1 wiring closure covers the five *enumerated* demand classes, and the gauge-covariance audit (CCXXI) covers the rule-gauge orbit as constructed there; a compiler demand outside that list is not excluded, and whether the seam physics derives a specific collar frame remains open. The formulation "pure-I is compiler-forced" is barred by the audit's frozen language rule.
7. **One of our own sandbox claims was withdrawn the same day it was made** (§8.8). The θ-mean certificates of the grid-offset wall family (CCCXLI) rested on a piecewise-affinity premise that a 90-digit source rebuild proved false; all three are retracted, their honest corrected enclosures are [−∞, +∞], and what remains is five *point* certificates at θ = ½ — never a mean, never an all-h statement. We record it as a limitation and not merely as a correction, for two reasons: it is the second same-day retraction of this campaign generation (the first being the instrument artifacts of §8.5), and it shows the failure mode this program has to keep guarding against — a rigorous finite computation carrying an unvalidated *analytic* premise. The measured lesson, frozen in the audit: a finite-difference curvature profile is not a derivative bound, and no amount of interval arithmetic downstream repairs a missing theorem upstream.
8. **The Obstruction Completeness Theorem (§8.9) is finite-rung and partly editorial, and it is not evidence about RH.** Its machine-asserted premises live on the built cells (h ≤ 2854, ladder cited to 12632) and on the corpus's own vocabulary: "the candidate map is complete" means complete over the routes and sign-source classes named in the campaign notes — an editorial completeness claim, typed as such, never a gate — and a genuinely new argument class outside that vocabulary is not excluded. E7–E10 remain classical citations. The theorem characterizes the *type* of the missing input; it says nothing about whether an input of that type exists, and the standing counter-evidence (NO-WITNESS: every certified read positive to h = 12632, decay flattening) travels with it. It is not a statement that RH is unprovable, by this route or any other. The round-108 audit (§8.15) makes the vocabulary-relative fence concrete with five named **unpriced** classes, absence-gated at 0 atlas hits each: ergodic/measure-rigidity positivity transfer; model-theoretic/o-minimal counting; bigger-group representation-theoretic positivity; GMC/Fyodorov–Hiary–Keating (reduces-in-spirit to magnitude, with an enumeration gap); and higher-order Fourier uniformity of Möbius — "no blanket-untested class left" is completeness over the corpus vocabulary, not over mathematics.
9. **The §8.10 records needed same-day corrections, and the audit that found them also convicted the corpus's own gate bookkeeping.** The round-87 bughunt found nine findings over rounds 79–85 (3 MAJOR / 6 MINOR / 0 FATAL, no verdict flips): the round-83 record conflated two different zero-tracking quantities — both artifacts of the same float64 refinement; the honest values (first-zero deviation −8.4×10⁻¹⁶ = the cache's own quantization of γ₁, mean displacement 0.0 at float64 resolution) are *stronger* than the published ones, but the published ones were wrong — a two-sided node count was paired with a one-sided census (honest 18-vs-3), and the published ε finals were refinement artifacts moving by up to 117% under the exact census (all rows staying CONVERGES). Per the standing convention the frozen probes and notes are not retro-edited: the correction record is the correction, and §8.10 carries only the corrected values. Separately, nine gates across the sandbox corpus were found hardcoded `check(..., True)` — never able to fail while counted in the N/N pass tallies, one of them the very line recording a headline verdict (the verdict independently right, the gate testing nothing). The failure mode is recorded as a limitation of the probe methodology: an N/N tally is not, by itself, evidence that N things were tested. And the §8.10 sign-position predictor is a finite measurement (79 lattice points, x ≤ 13); its exactness for all x is precisely the open RH-hard statement, not something the measurement supports in either direction.
10. **A second published sandbox record needed a correction, found by the program's own pricing round (round 97, §8.13).** The round-88 sign-position predictor was published (§8.10, and carried into §8.12 property 1) as exact at all 79 reachable lattice points with the all-x extension named as the open RH-hard seat. Round 97 extended the census to 18798 points (x ≤ 890) and measured the *operative* extension **false from x = 21 onward** (567 τ-screened counterexamples): the round-88 exactness was a small-x accident of the margin budget, by an arithmetic-free mechanism — the Fejér taper's tracking-variance floor against the 0.5 budget — that no taper in the family survives, and the same round priced the budget as classically out of reach (best unconditional miss ×8.2; even the RH-conditional bound reads 1.015 at the deepest rung), with the prefix/exceptional-set salvage dead in-window. Per the standing convention the frozen probes and notes are not retro-edited — the correction is the record, carried in §8.10, §8.12 and §8.13. The fence travels with it: what died is the deployed source-side approximant, not TPL(i) itself (the eigenvector truth side is unmeasured beyond x = 13), and the round-88 claim as published — exact at 79 points, x ≤ 13 — remains factually true; its operative extension is measured dead. We record it as a limitation for the same reason as limitation 7: a finite exactness measurement, however clean, is not evidence about its own extension.
11. **The published "three equivalent lemma forms" language needed a correction, found independently by the program's own two audits (rounds 108 and 109, §8.15).** The formula overstated the machine record: of the three forms, only the Hausdorff form is a proven RH-equivalence; the Weyl-disk and sign-position forms are RH-sufficient with open/measured/conditional converses (Form B's converse *is* literally the round-90 open lemma), every mutual equivalence routes through RH, and there is **no transfer between forms** — an explicit round-108 counterexample world keeps every Hausdorff cell positive while the Euler–Pick section fires, so certified regions are form-local and the k ≤ 7.4×10²¹ Form-A theorems say nothing about screw sections or Weyl disks. Two related headlines gained fences in the same correction: "Stage 3 conditional-complete" (the kernel-checked extraction accepts only H_cof, which no entry form produces — the weakest link is the finite-acceptance-versus-infinite-quantifier interface itself) and "standing disproof channels, open and empty" (both instruments are disproof-sound but reach-capped far below Platt–Trudgian — the certified ranges carry zero live falsification power, and "open and empty" is a theorem given the cited inputs, not an observation). Per the standing convention the frozen probes and notes are not retro-edited — the correction is the record, carried in §8.13, §8.14 and §8.15 and mirrored on every live surface. No number was wrong and no verdict enum flips; the drift was prose-level. We record it as a limitation for the same reason as limitations 7, 9 and 10: headline language is itself an artifact that must be audited, and an equivalence claim must carry its direction-by-direction proof status.
